- **Theory/Equivalence/Adjoint/Compose.v**: Mac Lane §IV.4 Exercise 2(b) (book p. 95, `maclane:IV.4:ex2`) — **the composite of two adjoint equivalences is an adjoint equivalence, with its unit and counit COMPARED to the constituents' rather than merely shown to exist**: `AdjointEquivalence_Compose A B : AdjointEquivalence (F' ◯ F) (U ◯ U')` (the issue's pinned name), built DIRECTLY on `Adjunction/Compose.v:173`'s `Adjunction_Compose` — its adjunction field IS that composite at `eq_refl` (`AdjointEquivalence_Compose_adjunction`), its two invertibility clauses the named `adjoint_equivalence_compose_unit_iso`/`_counit_iso` at `eq_refl`. Clause (a) of the exercise is `Theory/Equivalence/Bundled.v:94`'s pre-existing `EquivalenceOfCategories_Compose` (cells `:72`/`:83`, bundled `Equivalence_trans` `:115`), consumed and not rebuilt; the module is a NEW sibling of `Theory/Equivalence/Adjoint.v` because the issue's suggested placement is an existing file, mirroring how `Adjunction/Compose.v` stands to `Theory/Adjunction.v`. **THE COMPARISON LEMMAS COME IN TWO GRADES THAT ARE KEPT APART**: Mac Lane's whiskering formulas `AdjointEquivalence_Compose_unit : unit ≈ fmap[U] (unit' (F x)) ∘ unit x` and `_counit : counit ≈ counit' y ∘ fmap[F'] (counit (U' y))` hold at `≈` only — supplied by `:=` with no tactic, they are their donors `Adjunction_Compose_unit`/`_counit` (`:216`/`:224`) applied, and those are `Qed` corollaries proved from `to_adj_unit`/`from_adj_counit`, a rewriting step and not a conversion, so the grade is the donors' (both strict forms refuted and pinned, negatives 4–5); whereas `AdjointEquivalence_Compose_unit_transpose` and `_counit_transpose` hold at `eq_refl` — the composite's unit IS A's forward transpose of B's unit, and its counit IS B's inverse transpose of A's counit — with the two-sided inverses read back at `eq_refl` too (`_unit_inverse`: `η⁻¹ ∘ fmap[U] η'⁻¹`; `_counit_inverse`: `fmap[F'] ε⁻¹ ∘ ε'⁻¹`). That strict form is what the direct route buys. **THE ISSUE'S OWN ROUTE IS BUILT AND MEASURED, NOT DECLINED BY ASSERTION**: `AdjointEquivalence_Compose_via_equivalence := Equivalence_to_AdjointEquivalence (EquivalenceOfCategories_Compose (AdjointEquivalence_to_Equivalence A) (AdjointEquivalence_to_Equivalence B))`, and its premise checks out — the quasi-inverse of the composite equivalence IS `U ◯ U'` on the nose (`_via_quasi_inverse`, `eq_refl`, `quasi_inverse` of a `Build_EquivalenceOfCategories` being its second argument definitionally), so the constant elaborates at exactly the composite's type with no transport; but it is a DIFFERENT adjoint equivalence: whole record, underlying adjunction and unit components are each refuted at `eq_refl` (negatives 1–3) while the direct route's unit reduces as the control. A REASON is measured and labelled a reason, not an isolation: the two cells of `EquivalenceOfCategories_Compose` are `Qed`, so the refined transposes sit behind an opaque constant — yet an out-of-tree experiment rebuilding both cells `Defined` still stops one constructor further in, at the transposing isomorphism itself, so the `Qed` cells are NOT shown to be the sole blocker. Nothing is claimed about `≈`-agreement of the two routes in either direction (an adjunction structure on a fixed adjoint pair need not be unique; no proof, no counterexample). `AdjointEquivalence_Id C` is over `Adjunction_Id` (`Adjunction/Compose.v:65`, `Instance/Adjoints.v`'s `adj_id` reused): adjunction at `eq_refl`, unit and counit `≈ id` from `Adjunction_Id_unit`/`_counit`, both two-sided inverses `= id` at `eq_refl`. The INVERSE is `Theory/Equivalence/Adjoint.v:407`'s existing `AdjointEquivalence_swap`, CITED (`AdjointEquivalence_swap_readback` reads its adjunction field back as `:414`'s at `eq_refl`) and not rebuilt — its unit does not reduce, per the `Theory/Equivalence/Strict.v` measurement of that same chain, and a direct reducing swap is not attempted. **THE GROUPOID LAWS COME OUT FINER THAN "NOT STATABLE"**: at the CLASS, associativity and both identity laws are ILL-TYPED — `Compose` of functors is not associative on the nose and `Id` is not a strict unit for it, the `Adjunction/Pare.v` measurement — pinned as TYPING negatives 6–7 (plain `has type … expected`, no `cannot unify`, no universe clause), and no transport is invented; but the two bracketings have definitionally equal OBJECT actions, so the units and counits inhabit convertible types, and there the laws hold ON THE NOSE — six `eq_refl` Examples (`_assoc_unit`/`_assoc_counit`, `_id_left_unit`/`_counit`, `_id_right_unit`/`_counit`). An invertibility calculus for the PREDICATE `IsIsomorphism` rides along — `IsIso_id`, `IsIso_along` (transport along `≈`), `IsIso_comp`, `IsIso_fmap` — universe-FREE with LOAD-BEARING explicit binders (`IsIso_along@{o h p}`'s block is `Category`'s own `h <= p` and nothing else; `IsIso_fmap` keeps its two categories' six levels apart; written unannotated the same bodies minimize to identify hom with proof and are then rejected where the annotated forms are accepted, the probe's controls); `IsIso_fmap` DUPLICATES `Construction/Reflective/Idempotent.v:69`'s `fmap_IsIsomorphism`, restated on a measurement (that module's closure is 33) with the same statement up to the explicitness of `f` and the universe annotation, and the other three have no in-tree counterpart by NAME — nor by SHAPE: a sweep for `IsIsomorphism (_ ∘ _)`, `IsIsomorphism id` and any respectfulness of `IsIsomorphism` in `≈` finds nothing outside this file, the only `IsIsomorphism (fmap[_] _)` heads being the `fmap_IsIsomorphism` family and hypotheses. **NON-VACUITY IS NOT DEGENERATE ON THE AXIS THAT MATTERS, AND THE AXIS IT IS DEGENERATE ON IS DISCLOSED**: `indiscrete_square` composes `AdjointEquivalence_swap` of `Theory/Equivalence/Strict.v:839`'s `indiscrete_adjoint_equivalence` with that equivalence itself, an adjoint equivalence of `Indiscrete bool` with itself whose functor `IndT ◯ Erase (Indiscrete bool)` MOVES an object (`false ↦ true` at `eq_refl`, `indiscrete_square_not_id` by `discriminate`), so it is not an identity adjoint equivalence and does not even have the TYPE of one (TYPING negative 8); but `Indiscrete`'s hom family ignores its endpoints (`Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v:418` declares `hom := fun _ _ => unit`, the stdlib `unit : Set`, which is also where the witness block's `Set` comes from), every hom-set there being a singleton, so the MORPHISM-level statement "the unit at `false` IS the identity" is well typed and TRUE, at `eq_refl` and not merely at `≈`, and a morphism-level non-degeneracy statement is unavailable — the trap `Theory/Equivalence/Strict.v` records for its own counit; the object-level statement is the honest one and the only one made. **Universes measured off BOTH binder and block**: every adjunction-facing constant is over `Category@{u u0 u0}` in the BINDER with no such equation in any block; the composite carries `u0 = u2`, `u0 = u4`, `u2 = u4` (the three categories' hom-and-proof levels collapsed to one) and `u5 = u7`, all three OBJECT universes free; `AdjointEquivalence_Id` carries no equation at all; the witness block carries `Set < u3` through `Strict.v` from `Instance/One.v` and `Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v`. Donors probed with controls at the very same levels (negatives 9–13): **the IDENTITY FUNCTOR `Id` forces hom = proof ON ITS OWN** — easy to miss, and it means a probe writing `Id[Cu]` into an `Adjunction` measures `Id` and not `Adjunction`; the discriminating control is an arbitrary endofunctor `Cu ⟶ Cu`, which IS accepted, so `Functor` is not a donor — `Adjunction` is a donor independently (probed at two arbitrary endofunctors so no `Id` occurs in the command), `AdjointEquivalence` is a third that CANNOT be tested apart from `Adjunction` (its first field is `F ⊣ U`), `AdjointEquivalence_Id` has both in reach and isolates neither, and the composite's hom collapse is forced by functors in BOTH directions before any adjunction is formed (`Au ⟶ Bu` accepted, `Bu ⟶ Au` rejected under `ah < bh`). None claimed unavoidable. **PRIOR ART MEASURED WITH THE CRITERION STATED, because the count moves with it**: the bare word `AdjointEquivalence` occurs in FIVE files at the base commit INCLUDING its declaring one (`Adjunction/FullFaithful.v:285` prose, `Structure/Monoidal/Dual.v:581`, `Construction/Subcategory/Dense.v:388` and `Theory/Equivalence/Strict.v:571`/`:839` inhabitants), the substring in EIGHT (`Theory/Equivalence/Adjunction.v:36` prose, `Creation.v:66` and `Limit.v:387` consuming `AdjointEquivalence_swap_adjunction` without naming the class); under either criterion none composes two and none exhibits an identity one, so the issue's "three files" is stale both ways, and the strings `AdjointEquivalence_Compose`, `AdjointEquivalence_Id` and `AdjointEquivalence Id` occur nowhere else. **46/46 constants closed under the global context** — 40 glob heads (39 `def`, 1 `prf`; no `inst`/`proj`/`rec`) plus the 6 `Program` obligations of `IsIso_id`/`_comp`/`_fmap`; no `Record`/`Class`/`Inductive`, so no `Build_*`; zero collisions over the 52 heads-plus-probe names; closure 39 excluding self (26 without the witness section's three requires — all 13 arrive with `Theory.Equivalence.Strict`, `Instance.One` and `Instance.Discrete.Reconstruct` costing nothing on top and being required only for the names `Erase` and `Indiscrete`; no single general-theory require costs anything alone; donors 22/20/20), probe 40; four `Defined` lines (the obligations of `IsIso_comp`/`IsIso_fmap`), NONE load-bearing — all four flip to `Qed` with every `eq_refl` readback intact, measured — the one `Qed` being `indiscrete_square_not_id`, which produces no data. `Test/ProbeAdjointCompose379.v` mirrors the target's `Require` list and carries **14 `Fail`s = 1 instrument + 13 negatives of THREE kinds** (5 CONVERSION, 3 TYPING, 5 FORMABILITY), each stripped one at a time with its whole error read, every constant a negative names guarded outside a `Fail` — including the identity functor `Id`, which the builder's cut named only INSIDE negative 9 so that renaming it left that negative silently green, found by audit and closed with a top-level `Check @Id.` that now breaks the probe under the rename — rename-simulated 6/6 over the target constants the negatives name; `make todo` grows by 16, all in the probe (14 `Fail` commands + 2 prose lines), zero in the library, zero TODO/FIXME/jww. NOT delivered: no direct reducing swap; no `≈` comparison of the two routes; no uniqueness of the composite; no category of categories and adjoint equivalences, so the groupoid laws are not packaged as a structure; no transport of an adjoint equivalence along a natural isomorphism of either adjoint; no 2-categorical reading and no relation to `Theory/Bicategory/Adjunction.v`; and no witness at three genuinely distinct categories
- **Adjunction/Diagonal/Coproduct.v**: the coproduct bifunctor `+(C) : C ∏ C ⟶ C` (dual to Functor/Product/Internal.v's `×(C)`) and the covariant adjunction `+(C) ⊣ Diagonal_Product C` — stated in `C` with no residual `^op`, mirroring the transposes of Adjunction/Diagonal/Product.v — together with named unit and counit (`coproduct_unit := (inl, inr)`, `coproduct_counit := id ▽ id`) each identified with the generic `unit`/`counit` of Theory/Adjunction.v, the counit identification being a definitional check
- **Theory/Lawvere.v**: Lawvere theories — the `LawvereTheory` class mirroring the PROP class's relaxed shape (`law_of_nat` naming function + propositional strictness equalities on OBJECTS; bijectivity deliberately not required, consumers state reachability), with `law_pow`/`law_pow_one`; the skeletal base `FinSetOp_Lawvere` on `FinSet^op` whose strictness fields compute by `eq_refl` even at open variables (Instance/FinSet/Lawvere.v); models as Cartesian+Terminal-preserving functors with the model category `Models T C` a full subcategory of the functor category, pack/unpack mutually inverse (Theory/Lawvere/Model.v); Sets-models with the underlying-set functor `ev1` and `ev1_Faithful` under an explicit reachability hypothesis — the honest price of the relaxed class, trivially witnessed on the base by `FinSetOp_reach` (Theory/Lawvere/Sets.v); the finitary-monad connection hypothesis-scoped over a given left adjoint `L ⊣ ev1` — `Lawvere_Monad` via the transparent induced monad, `Lawvere_EM_Comparison`, and `Lawvere_crude_monadicity` re-exposing the crude-monadicity hypotheses verbatim (full equivalence ledger 2; Theory/Lawvere/Monad.v); the PROP-spine bridge — cartesian monoidal/braided/symmetric structure via the CC_* instances and the interpretation tower `Lawvere_PROP_interp` (+Monoidal/Strict/Braided/Symmetric) from the FreePROP universal property under a disclosed strictness hypothesis pack (`coh := eq_refl` on skeletal instances, remaining equalities by nat induction), with the Fox-theorem pointer joining the cartesian and copy/discard spines (Theory/Lawvere/PROP.v)
- **Theory/Multicategory.v**: Symmetric multicategories with zipper single-slot composition — `mcomp {Γ₁ Γ₂ Δ b c} : mhom (Γ₁++b::Γ₂) c → mhom Δ b → mhom (Γ₁++Δ++Γ₂) c` (no heterogeneous-list telescopes), boundary casts `mcast` with the groupoid pack and any-proof law variants, unit laws through a named splice-equation kit, both associativity shapes (nested + disjoint over their boundary equations); the symmetric action `msym` indexed by the Type-valued `tperm` witness (plan erratum: stdlib `Permutation` is Prop with no elimination into Type; `tperm_Permutation` bridges back) with a disclosed SCOPE note (witness-indexed action; symmetric-group descent and slot-crossing equivariance deferred — every in-tree instance is expected to satisfy both, though neither is proven in-tree). Multifunctors with the heterogeneous setoid (Theory/Multicategory/Functor.v); the representable multicategory of a symmetric monoidal category via `tensor_list`/`tfold` folds and a braid-built `perm_arrow`, developed once over an object realization `ob : A → C` — `RepresentableMulticategory` threads UIP-on-object-lists (Grothendieck/Strict precedent) and `ColouredPROP_Multicategory` derives it from the colour decider by axiom-free Hedberg (Theory/Multicategory/Representable.v); the endomorphism operad `pow`/`EndOperad` with the genuine fork-algebra braid action and nat-level UIP normalization (Theory/Multicategory/Endomorphism.v); operads as one-object multicategories with `ohom` accessors and the two-sided presentation round trip (Theory/Multicategory/Operad.v); operad algebras `OperadAlgebra := MultiFunctor` into `EndOperad` with the convenience constructor (+ `alg_act_Build` sanity), the category `OperadAlgebras`, and the Comm example — algebras of the terminal operad in Sets are commutative monoids, both directions, connecting Instance/CMon.v (Theory/Multicategory/Algebra.v)
- **Functor/Product/Fixed.v**: Mac Lane §I.4's fixed-factor product — for any cartesian `C` and fixed `H`, the functor `H × −` (`fixed_product_functor`, object map `G ↦ H × G`, `fmap := second`, laws exactly `second_id`/`second_comp`), and for `f : H ~> K` the transformation `H × − ⟹ K × −` with components `first f` (`fixed_product_transform`, whose `naturality` is `symmetry (first_second f g)` and whose `naturality_sym` is `first_second f g`, in that orientation), packaged up to the currying `fixed_product_curried : C ⟶ [C, C]`. The binoidal comparison is stated as proof, and it runs OPPOSITE to the obvious guess: this tree spells the product of morphisms `split` = `bimap[×(C)]`, and the composite `Monoidal_Binoidal ∘ Cartesian_Monoidal` — which the tree already reaches for a cartesian category *with a terminal object* — hits Mac Lane's `f × id` and `id × g` ON THE NOSE (`alt_is_inj_left`/`alt_is_inj_right`, by `reflexivity`), whereas this file's `first`/`second` match only up to `≈` (`fixed_product_bimap`, `fixed_product_transform_bimap`, `fixed_product_inj_right_fmap`). The components are `first f` because issue #240 prescribes them verbatim; `alt_transform` carries the `split f id` spelling alongside, related by `alt_transform_agrees`. The SOLE reason for the terminal-free `Cartesian_Binoidal` is the hypothesis count — `Class Cartesian` carries no terminal object — and its `inj_right`/`inj_left` corollaries are instances of `FromAFunctor_ToAFunctor` (Theory/Functor.v:419), true of every functor, recording only that the packaging round-trips. Non-degeneracy is proven, not assumed: `fixed_product_transform_faithful` (distinct `f` give distinct transformations, no terminal object needed) and `fixed_product_transform_reflects_id`. Instantiated at `Grp` over the pre-existing `Grp_Cartesian` (`Grp_fixed_product`, `Grp_fixed_product_transform`) with a non-degenerate witness: the zero endomorphism of Z/2, proved to have no left inverse and hence `Grp_Z2_zero_not_iso : IsIsomorphism Grp_Z2_zero → False` (the morphism-level predicate — the object-level `Isomorphism Z2 Z2 → False` would be FALSE, by `iso_id`), to induce a transformation that is not `nat_id`, and to have a component that moves `(true, true)` and is not monic
- **Theory/Size.v**: Size and foundations vocabulary (Mac Lane §I.6, Awodey 1.11/1.12, Riehl 1.1.6/1.1.7) — `LocallySmall` and `Small`, predicates the tree had never declared (`Theory/Lawvere/Sets.v:44` records the gap). Both are relative to levels. ALL of `Small`'s content is in its strict `uo < o, uh < h`; `Test/Size.v` writes out the trivial discharge and shows it REJECTED, in two separate commands because the combined one is refused inside its hom sub-term and so says nothing about `uo < o` on its own. `LocallySmall` is deliberately NON-strict, so `locally_small_ambient` witnesses it for EVERY category — Riehl 1.1.7's "each is locally small" as a lemma, recording that `Class Category`'s `homset` field builds it in; the converse "not small" half is NOT statable, its negation being a metatheoretic claim. Objects resize up to **`ObjEq`**, a level-polymorphic identity type declared in-file — NOT stdlib `=`, which is pinned to a global universe on 8.19/8.20 and cannot be mentioned under an explicit universe-constraint clause; homs resize up to a genuine setoid isomorphism (both directions respect `≈` — `ls_from_resp` is a field because it is not derivable, and without it the structure is only a section/retraction pair). Riehl 1.1.6's unindexed packaging is `ArrowQuiver`/`ArrowQuiverOfCat`/`TotalMor` (no reflexive quiver existed in tree; the tree's `Quiver` is indexed with no identity selection), where the dom/cod retractions hold by `obj_refl` — a book axiom made definitional rather than proved; both packagings are supplied but their EQUIVALENCE is not established. **Erratum corrected**: `Check (Cat : obj[Cat])` SUCCEEDS — universe polymorphism instantiates the two occurrences at different levels, so it is the Cat/CAT tower (Riehl §1.3) rather than self-membership, and the pinned-instance form `obj[Cat@{a b c d e}] := Cat@{a b c d e}` is what is actually rejected; the `Sets` case is an ordinary type error (`obj[Sets]` is `SetoidObject`), not a universe inconsistency. Full mapping in **docs/SIZE.md**; the Awodey §8.3 Yoneda size statements are documented there but NOT formalized.

### Structures (Internal Properties)
- **Structure/Thin.v**: Thin categories — `Thin C := ∀ x y (f g : x ~> y), f ≈ g`, closed under opposites, with every morphism monic and epic and every opposing pair an iso candidate (`thin_iso`); `thin_preorder C` (inhabited-hom on objects, Prop-squashed; deliberately renamed to avoid shadowing `Theory/Category.v:282`'s exported Type-valued `hom_preorder` instance, which is the formulation that would dissolve the choice hypothesis — the squash is forced by `Instance/Proset.v`'s Prop-valued `Proset`) with its `PreOrder` witness (a plain `Definition`, deliberately not an instance). The thin-side reconstruction `thin_Proset_iso : Thin C → HomChoice C → C ≅[Cat] Proset (thin_PreOrder C)` takes the morphism-choice hypothesis explicitly (extracting a witness from the Prop-valued ∃ needs it; on prosets it is discharged, homs being the relation itself). Mac Lane §I.2 construction 7's correspondence, with strengths measured per leg in Instance/Proset/Order.v: `thin_preorder_Proset` recovers the relation pointwise-iff unconditionally, and on the nose (`thin_preorder_Proset_eq`) under propositional+functional extensionality taken as explicit hypotheses (sufficiency is what is proved; necessity is not claimed)
- **Instance/Proset/Order.v**: The order-theoretic development over `Instance/Proset.v` — Seven Sketches §§1.2.2/1.3.1 and Riehl §1.2. `Equivalence_is_PreOrder`/`PreOrder_Symmetric_is_Equivalence` (a preorder is an equivalence minus symmetry, both ways); discrete and codiscrete preorders with codiscrete terminal and discrete initial among preorder structures on a carrier (uniqueness clauses included); `comparable x y := (x ≤ y) + (y ≤ x)` with `TotalOrder` characterized by total comparability and a header note separating comparability from decidability; discrete-preorder comparability (self-comparable, distinct incomparable); `nat` a `TotalOrder` with the reverse order exhibited as `(ℕ,≤)^op`; `(Proset P)^op ≅ Proset (flip P)` in Cat (Riehl §1.2); infimum/supremum named and proved to coincide with the discrete-two-object limit/colimit over an *arbitrary preorder* (totality is needed only for existence, not the identification), two-element order worked, colimit half also restated through `IsAColimit` — stated directly; the general identification lemma belongs to issue #422 (whose suggested module is this same directory), and the header records that these are its binary-shape case, to be consumed rather than restated when #422 lands
- **Structure/Terminal.v**: Terminal and initial objects, and their uniqueness — the bundled `Terminal` class with `one`/`one_unique` and, by the `Initial C := Terminal (C^op)` notation, `Structure/Initial.v`'s `zero`/`zero_unique`; uniqueness up to a *unique* isomorphism in three strengths (`terminal_unique` + `terminal_arrow_unique`, packaged as the single `terminal_unique_up_to_unique_iso : ∃! _ : 1 ≅ 1, True` over `iso_setoid`, with the initial duals), and `zero_object_unique`/`zero_object_unique_initial` joined by the proved `zero_object_unique_compat` (Structure/ZeroObject.v). The object-level predicates `IsTerminalObj`/`IsInitialObj` (`∀ x, Unique (fun _ : x ~> c => True)`) convert both ways with the bundled classes and are what let terminality be used as a *property*: repleteness `Terminal_iso`, the full subcategory `TerminalObjects` with `TerminalObjects_Replete` and singleton hom-sets, and — given an inhabitant — `terminal_sub_contractible`, Riehl's Lemma 1.6.16 in full (`EquivalenceOfCategories (Erase (Sub C TerminalObjects))`; the "empty OR contractible" disjunction is *not* stated, since deciding it is excluded middle for "C has a terminal object" — Construction/Subcategory/Terminal.v). `Terminal_transport`/`Initial_transport` carry either object along an equivalence (Theory/Equivalence/Terminal.v). Riehl Exercise 1.6.i gives `terminal_initial_arrow_iso` and with it `ZeroObject_from_arrow`, the first in-tree construction that *derives* `zero_coincide` rather than assuming it. Terminality as a universal property is `TerminalIsUniversalProperty`/`InitialIsUniversalProperty` — the instance `Structure/UniversalProperty.v`'s `univ_property_unique_up_to_unique_iso` had always lacked at its own illustrating case — with `terminal_unique_up_to_unique_iso_via_UP` re-deriving the concrete statement through it (Structure/UniversalProperty/Terminal.v). Strict initiality is `initial_strict : (x ~> 0) → x ≅ 0` with `initial_strict_unique` (Structure/BiCCC.v — cartesian closed + initial suffices, no `Cocartesian`), instantiated at `Sets` and at skeletal `FinSet`, where it computes to `∀ x : nat, (x ~> 0) → x = 0` (Structure/BiCCC/Strict.v)
- **Structure/Cartesian.v**: Products via universal property
- **Structure/Cartesian/Closed.v**: Exponentials and internal hom (the live
  cartesian-closed development; `Structure/Closed.v` is an incomplete
  Eilenberg–Kelly stub whose `Class Closed` is not yet in force). The currying
  adjunction is packaged in Structure/Cartesian/Closed/Adjunction.v: the
  endofunctors `Prod_Functor S = (− × S)` and `Exp_Functor S = (−)^S` (the
  latter the partial application of Functor/Hom/Internal.v's internal-hom
  bifunctor, `Exp_Functor_InternalHom`), the named naturality lemma
  `eval_natural` with its bundled `eval_Transform : (−)^S × S ⟹ Id`, and
  `Curry_Adjunction : Prod_Functor S ⊣ Exp_Functor S` in the hom-set form of
  Theory/Adjunction.v — `curry`/`uncurry` as the transposes and `eval` as
  counit, all three by `reflexivity`, with the triangle identities inherited
  from the general theory; this turns the prose remark of Theory/Adjunction.v
  (lines 93-94) into a theorem. `Curry_Representable` is the first instance of
  `Functor/Representable.v`'s class (and its first consumer: resolution finds
  it, and the class's `Representable_to_obj` coercion reads the representing
  object back), witnessing Riehl's Example 2.1.6(iv) — the presheaf
  `C(− × S, B) : C^op ⟶ Sets` is represented by `B^S`, with the Yoneda
  universal element `eval` (`Curry_repr_universal`). That is a claim about
  THAT class: representability content also lives under
  `Structure/UniversalProperty.v`'s `IsUniversalProperty`, which has three
  instances. Note also that the counit's being `eval` on the nose is a
  consequence of the transposes alone, not of defining the exponential
  endofunctor directly — what the direct definition buys is that the four
  naturality fields discharge from the `Closed` corollaries verbatim
  (audit-corrected). The `Sets` model, where the counit computes to
  `e(h, s) = h s` and the transpose of addition on a `nat` setoid witnesses
  non-vacuity, is Instance/Sets/Cartesian/Closed/Adjunction.v, which also
  carries the Mac Lane §V.4 corollary that `− × X` preserves all colimits
  (`Sets_prod_preserves_colimits`, `CCC_prod_preserves_colimits`) — the
  library's only colimit-preservation witness of this kind
- **Structure/Cartesian/Closed/Natural.v**: Naturality of the exponential laws (Mac Lane §II.5 Ex 2; Riehl §1.4 Example 1.4.11) — the four pointwise isos upgraded to natural isomorphisms with their sides packaged as eight DIRECT functor records over product/opposite domains: `exp_prod_l_natural : @Isomorphism ([(C^op ∏ C^op) ∏ C, C]) …` and `exp_prod_r_natural` over Cartesian+Closed alone, `prod_coprod_r_natural`/`exp_coprod_natural` after a mid-section Cocartesian Context (so the first two never carry it). The components ARE the existing `exp_prod_l`/`exp_prod_r`/`prod_coprod_r`/`exp_coprod` DEFINITIONALLY — eight `reflexivity` agreement lemmas (stated with `≈`, morphisms) plus eight `eq_refl` object-action Examples (objects) — so the upgrade adds naturality, not new components. The transpose triangle on ARBITRARY generalized elements relates the internal iso to `exp_iso`'s legs: `exp_prod_l_transpose : to exp_prod_l ∘ f ≈ curry (curry (uncurry f ∘ to prod_assoc))` and `_untranspose` dually. Two per-variable SPECIMEN corollaries — the joint square instantiated at identity arrows, covering Law 1's base variable and Law 4's first summand only, the file saying so — since the joint squares imply every per-variable square; `NaturalIso_of_pointwise` (pointwise family + one square ⟹ natural iso) and the `ihom` calculus stated for reuse (`ihom` IS `InternalHomFunctor`'s fmap read in C, machine-checked at Leibniz `eq_refl` by `ihom_is_InternalHomFunctor_fmap`); `prod_assoc_to_natural`/`_from_natural` general but disclosed as not consumed by the squares. Engineering finding recorded in-file: an inline `fobj` inside `Program Definition` lets Program defer an unresolved `Cartesian` instance into an obligation that `Unset Transparent Obligations` (Lib/Foundation.v) makes OPAQUE — `fobj[F]` then converts with nothing; elaborate object/arrow actions as ordinary Definitions first. The Cat note is Instance/Cat/Exponential.v: all four pointwise isos AND all four `_natural` upgrades instantiate at Cat with explicitly spelled functor-category types (`[C ∏ D, E] ≅ [C, [D, E]]`, `[C, D ∏ E] ≅ [C, D] ∏ [C, E]`, `C ∏ (D ∐ E) ≅ (C ∏ D) ∐ (C ∏ E)`, `[D ∐ E, C] ≅ [D, C] ∏ [E, C]`), inhabited by the generic instances so the two readings agree by conversion — no universe wall. The decategorification half (Riehl's second clause) is Instance/FinSet/Decategorify.v: `Card_Groupoid : Groupoid Set_f ⟶ DiscreteCat nat` — THE cardinality functor from the groupoid of finite sets and bijections to the discrete category of naturals (fobj `setf_cardinality`, fmap `setf_cardinality_iso_invariant`, which forgets WHICH bijection — that forgetting IS decategorification; laws by axiom-free `UIP_dec` on nat) — with the identity-on-objects skeletal shadow `Card_FinSet : Groupoid FinSet ⟶ DiscreteCat nat` (fmap := `FinSet_skeletal`), and the four arithmetic identities DERIVED from the categorical isomorphisms — each PROOF TERM is `λ x y z, FinSet_skeletal <the iso>` with no arithmetic induction of its own (the counting lives inside `FinSet_skeletal`'s pigeonhole engine and FinSet's instance layer, per the file's disclosure; none of `lia`/`Nat.pow_*`/`Nat.mul_add_distr_l` appears): `nat_exp_prod_l : z^(x·y) = (z^y)^x`, `nat_exp_prod_r : (y·z)^x = y^x·z^x`, `nat_prod_coprod_r : x·(y+z) = x·y+x·z`, `nat_exp_coprod : x^(y+z) = x^y·x^z`, each literally `FinSet_skeletal` of the corresponding iso at skeletal FinSet (whose product/coproduct/exponential object actions are `*`/`+`/`^` on nat), with `nat_exp_coprod_is_Card_image` identifying one as `fmap[Card_FinSet]` of the iso by `eq_refl`, `DiscreteCat_nat_Thin` recording that the naturality content evaporates in the discrete target, and the header disclosing that `lia` would prove the identities — the derivation route is the content. Every `=` in that file is between OBJECTS (nats) or is a `DiscreteCat` hom (an equality proof), the setoid discipline's sanctioned exceptions
- **Structure/Monoidal.v**: Tensor products with coherence
- **Structure/Monoidal/CopyDiscard.v**: Copy/discard (gs-monoidal) categories — comonoid supply with no naturality; deterministic morphisms and the wide subcategory Det in CopyDiscard/Deterministic.v
- **Structure/Ring.v**, **Structure/Lattice.v**: Mac Lane §III.6 (book p. 75) — **ring objects and lattice objects by INTERNALIZATION**, the two examples Mac Lane names next after monoid and group objects, over `Cartesian` + `Terminal` and reusing `Structure/Monoid.v`'s `Monoid := @MonoidObject C CC_Monoidal`. **THE ISSUE'S SUGGESTED NAME WAS UNUSABLE, NOT MERELY CONFUSING**: `RingObject` (`Theory/Algebra/Rig.v:469`) AND `RigObject` (`:103`) are both in scope in `Structure/Ring.v`, whose `Sets` section compares against them (`Structure/Lattice.v` requires neither and compares against its own `SetoidLattice` instead) — hence `InternalSemiring`/`InternalRing`/`InternalSemilattice`/`InternalLattice`, with the header recording that the tree's own "full spelling = internal, abbreviation = set-level" convention (`GroupObject`/`GrpObject`) could NOT be followed because Rig.v inverts it. **THE MATHEMATICAL CONTENT IS WHAT IS DERIVED RATHER THAN ASSUMED, AND BOTH DERIVATIONS ARE MATCHED BY REFUTATIONS SHOWING THE ASSUMPTION IS OTHERWISE NECESSARY.** For a RING, **annihilation is a THEOREM** — `ring_annihilate_l`/`_r` via `ring_cancel_idem` (`k ≈ k + k` forces `k ≈ 0`), a morphism-level cancellation chase with NO elements — so `InternalRing_InternalSemiring` is a DERIVATION, not a repackaging; and `bool_or_not_annihilating` shows why a SEMIRING must assume it, disjunction on `bool` as BOTH halves satisfying commutativity and BOTH distributivity laws while REFUTING annihilation — so the annihilation PAIR is not implied by the rest, and `isr_annihilate_l` in particular is not; only the `_l` shape is refuted, `isr_annihilate_r`'s independence following from `bool_or_comm` but not stated separately. For a LATTICE, **idempotence is DERIVED from absorption alone** (`lattice_join_idem`/`lattice_meet_idem` — no unit, no unitor, no commutativity), so it is not a field, with `lattice_bot_meet`/`lattice_top_join` deriving the bound annihilations the same way and `bool_join_not_absorbing`/`bool_xor_not_idempotent` showing absorption and `isl_idem` are themselves independent. `InternalRing_GroupObject` passes `ir_add` through UNCHANGED with no transport anywhere — but read the inverse fields precisely: the ring's own `ir_neg_left`/`ring_neg_right` are REJECTED at those positions (`GroupObject` wants the `bimap`/`∆`/`eliminate` spelling where the ring states the `fork`/`one` one), and it is the bridging lemmas `ir_neg_left_split`/`ir_neg_right_split` whose statements meet the field types by conversion. Lattices are BOUNDED (the units are bottom and top); an unbounded variant would need a semigroup-object notion the tree does not have. `Sets` instances run both ways against Rig.v's records, with all five DATA fields returning at `eq_refl` on the set-level side. **Universes measured off BOTH the constraint block AND the binder**: all four classes are `@{u u0 u1}` over `Category@{u u0 u0}` — hom and proof IDENTIFIED, the OBJECT universe FREE (only `≤` bounds) — and the identification has **THREE INDEPENDENT DONORS**, `Cartesian`, `Terminal` and `Monoidal` each rejected under `Constraint uh < up` against a hom control accepted at those levels, so no single one is "the" cause. **`MonoidObject` is NOT a fourth donor and an earlier revision wrongly probed it as one**: its signature takes a `Monoidal@{u u0}` argument over `Category@{u u0 u0}`, so `@Monoidal C` is rejected before any field of it is consulted — it cannot be tested apart from `Monoidal`, and whether it identifies anything OF ITS OWN is UNKNOWN, not refuted. **`dup_left@{u u0}` is this tree's sharpest instance of an identification hiding in the BINDER**: its constraint block is LITERALLY EMPTY while its binder reads `Category@{u u0 u0}`, so a reader who checks only the block concludes "no identification" and is wrong — inherited from `Cartesian`, not introduced here. **A `Set` PIN WAS FOUND AND FIXED**: unannotated, `Sets_Monoid` minimizes to `SetoidObject@{Set Set}` and would have confined every `Sets` result to Set-sized carriers (the `Instance/Sets/Products.v:409-424` hazard again); explicit binders lift it, and all six Sets-side constants now carry `o < so` as their only STRICT constraint and no `Set` anywhere — the rest of each block is some fifteen `≤` bounds against donor universes (`projections`, `prod_rect`, `Basics.compose`, `eq_ind`), among them `o ≤ q` relating two DECLARED binders, so "and nothing else" would be wrong even read charitably. **A PRIOR-ART CORRECTION**: `GroupObject` (`Structure/Group.v:112`) is the only record with a `MonoidObject` field, but TWO more carry the SIBLING class `Monoid` — `Theory/Algebra/CommutativeMonoid.v:49` and `Theory/Algebra/Frobenius.v:128`, the latter pairing its monoid with a COMONOID (different variance, no absorption) — so what survives is the narrower "no in-tree class carries TWO monoid structures on one object **as `Monoid`-typed fields**", and all three records are named. **That qualifier is load-bearing and the headers now carry it**: the survivor was billed as a search "by shape rather than by name" when it is a sweep by the field type's NAME, and BY SHAPE the pattern DOES occur — sweeping for record bodies with two `_assoc` axioms returns `Theory/Algebra/Rig.v:103`'s `RigObject`, which carries `(rig_zero, rig_add)` and `(rig_one, rig_mul)` on one setoid carrier, elementwise rather than as internal monoid objects, and which is precisely the set-level theory these files internalize. **THE #389/#1003 HANDOVER IS NARROWER THAN IT LOOKS AND THE FILE SAYS SO**: `Instance/Proset/Limit.v:101-109` reserves `Meet`, `Join`, `Lattice` and `BooleanAlgebra` for this effort, but these classes are INTERNAL-ALGEBRAIC (an object of an arbitrary cartesian category carrying two operations), NOT order-theoretic, so the bridge to that file's `IsGLB`/`IsLUB` is explicitly NOT delivered and a later effort still has to build it. Two engineering findings documented in both headers: `mappend`'s type `fobj tensor (mon, mon) ~> mon` is CONVERTIBLE with `mon × mon` but not syntactically equal, so two elaborations of `mappend[M] ∘ swap` record different object arguments and `rewrite` cannot match them — every `mappend`/`mempty` is therefore ASCRIBED; and `Structure/Group.v` declares `Notation "'inverse' [ G ]"`, making `inverse` a keyword that no `@inverse` or field assignment can parse, so `InternalRing_GroupObject` applies `@Build_GroupObject` positionally. Computing witnesses `Nat_ISemiring`, `Int_IRing` (0 ≉ 1) and `Bool_Lattice`/`Bool_ILattice` (⊥ ≉ ⊤). **104/104 and 103/103 constants closed under the global context** — `Print Module` lists **102 and 100**, every `Record` INCLUDED (`SetoidLattice` among them), so the unlisted names are the **five `Build_*` constructors** and nothing else: 102+2 = 104, 100+3 = 103. `Test/ProbeRingLattice340.v` pins the three FORMABILITY donor negatives the targets cannot state (a library file cannot carry a `Constraint` declaration without constraining itself), each stripped and its kind counted separately, plus the `dup_left`/`dup_right` binder measurement as `About`-able positives; the targets' own four refutations are all CONVERSION, so the two kinds are split across the files. Rename-simulated **3/3** unpadded with `Require` lines PRESERVED. A first run scored 3/4 and showed `Monoidal` named ONLY inside negative 3 — that was the tell that negative 3 was testing `Monoidal` and never reaching `MonoidObject`, and it was first read as an incidental guard gap; the negative is now written as what it measures. NOT delivered: no Lawvere bridge (`Theory/Lawvere/Model.v`'s `Model`/`Models` are a header pointer, explicitly not a theorem); no category of internal (semi)rings or lattices, no homomorphisms or forgetful functors, so the `Sets` round trips are NOT packaged as an equivalence; no closure under products or exponentials; no instance in any ambient category other than `Sets`, so "a ring object in Top is a topological ring" and "the opens of a space form a lattice object" stay prose; no commutative-ring variant, ideals or modules; no unbounded lattice; no distributive lattices, Heyting or Boolean algebras (that is #389); and `SetoidLattice` is a set-level record living under `Structure/`, off-pattern for `Theory/Algebra/` and disclosed as forced by the two-file scope
- **Structure/Monoidal/Markov.v**: Markov categories (copy/discard + semicartesian); Fox's theorem in Markov/Fox.v (all-deterministic ⟺ cartesian)
- **Structure/Premonoidal.v**: Premonoidal categories over Structure/Binoidal.v — centre Z(C) as a monoidal wide subcategory (Binoidal/Central.v, Premonoidal/Centre.v), Monoidal ⟺ all-central premonoidal (Premonoidal/Monoidal.v), Freyd/effectful categories (Premonoidal/Freyd.v); Kleisli premonoidal structure and thunkability in Monad/Kleisli/{Premonoidal,Commutative}.v and Monad/Thunkable.v; graded monads in Monad/Graded.v; strict premonoidal = monoid in (StrictCat, □) in Instance/StrictCat/Premonoid.v
- **Structure/Monoidal/Drinfeld.v**: The Drinfeld (monoidal) centre `Z(C)` — `HalfBraiding` (a natural family satisfying the half-braiding hexagon), objects `(x, half-braiding)` with intertwiner morphisms (proof-irrelevant witness), `Drinfeld_Monoidal`, `Drinfeld_Braided` (a braided monoidal category with BOTH hexagon identities at full strength), and the forgetful `Drinfeld_Forget`; this is the monoidal centre, distinct from the premonoidal centre of Binoidal/Central + Premonoidal/Centre
- **Structure/Monoidal/StarAutonomous.v**: Star-autonomous categories (definition level) over a genuine general base `SymMonClosed` — symmetric monoidal closed, introduced in-file because the in-tree `ClosedMonoidal` bundles `CartesianMonoidal` (over a cartesian closed base a dualizing object forces a preorder by Joyal's no-go, excluding Rel/FdVect/coherence spaces); provides the dual/linear-negation functor `(- ⇒ ⊥) : C^op ⟶ C`, the double-dual endofunctor, and the `StarAutonomous` class (dualizing object, closed transpose, double-dual iso with naturality), with par/linear-distributivity and the canonicity coherence ledgered
- **Structure/Monoidal/Dual.v**: Mac Lane §IV.2 construction 3 (book p. 88, `maclane:IV.2:construction3`) — **the dual-object functor is adjoint to ITSELF on the right**, and the canonical double-dual map is CONSTRUCTED rather than assumed invertible. `dual_self_adjoint_on_the_right d : AdjointOnTheRight (dual d) (dual d)` for an arbitrary object `d` of an arbitrary `SymMonClosed` — **not** the cartesian-bundled `ClosedMonoidal`, which the issue's reviewer check excludes because a cartesian base would trivialise the example. No field failed and none was weakened: all four naturality laws are instances of `dual_transpose_nat_dom`/`_nat_cod`, and both isomorphism laws are `dual_transpose_invol`. **THE TYPE FIT IS EXACT**: with `A = X = C` and `S = T = dual d` (both `C^op ⟶ C`), #358's class unfolds to Mac Lane's `C(a, x ⇒ d) ≅ C(x, a ⇒ d)`. **THE DONOR DEVIATION DICTATES THE WHOLE FILE**: `StarAutonomous.v`'s `ump_exponents'` is in `∃!` form and is NOT wired to `curry' := to exp_iso`, so the beta law for the packaged `curry'` is underivable from the class fields — everything transposes through `dcur`, never `curry'`, and `dcur_respects` is `#[local]` so nothing downstream may `rewrite` under `dcur` (`dcur_Proper`, `:312`, restates it in three lines and is re-exported as `dcur_Proper_ex`). By contrast the `Remove Hints Sets_Product_Monoidal` line is **DEFENSIVE, not required, and that is measured**: deleting it leaves BOTH the target and the probe compiling clean, with both probe negatives failing byte-identically — a scout reported it as necessary from its own scratch file and that reading does NOT transfer, because here `C` arrives carrying a `SymMonClosed` instance whose coercion already determines the monoidal structure, so resolution is never offered a choice. **PRIOR ART, MEASURED — AND THIS IS NOT THE CLASS'S FIRST SELF-ADJOINT WITNESS**: `Powerset_AdjointOnTheRight` (`Adjunction/Right.v:717`) already has `Powerset_Prop_op` in BOTH slots, so no first-`S = T` claim is made; what is new is the first witness arising from a MONOIDAL CLOSED structure rather than a fixed concrete category, and the first identification of the resulting unit and counit with a canonical map (the words `double dual` occur nowhere in `Right.v`). Two near-collisions were found by sweeping all fifty declared names and renamed around rather than risked — `dual_ev` is taken by `Instance/FdVect/DoubleDual.v:329` and `double_dual_natural` has 9 occurrences across the two `Instance/FdVect` files — because `make print-assumptions` loads many modules into ONE scope, where a shared name silently audits the wrong constant. **THE UNIT AND COUNIT ARE BOTH `dd_unit`, AT `eq_refl`**: `unit_is_dd_unit` and `counit_is_dd_unit`, which is Mac Lane's remark that for a self-adjunction on the right the counit IS the unit read in the opposite category — and it is not a packaging coincidence, since `aor_to_is_from_swapped` records at `eq_refl`, on the WHOLE `SetoidMorphism` rather than pointwise, that `to aor` and `from aor` are the SAME function `dual_transpose` at swapped indices. Off the diagonal the two legs do not even share a type, so `to = from` is not statable there — pinned as the TYPING negative. **EXACTLY ONE identification falls back to `≈`, with the residue exhibited rather than described**: `dd_unit` is `dual_transpose id`, which unfolds to `dcur (eval' ∘ (id ⨂ id) ∘ braid)` against the donor header's own phrase `dcur (eval' ∘ braid)`; the two differ by `id ⨂ id`, and `bimap`'s `fmap_id` is an opaque law field so conversion cannot remove it. The DIRECTION of that fallback was a design choice made on a measurement — spelling `dd_unit` the other way round would move the `≈` onto the unit/counit identifications, which are what the construction is actually about. **THE STAR-AUTONOMOUS RELATION IS MADE EXPLICIT, FILLING A GAP THE DONOR ITSELF FLAGGED**: `StarAutonomous.v`'s header says `star_double_dual` "posits SOME natural isomorphism … rather than requiring the CANONICAL dualization map … to be invertible", deferring the pinning to its ledger 4; here `StarAutonomous_of_dd_unit` shows that if the canonical map is invertible the class is inhabited with `star_double_dual`'s FORWARD LEG literally `dd_unit` (`star_double_dual_is_dd_unit`, `eq_refl` — the field is an `Isomorphism`, so it cannot be a morphism itself), and `dual_AdjointEquivalence` upgrades the self-adjunction. `StarAutonomous.v` is NOT modified. **UNIVERSES MEASURED OFF BOTH BINDER AND BLOCK — AND THE BLOCK ALONE GIVES THE WRONG ANSWER**: every constant is over `C : Category@{u u0 u0}`, hom identified with proof by REUSING the level variable in the BINDER, while the constraint blocks carry NO equation at all, only bounds. The identification is INHERITED and FOUR donors are each sufficient alone (`Opposite`, `Monoidal`, `SymmetricMonoidal`, `SymMonClosed`, each rejected under `Constraint ch < cp` against applied hom/identity controls accepted at those very levels) — but **read four as four DONORS, not four independent CAUSES**: the monoidal three NEST (`SymMonClosed` ⊃ `SymmetricMonoidal` ⊃ `BraidedMonoidal` ⊃ `Monoidal`), so at most TWO are independent and `Monoidal` alone already suffices. No `Set` in any binder or block; none claimed unavoidable. **50/50 constants closed under the global context** — 42 source-declared plus the EIGHT `Program` obligations no source sweep sees (six for the self-adjunction, two for the unit); the file declares no `Record`/`Class`/`Inductive`, so there is no unlisted `Build_*`. **SEVEN negatives of THREE KINDS plus a separate instrument check** (eight `Fail` commands in all; the probe has two negatives plus its own instrument), and the tail alone does NOT separate two of them: the four FORMABILITY ones end `universe inconsistency`, the two CONVERSION ones end `cannot unify` between two terms of ONE type, and the TYPING one ALSO ends `cannot unify` but on the OBJECT VARIABLES, its body reporting two DIFFERENT objects of `Sets` as expected and actual. `Test/ProbeDual359.v` restates the name-bearing ones from OUTSIDE the target (an in-file `Fail` renames in lockstep with what it guards) and mirrors the target's full `Require` list AND its `Remove Hints` line, without which a negative could fail for a reason it never measured. NOT delivered: **no concrete `SymMonClosed` witness, so every result is a conditional** (inherited — the donor constructs none either, and the in-tree `ClosedMonoidal` is cartesian and excluded on the donor's stated no-go grounds); no CONVERSE to `StarAutonomous_of_dd_unit` (the class does not tie its field to the canonical map — CONVERSION negative 2 pins that conversion does not SETTLE the equation, explicitly NOT that it is false, and no countermodel is claimed); no `EquivalenceOfCategories` composite; no self-adjointness on the LEFT and no instantiation of `right_does_not_imply_left`; no uniqueness of the dual functor; no functoriality or naturality in `d`; no triangle identities restated in `Adjunction/Natural/Transformation.v`'s vocabulary; and nothing about `⅋`, linear distributivity or Barr coherence, which stay the donor's ledger 4.
- **Structure/Limit.v**: Limits and colimits — with the transfer vocabulary alongside: preservation and iso-reflection in Structure/Limit/Preservation.v, and CREATION in Structure/Limit/Creation.v. The cone-level predicate `IsLimitCone` (a given cone is universal) is what the apex-only `IsALimit` cannot express and what `PreservesImageLimit` (Construction/Comma/Limit.v) had been writing out by hand — the two bridges are proof-free repackagings whose round trips are `eq_refl` (Construction/Comma/Creation.v), so GAFT and SAFT already consume it. `CreatesLimit K F` carries three fields (a lift of every limiting cone downstairs, a cone isomorphism from its image, and reflection); Mac Lane §V.1's other two clauses are theorems, not fields — `creates_limiting` (the lift is limiting) and `creates_lift_unique` (any cone lying over the given one is canonically isomorphic to the lift, uniqueness in the form a setoid library supports). `StrictlyCreatesLimit` is Riehl §3.4.7's on-the-nose variant over `StrictLift` (object `=` on the apex, `≈` on the legs), with `StrictlyCreatesLimit_CreatesLimit` relating them. Mac Lane §V.4 Theorem 2 in both halves: `creation_preserves_limit` (cone-level, with the downstairs limit as an explicit hypothesis, per Riehl's footnote) and `creates_limits_Complete`/`creates_limits_continuous`, plus creation under composition and the colimit duals by `C^op`. Witnesses: `EM_Forget` strictly creates every limit and limits of algebras are computed on carriers (Monad/Eilenberg/Moore/Limit.v, with `EM_Complete` proving Structure/Complete.v:58's standing prose, and an axiom-free instantiation at `Id_Monad` over `Coq`); `comma_proj2` given `Complete C` (Construction/Comma/Creation.v, its lift agreeing with the chosen downstairs limit by `eq_refl` on both apex and legs); and every equivalence, hence `Id[C]` (Theory/Equivalence/Creation.v), which is also how `equivalence_creates_limits` finally earns its name
- **Structure/Pullback/Reduction.v**, **Structure/Pullback/Wide.v**: The classical interdefinability of the finite (co)limit generators (Mac Lane §III.3 Ex 2, §III.4 Ex 7/9/10; Awodey §5.2 Prop 5.7 + Cor 5.8, §5.4 Prop 5.16, §5.7 Ex 3/5; Riehl 2nd ed. §3.5 Lemmas 3.5.15/3.5.16) — the reduction toolkit `Structure/Topos.v` had disclosed as unformalized since it was written, and the two `jww` TODOs `Structure/Pullback.v` had carried since 2017, both retired in the same commit. Every reduction is stated FIRST at the apex-pinned level (`IsPullback`/`IsEqualizer`/`IsCartesianProduct`/`IsIndexedProduct`) and only then packaged: `pullback_of_equalizer` with `HasPullbacks_of_Cartesian_HasEqualizers`, the Awodey converse `equalizer_of_pullback` (which deliberately does NOT use `equalizer_monic` — the pullback's own uniqueness clause discharges the factorization directly, disclosed in-header as a departure from Awodey's argument), the kernel pair as an equalizer (`kernel_pair_IsEqualizer`, tied to the PRE-EXISTING `Structure/Regular.v:46` `kernel_pair` by `kernel_pair_is_pullback_along_itself` rather than shadowing it), `product_of_pullback`/`Cartesian_of_HasPullbacks_Terminal`/`HasEqualizers_of_HasPullbacks_Terminal`, and the dual `HasPushouts_of_Cocartesian_HasCoequalizers`. **The issue's own framing was WRONG and the file proves it rather than repeating it**: Mac Lane's Ex 9 and the Awodey/Riehl construction are NOT "the same square read the other way" — Mac Lane pulls back `⟨id,f⟩` and `⟨id,g⟩`, BOTH issuing from `x`, over `x × y`, while Awodey and Riehl pull the diagonal `y ~> y × y` back along `⟨f,g⟩`. The refutation comes in TWO KINDS kept lexically apart: the codomains differ (CONVERSION), and the legs are not even parallel so the comparison CANNOT BE STATED (FORMABILITY — `aw_pair f g : x ~> y × y` against `x ~> x × y`). Structurally, both Mac Lane legs are split monic with the one common retraction `exl`, whereas `exl ∘ aw_pair` is `f`, which is not an identity FOR GENERAL `f` — a hedge that matters, since at `f := id` Awodey's leg IS split, by this file's own `ml_right_split`; the lemma was renamed `aw_pair_retracts_to_f` because its first name, `aw_pair_not_split`, asserted more than it proves and more than is true. What IS proved is the weaker positive statement: the two vertices are canonically isomorphic AS EQUALIZERS (`maclane_awodey_iso`, `maclane_awodey_iso_commutes` — commuting with BOTH equalizing maps, not merely an iso of objects), with one degenerate definitional coincidence at `f := id`. It is NOT proved that no cospan isomorphism exists at particular `f`, `g`, and the header says so. **The duality is measured, not assumed**: `HasCoequalizers C = HasEqualizers (C^op)` is REFUTED at `eq_refl` (records are nominal) — yet every FIELD type IS convertible, so both bridges are `:=` with no tactic and both record round trips close by `eq_refl`; the residue is exactly TWO steps, `HasEqualizers_op_of_HasCoequalizers` and its mirror `HasCoequalizers_of_HasEqualizers_op`, each destructing the `sigT` (an earlier draft said one, overlooking the second in the same section). `Coprod`/`inl`/`inr` are the op-product/`exl`/`exr` by `eq_refl`, so section (A) applies at `C^op` verbatim. Wide.v adds `IsWidePullback`, `WidePullback`, `HasWidePullbacks` (declared but UNINHABITED) and Riehl's Lemma 3.5.15 in BOTH directions (`wide_pullback_product`/`product_wide_pullback`), with `one_unique` VISIBLE as a single named step — discharging the commutativity condition forward, manufacturing it backward. **The empty index comes out right, and for a structural reason**: the terminal object IS the empty wide pullback, proved both ways and genuinely consuming the emptiness — the opposite outcome from the sibling `Structure/Equalizer/Wide.v`, whose empty case is an obstruction RELATIVE TO ITS LIMIT PRESENTATION, and no pointed section or `i0` hypothesis appears here. **Read the contrast narrowly.** An earlier draft of this bullet explained it by saying a wide fork "carries a second leg the empty condition leaves unconstrained" — that is FALSE about that file's record (`IsWideEqualizer` has two fields and ONE leg, and that file says three times that no leg-carrying record is built there), and the polarity was inverted: the fork's LACK of a second leg is why `i0` is needed, the cone's second leg being MANUFACTURED as `fs i0 ∘ h`. The honest statement is weaker — this file builds NO limit presentation, so no analogous disagreement can arise, and the contrast partly reflects that less is claimed here rather than that the notion is better behaved. Non-vacuity at `Sets_HasIndexedProducts`. SCOPE, and it is narrower than "finite limits are redundant": only the BINARY and PARALLEL-PAIR generators are related. No finite-shape induction is performed, so `Complete`/`Cocomplete` is NOT concluded and Riehl 3.5.15 is not stated for arbitrary finite shape; (E)'s hypotheses are not shown minimal and have no converse; the produced `Cartesian` is not compared with any pre-existing one on the same category; `IsWidePullback` has NO limit presentation (nothing relates it to a `Limit` over a wide-cospan shape, so there is no analogue of `Structure/Pullback/Limit.v`); `wide_pullback_unique` gives a bare `≅` with no leg equations; and Reduction.v makes no universe claim at all. `Test/ProbeReduction.v` guards the boundary — 8 negatives (3 conversion in the D-section, 2 formability, 2 duality, 1 wide) plus a separate instrument check, against controls naming their own constants, each negative stripped and its failure KIND confirmed. The rename guarantee was NOT true as first shipped — an independent audit found `ml_right`, `HasCoequalizers` and `HasEqualizers` named in no control, so renaming any of them left the file compiling and its negative vacuously green; controls for all three were added, the same defect family two earlier probe files in this tree shipped with. Wide.v's three universe measurements were likewise measured-but-unguarded and are now shipped as probe sections, the donor `IsIndexedProduct` rejected at the same levels so that "inherited" is measured rather than asserted; Wide.v ships no `Fail` of its own, so its refutations are pinned there for the first time

- **Structure/Limit/Unique.v**: Riehl §3.1 Proposition 3.1.7 — essential uniqueness of limits WITH the leg equations, from the mediator calculus alone (`limit_med`/`limit_med_commutes`/`limit_med_eq`, no representability detour): `limit_unique_iso : c ≅ d` between any two `IsALimit F` witnesses, `limit_unique_iso_legs` carrying both leg-commutation families (the reviewer bar: not a bare `≅`), `limit_unique_iso_unique` (ANY morphism commuting with the two leg families is the isomorphism — the proposition's content), and the automorphism corollary `limit_endo_id`; the colimit statements are literal op-instances of the limit ones re-typed through the covariant `colimit_inj` vocabulary (`colimit_unique_iso`/`_injs`/`_unique`, `colimit_endo_id`; the mediator is definitionally the opposite side's, `colimit_unique_to_is_op`) — not a second proof. - **Theory/Algebra/Monoid/Product.v**: Mac Lane §VII.3 Exercise 1 (+ Awodey §2.6's componentwise product) — **the category of internal monoids has finite products**: `Mon_Cartesian : @Cartesian (Mon C)` and `Mon_Terminal : @Terminal (Mon C)`, with the product object `x × y` carrying `mu := (μX ∘ exl⨂exl) △ (μY ∘ exr⨂exr)` and `eta := ηX △ ηY`, the terminal monoid being `1` with both structure maps `one`, and `exl`/`exr`/`fork`/`one` each proved a monoid homomorphism. **THE ISSUE'S CENTRAL OBSTACLE IS REAL, THOUGH NARROWER THAN A FIRST DRAFT OF THIS BULLET SAID — TWO SPELLINGS PRINT ALIKE, NOT THREE.** `Theory/Algebra/Monoid.v` declares `Class Monoid (X : C)` with fields `mu`/`eta`/`mu_assoc` over a GENERAL monoidal base, and that is what `Mon` is built on (`Theory/Algebra/Monoid/Hom.v:6` Requires `Category.Theory.Algebra.Monoid` and never `Structure/Monoid.v`); `Structure/Monoid.v:124` declares the sibling `Class MonoidObject` (`mempty`/`mappend`/…); and `Structure/Monoid.v:173` declares `Definition Monoid := @MonoidObject C CC_Monoidal`, an ALIAS at the CARTESIAN base. `Product_Monoid` (`:179`) takes the alias as its ARGUMENTS and concludes `MonoidObject (product_obj x y)` **with the monoidal instance suppressed in the display**. Measured precisely: `@Monoid` and `@MonoidObject` both print `∀ C : Category, Monoidal → obj[C] → Type` and are indistinguishable, but the `:173` alias prints `∀ C : Category, Cartesian → Terminal → obj[C] → Type` — **visibly different**, so a single `Check` separates it and the claim that display "cannot tell the three apart" was FALSE and is withdrawn. What genuinely bites is that `Product_Monoid`'s CONCLUSION does not reveal which tensor it is at. `Structure/Monoid.v:42-46` calls the two "equivalent up to field renaming", which is exactly right, and the passage built here (`Monoid_of_MonoidObject`/`MonoidObject_of_Monoid`, both round trips at `eq_refl` on the WHOLE record, both being primitive-projection records with eta) is trivial — **but trivial only AT A FIXED BASE, and that qualification is the whole design question**. **THE RECONCILIATION ROUTE WAS CHOSEN ON A MEASUREMENT, NOT ON TASTE, AND THE PAYOFF IS CONCRETE**: the product monoid is proved DIRECTLY on the `mu`/`eta` class over an ARBITRARY `Monoidal`, never routing through `Product_Monoid` — because `Product_Monoid`'s tensor IS the categorical product, so routing through it would force `Mon`'s monoidal structure to be `CC_Monoidal` and shrink the theorem from "every monoidal base with finite products" to "bases monoidal via their own products", narrower than both Mac Lane and `Mon`'s own signature. That is not hypothetical: the tree's own `Mon_Sets` is `@Mon Sets Sets_Product_Monoidal` (`Instance/Roster.v:390`), and **`Sets_Product_Monoidal` is NOT `CC_Monoidal Sets`** — pinned as a conversion negative — so the routed version would not have reached it, while the direct one does (`Mon_Sets_Cartesian`/`Mon_Sets_Terminal`). Two scope notes: the identification with Roster's `Mon_Sets` is by SOURCE READING, `Instance/Roster.v` being deliberately not required, so no `eq_refl` pins it; and the negative refutes CONVERTIBILITY, not isomorphism — a transport along a monoidal equivalence is simply not in tree. **NO CARTESIAN HYPOTHESIS IS NEEDED ON THE MONOIDAL SIDE**: `Mon_Cartesian` takes a general `Monoidal C` plus `Cartesian C`, the two binary operations (⨂ and ×) never interacting — the proof's naturality inputs are `Monoidal` FIELDS, though "only" would be inaccurate — it also spends `bimap_comp` and the two `bimap_id_*` lemmas (tensor functoriality), the component monoids' own laws, and the `Cartesian` fork calculus. `Mon_Forget`'s action on the product and terminal DATA is Leibniz-equal to `C`'s, recorded by six `eq_refl` Examples (product object, terminal object, `exl`, `exr`, `fork`, `one`) — but **"creates" in `Structure/Limit/Creation.v`'s sense is NOT claimed**, and cone-level preservation is not stated. Strengths measured strict-first: the unit agrees with `Product_Monoid`'s at `eq_refl` while the MULTIPLICATION is `≈` only, and the diagnosis is neither proof fields nor missing eta but genuinely different TERMS — `Product_Monoid` writes `split μX μY ∘ toggle` where this file writes the fork of two composites, the residue being one `fork_comp` then `fork_inv` then `exl_fork`/`exr_fork`, all `Qed` corollaries in `Structure/Cartesian.v` so no unfolding converts them. **Universes measured off BOTH block and binder**: every constant THAT BINDS A `C` is over `C : Category@{u u0 u0}` (the `Mon_Sets_*` pair binds none, being at a fixed base) — hom and proof identified — and that is attributed by probe rather than assumed, each of `@Monoidal C`, `@Cartesian C` and `@Terminal C` being independently rejected under `Constraint uh < up` while `x ~> y` and `id[x]` are accepted at those levels, so there are three donors and nothing here adds to it; the blocks carry only bounds, with `Monoid_Product` and the hom lemmas adding a strict `u0 < u1` and three `prod_rect` bounds that have **TWO SEPARATE CAUSES** (a first draft attributed both to the first): `fork_inv`'s Type-valued conjunction accounts for the `prod_rect` bounds ONLY, while the strict `u0 < u1` comes from setoid `rewrite` inside a `Program` obligation — isolated by rebuilding the same shape twice, `apply`-discharged obligations giving `@{u u0}` and `now rewrite` ones giving `@{u u0 u1}`, with the supplying `Morphisms` constant not identified; **the packaging is NOT free** — `Mon_Cartesian@{u u0 u1 u2}` concludes `Cartesian@{u2 u0}` with a NEW level `u2` for `obj[Mon C]`, bounded below by both of `C`'s levels and identified with neither (the bounds are measured, the sigma explanation an attribution) — and at `Sets` it costs nothing, `Mon_Sets_Cartesian@{u u0} : Cartesian@{u0 u}`, since `Sets`' own `u < u0` already satisfies both. No `Set` anywhere. **A PROBE-HYGIENE EPISODE IS RECORDED IN-FILE RATHER THAN QUIETLY FIXED**: the first version of the typing negative sat INSIDE the section, where `C` and `M` are section VARIABLES rather than parameters, so the application passed `C` into the object slot and the command failed with an ARITY error at an argument elaborated before the nominal subject — a false pass saying nothing about the monoidal instance. Moving it after `End` fixed it, and the negative now fires on `Product_Monoid X Y` itself. **SEVEN negatives now, THREE KINDS**: the original four (2 typing, 2 conversion), each stripped and its kind read, plus **three FORMABILITY negatives added after audit** — the universe measurement had been REPORTED in the header and pinned NOWHERE, so nothing in the build would have noticed an upstream annotation changing it; `@Monoidal C`, `@Cartesian C` and `@Terminal C` are now each rejected under a section-local `Constraint uh < up` against controls at those very levels. A missing control was added too: `@CC_Monoidal` applied to explicit arguments had occurred ONLY inside its own `Fail`, so a signature change would have turned that guard vacuously green with an `Illegal application` rather than a `cannot unify`. Rename-simulated over the constants the negatives name — **twelve, not thirteen**, `Monoidal` being named by no negative. No separate `Test/` probe file: the target carries all seven with controls beside them, section-local `Universes`/`Constraint` being measured not to leak. **40/40 constants closed under the global context** — `Print Module` lists all 40 including the 9 `Program` obligations, and the file declares no `Record`, so there is no unlisted `Build_*`. Read the GRADE: that is a ONE-TIME measurement; the `make print-assumptions` gate carries **18 of the 40** permanently, the ungated remainder being `Monoid_Product_mu_is_Product_Monoid`, four of the six `Mon_Forget_*`, `prod_fork_eta`, `mon_prod_mu`/`_eta`, the four `mon_prod_*_ex*` lemmas, `mon_prod_assoc_leg` and all nine obligations. (The direct in-file use of `right_inverse` is in `right_inverse_r` at `:427`, not in `grp_prod_right_leg`, which uses `right_inverse_r`.) **HOW #503 IS CONSUMED, AT THREE MEASURED GRADES — and `Mon_Cartesian` itself is NOT among them, which is the honest part**: `Mon C`'s objects are `{x & Monoid x}` and `GrpCat`'s are `{x & GroupObject x}`, a DIFFERENT sigma, so there is no `Cartesian` transport between them and none is claimed; what instantiates are #503's factored COMPONENTS. The list was built from four GUESSED names rather than derived from this file's own declared names under the rename, so `GrpCat_Forget_Faithful` ↦ `Grp_Forget_Faithful` was never searched for at all. **TWO ENGINEERING FINDINGS, both about NAMES and both reproduced with their exact messages**: `inverse` is effectively a KEYWORD downstream of `Structure/Group.v:131`, whose notation quotes the token, so `@inverse _ _ _ G` is a **PARSE** error (`[global] or [pattern_ident] expected after '@'`) and `{| … ; inverse := _ |}` is rejected (`['|}'] expected after [record_declaration]`) — and **neither can be pinned as a `Fail` probe, because a parse error aborts the file even inside `Fail`**, which is why both are documented in prose; the workarounds are the qualified `@Category.Structure.Group.inverse` (a qualified path DOES parse after `@`) and `Build_GroupObject`, with `group_inverse_is` pinning agreement by `eq_refl`. The negative therefore refutes only the same-RECORD claim. **Universes: the BINDER and the BLOCK disagree, and that is the finding** — every constant binding a `C` is over `Category@{u u0 u0}` (hom and proof IDENTIFIED; 110 occurrences over the 55 named constants, 140 over all 70), while the constraint blocks contain **no `=` at all**, so reading the blocks alone would report no identification. the uniqueness triple is what replaces it. So all three adjunctions the DoD asks for exist, and the reason they are not one string is a theorem. **`objects_not_functorial_over_Cat : ObjRespectsCat → False` IS THE KEYSTONE**: over `Cat` the swap endofunctor of `Indiscrete bool` is naturally isomorphic to the identity (every hom-set is `unit`, so `Id ≈ SwapI` — `swap_iso_in_Cat`) while moving every object, and any objects functor must compare object maps by LEIBNIZ equality since `ObjSetoid`'s `equiv` IS `@eq`. Hence no objects functor exists on `Cat` **INTO A TARGET COMPARING OBJECT MAPS BY LEIBNIZ EQUALITY** — and that qualifier is load-bearing, not a hedge: an objects functor `Cat ⟶ Sets` over the ISOMORPHISM setoid DOES exist (compiled, zero obligations, closed under the global context, both actions `eq_refl`), and issue #357's item 1 offers exactly that alternative. So the strict reading is the EXERCISE'S CHOICE; what is forced by refutation is that ONCE it is taken the domain and codomain follow. This vindicates the note at `Theory/Connected/Components.v:576-578`. **A SECOND CRUX THE ISSUE DOES NOT ANTICIPATE**: the target must be `Coq`, not `Sets` — `disc_carrier_not_Sets_functor` and `ind_carrier_not_Sets_functor` refute both halves, because `Sets`' hom-setoid identifies maps that are not pointwise Leibniz-equal (a 'blurred' `bool` witnesses it) while `StrictCat`'s `eq_on_obj` demands exactly that. `Coq` is AN in-tree category of sets whose `≈` on morphisms is pointwise `=` and the natural choice here — not the only one, `Instance/Ens.v:47,:69` and `Instance/EnsV.v:195` also qualify. **TWO OF THE ISSUE'S PREMISES ARE FALSE, both about things already in tree**: it says the codiscrete half 'has no construction at all — searching for it finds only comments', whereas `Indiscrete (A : Type) : Category` is `Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v:416` with `hom := fun _ _ => unit` (reused, not rebuilt); and its item 4 asks that the components functor be defined, whereas `Pi0 : Cat ⟶ Sets` is `Theory/Connected/Components.v:579` (reused — only the adjunction was missing). **A DONOR `Set` PIN WAS FOUND AND ROUTED AROUND**: `Instance/Discrete.v:57`'s `DiscreteCat_Functor` (`:52` before this commit's own prose edit moved it) is universe-unannotated and pins its SOURCE at `DiscreteCat@{u Set Set}`, so the string is built over a locally annotated `disc_ext` instead. Strengths measured strict-first: the objects functor's object and arrow actions, the discrete functor's, both legs of the transposition, and — the two worth naming — **the unit IS the identity and the counit's object action IS the identity, both at `eq_refl`**; the single `≈`-only step IN `Objects.v` — the other two files have their own — is the round trip `disc_ext (fobj[F]) ≈ F`, which spends exactly `fmap_id` (and note `apply fmap_id` FAILS there, the elaborator declining to unify `id{?C}` against `eq_refl` — `exact (@fmap_id _ _ x a)` works). `Build_Adjunction'` is the cheap presentation because its two naturality clauses live in `Coq`, where `≈` is pointwise `=`, so both close by `reflexivity`; the full `Build_Adjunction` would have put them in `StrictCat` as transport-laden strict-equality goals. Separations proved rather than assumed: `Objects_not_Faithful` and `disc_indisc_not_iso`. **184/184 constants closed under the global context** — 118 source-declared plus **66 `Program` obligations** no source sweep sees — with **zero name collisions** across all 118. The prose at `Instance/Discrete.v` and `Structure/Discrete.v` that named these adjoints without building them is updated to cite them AND corrected: both said `Set ⟶ Cat`, which the keystone refutes. NOT delivered: Mac Lane's full four-term string as one object — nothing here claims it is in the tree; and the issue's Verification block pins `Discrete_Objects_Adjunction`, `Objects_Indiscrete_Adjunction` and `Components_Discrete_Adjunction`, none of which exist (delivered as `Disc_Objects_Adjunction`, `Objects_Indisc_Adjunction`, `Components_Disc_Adjunction`).
- **Structure/Limit/Constant.v**: Mac Lane §IV.2 Exercise 8 (book p. 90) — **the limit and colimit of a CONSTANT diagram over a connected shape are the constant itself**, with the diagonal fully faithful and the limits ABSOLUTE. **THE HYPOTHESIS IS `ConnectedNonempty`, NOT `Connected`, AND BOTH HALVES ARE PROVED NECESSARY** — which is sharper than the issue asks: `const_limit_not_from_bare_connected` refutes the bare in-tree `Connected` reading at the EMPTY shape (`_0` is vacuously connected, `Zero_Connected`, yet the limit of a constant diagram there is the TERMINAL object, and `TwoX_not_terminal` shows that is not `c`), while `const_limit_not_from_point_alone` refutes inhabitedness alone at the two-object discrete shape. So the in-tree `Connected` (`∀ x y, ZigZag x y`, with no inhabitedness clause) is genuinely the wrong reading here, and the statement says so rather than footnoting it. **THE ISSUE'S "Current state" IS FALSE ON TWO OF ITS THREE CLAIMS, both because of already-merged work**: it says no connectedness predicate exists (`ZigZag`/`Connected` are `Structure/Groupoid/Connected.v:122,:133`, pre-existing and reused, never redeclared here) and that there is no `Δ ⊣ Lim` adjunction for part (b) to describe (`Diagonal_Limit_Adjunction` and `Colimit_Diagonal_Adjunction` are `Adjunction/Diagonal/Limit.v`); its third claim, that `Diagonal_Unique` is a factorisation rather than a computation of `Lim (Δ c)`, is accurate. **THE CENTRAL ABSENCE IS REAL AND WAS MEASURED BY SHAPE**: the only near-hits are in `Structure/Limit/Weighted.v` and put the constant on the **weight** side (`conical_weighted`'s weight is `Δ[J](terminal_obj)` with `F` arbitrary) — a different statement. **THE ENGINE IS ONE REUSABLE LEMMA WITH NOTHING CATEGORICAL IN IT**: `leg_zigzag` closes a bare leg family under zig-zags, `zz_fwd` and `zz_bwd` consuming the SAME hypothesis at swapped arguments and opposite rewrite directions, so there is no asymmetry to manage; each side's step lemma is then one `id_left` through cone coherence, because `fmap[Δ[J](c)] f` IS `id[c]` by `eq_refl`. That identity is also why `const_acone` is a plain `Definition` with ZERO obligations. **THE COLIMIT HALF CANNOT BE GOT BY INSTANTIATING THE LIMIT HALF AT THE OPPOSITES, AND THAT IS MEASURED**: `Opposite_Functor Δ[J](c)` and `Δ[J^op](c)` are NOT convertible (pinned), so `ACone n G` does not transfer — yet BOTH DATA FIELDS agree at `eq_refl`, locating the difference in the three rebuilt law fields. It is built directly instead, and the cocone side needs NO `Connected (J^op)` (which the tree could not supply — there is no `zigzag_op`): `obj[J^op]` IS `obj[J]`, so `leg_zigzag` is instantiated at `J` with ambient `C^op`, the explicit `@` being required or inference picks `J^op` and demands opposite arrows. Strict readbacks: the apex, every cone leg, every limit leg and the mediator (which IS the competing cone's leg at the chosen point) all at `eq_refl`. Item 3 is delivered with a FINER decomposition than the issue anticipates — **`Diagonal_Faithful` needs only a POINT of `J`, not connectedness**, while `Diagonal_Full` needs the whole hypothesis, and each half has its own refutation (`Diagonal_Zero_not_Faithful`, `Diagonal_Two_Discrete_not_Full`). The reviewer check is met by `two_discrete_const_not_limit`, whose argument makes the pair of legs surjective onto `bool × bool` from the universal property alone and closes by pigeonhole — with `td_const_IsALimit_product` showing what the disconnected shape computes INSTEAD (`c × c`), so the refutation is quantitative rather than an existence failure. **89/89 constants closed under the global context** — 87 source-declared plus two `Program` obligations no source sweep sees — with **zero name collisions** across all 87. NOT delivered: the general (non-connected) case of part (b) via `ComponentDecomposition` — nothing composes with `Structure/Limit/Components.v` and no bridge exhibits the connected case as the one-component instance; and no absolute-limit THEORY beyond the two definitions `AbsoluteLimit`/`AbsoluteColimit` — which this file DOES introduce, the string having had zero prior occurrences — and their two witnesses: nothing relates them to `PreservesLimit`, `PreservesLimitCone`, `ContinuousFunctor` or `CreatesLimit`, and split coequalizers are not related to them. - **Structure/Limit/Components.v**: Mac Lane §IV.2 Exercise 7 (book p. 90) + Riehl §3.6 `riehl:3.6:construction-cat-coproducts` — **a limit over a coproduct of index categories is the product of the limits over the summands; a category EQUIPPED WITH A DECOMPOSITION AS DATA whose index satisfies `IdxUIP` is the coproduct of its connected components; and hence every limit over a shape carrying a decomposition is a product of limits over connected shapes**. All three clauses are delivered as theorems, with a fourth result factored out because nothing in it is about components: **`xfer_IsALimit`, a FULL and ESSENTIALLY SURJECTIVE functor of shapes transfers limits, with faithfulness NEVER CONSUMED** — which is exactly why **part (c) costs no `IdxUIP` at all** while the equivalence of part (b) does. **THE ISSUE'S PREMISE IS FALSE AND WAS FALSE BEFORE #352**: it asks for the zig-zag relation to be built from scratch, claiming no connectedness predicate exists anywhere in the tree, whereas `Structure/Groupoid/Connected.v:122,:133` has declared `Inductive ZigZag {C : Category}` and `Definition Connected (C : Category)` for an ARBITRARY category since a pre-existing commit; `Theory/Connected/Components.v` reuses them and so does this file, which redeclares neither (measured: zero `ZigZag`/`Connected` declarations here). **PART (b)'s OBSTRUCTION IS PROVED RATHER THAN ASSERTED, AND IT IS WHAT FORCES THE DESIGN**: π₀ is a COARSER SETOID ON THE SAME CARRIER (`carrier (pi0 C) = obj[C]` by `eq_refl`), so it supplies no quotient TYPE to index a coproduct by, and indexing `SigmaCat` by it yields ONE SUMMAND PER OBJECT — at the walking arrow, two summands where there is one component. `naive_pi0_sum_not_connected` proves that category disconnected while `Two_Connected` proves `_2` connected, and `no_ESO_into_naive_pi0_sum` turns it into the consumer-facing form: NO essentially surjective functor `_2 ⟶ naive_pi0_sum` exists. **Read that at its strength** — it refutes THAT index and is NOT a proof that the two categories are inequivalent. The decomposition is therefore DATA, on `Theory/Skeleton.v:355`'s `Skeleton` model: `ComponentDecomposition` carries `cd_index`, `cd_rep` (a chosen representative), `cd_part`, `cd_join` and the uniqueness field `cd_sep`, **consuming NO choice principle**, and no category is shown to HAVE a decomposition — the two concrete ones are built by hand. Riehl's uniqueness clause is `cd_transfer`/`cd_transfer_round`, sharpened by `cd_transfer_part` so that two decompositions cut the category into the same pieces rather than merely having equinumerous index sets. **A UNIVERSE TRAP DECIDED THE SHAPE OF PART (a), AND IT IS GUARDED RATHER THAN MEASURED**: `@iprod`'s binder reads `C : Category@{_ Set Set}` — hom AND proof pinned to the literal `Set`, since it is defined over `Limit (DiscreteCat_Functor f)` — while `@IsIndexedProduct` is `C : Category@{_ u u}`, identified but FREE (the #139 binder trap: `iprod`'s constraint block shows only a bound on a different level). The issue's own reviewer check asks that part (a)'s right-hand side use `iprod`; taken literally that would have confined the theorem to `Set`-homed categories WITHOUT SAYING SO, so part (a) is stated FIRST at `IsIndexedProduct` and only then read through `iprod` as `coprod_IsALimit_iprod`, with the pin pinned as a FORMABILITY negative (`Cannot enforce Set = uh`) against APPLIED controls at those very levels. The projections agree with restriction along the injections (`coprod_proj_is_restriction`), and the isomorphism packaging `coprod_limit_iso` carries its leg equations (`coprod_limit_iso_legs`) rather than being a bare `≅`. **`IdxUIP` is spent in exactly ONE place** — `cd_compare_Faithful`, inherited by `cd_equivalence` — while `cd_compare_Full` and `cd_compare_ESO` take no hypothesis; it is **NOT shown necessary** (no countermodel, no forcing theorem in the style of `sigma_inj_Full_forces_UIP`) and is discharged by Hedberg for decidable indices. **117/117 constants closed under the global context, of which the `make print-assumptions` gate carries 51 permanently** — 111 source-declared plus the five `ComponentDecomposition` field accessors and the constructor, none of which a source sweep sees — and **zero name collisions** across all 111. Non-vacuity is proved rather than asserted at three points: `_2` as a one-component shape, `TwoSum` as a genuinely two-component one with its summands proved separated (`TwoSum_not_connected`, `TwoSum_component_objects_differ`), and a `Coq` witness whose mediator COMPUTES (`CoqPair_med_computes`) with a two-element apex. NOT delivered: no dual (nothing is stated for colimits); no proof that a category HAS a decomposition; no relation between `ComponentDecomposition` and `pi0` as an isomorphism of index types; `IdxUIP`'s necessity; and no naturality of any identification in the diagram.
