But #336's remaining Riehl box — that the identity-matrix comparison **IS AN ISOMORPHISM** for finite index, with `⊕` the common value — is **#336's OWN work, BLOCKED on #536's `⊕` object** (only the object is transferred: "is #536's first Work item; consume it"), and its own text says "the n-ary case is genuine new work". **#336 states it "cannot close while [these boxes] are unproved", so this work ADVANCES #336 rather than closing it** — the other box of that pair, the arbitrary-index determination, IS delivered here with both halves; no invertibility criterion for `can_matrix` and hence no n-ary semiadditivity; no functoriality or naturality of `matrix_mor`; no transpose or duality. Riehl's abelian-category footnote is recorded as **structurally unprovable as stated in-tree** rather than pursued: `Structure/Abelian.v:137-138` carries `abelian_additive` and `Structure/Additive.v:37,40` carries `additive_biproducts` as DATA, so an in-tree abelian category HAS biproducts by hypothesis and nothing remains to derive
- **Instance/Parallel/Wide.v**, **Structure/Equalizer/Wide.v**, **Structure/Coequalizer/Wide.v**: Mac Lane §III.3's passing remark made precise — (co)equalizers of an ARBITRARY family of parallel maps. `WideParallel I` is the shape (two objects, one non-identity arrow `ParX ~> ParY` per `i : I`), `AWide fs` the diagram. **The obvious generalization is NOT the one taken, and the reason is a measurement**: `Instance/Parallel.v:30`'s `ParHom : bool → ParObj → ParObj → Set` invites reading its two arrows as already index-parameterized, but THREE of its four constructors (`ParIdX`, `ParIdY`, `ParOne`) sit at the tag `true` and only `ParTwo` at `false` — the `bool` is a DISCRIMINATOR on the hom-set `ParX ~> ParY` doubling as a dummy tag on the identities, not a free index, so substituting an arbitrary `I` leaves the three identity-side constructors with no distinguished element of `I` to sit at and does not typecheck. The wide shape is therefore a different ENCODING rather than a substitution instance, which is exactly why the comparison had to be PROVED. The encoding computes the hom family by matching on the two objects (`hom ParX ParY = I`, so an arrow IS an index by `eq_refl`, and `fmap[AWide fs] i = fs i` likewise); `WParVoid` is a universe-polymorphic empty type declared in-file so all four branches of the hom family sit at one universe by construction — the tree has no polymorphic empty type (measured: `Lib/Setoid.v:56` supplies the polymorphic `poly_unit`, and all ten `Empty_set` users including `Instance/Zero.v` take the pinned one) — but it is NOT forced, rebuilding the shape with the `Set`-pinned `Empty_set` giving a character-for-character identical constraint block, since `Set` sits below every `Type@{i}`. Named `WideParallel` because `Parallel` is taken by the shape it generalizes and this file Requires it; `ParObj`/`ParX`/`ParY` are IMPORTED, not redeclared, so "the same two objects" is definitional. `IsWideEqualizer`/`IsWideCoequalizer` are literal generalizations of the binary records — one equation-family field (`wfork_eq : ∀ i j, fs i ∘ e ≈ fs j ∘ e`) plus one descent field, no extra data — and BOTH have **EMPTY universe constraint blocks** over `C : Category@{u0 u1 u1}` with the index free. **THE MAIN FINDING: the elementary record and the (co)limit COME APART AT AN EMPTY INDEX.** A cone over `AWide fs` carries TWO legs; with `I` inhabited the second is determined by the first and the cone condition collapses to the fork equations, which is what makes the two presentations agree — but with `I` empty the fork condition is vacuous, so `id[x]` satisfies the record while a limit over the shape is a binary product. So all four round trips (`wide_equalizer_is_equalizer`/`is_wide_equalizer_limit` and the coequalizer duals) take an explicit `i0 : I` — but **necessity is proved for only two of the four**, and the scope is stated rather than blurred: what is refuted is the ELEMENTARY-to-(CO)LIMIT direction at a PINNED apex, so `is_wide_equalizer_limit` and `is_wide_coequalizer_colimit` have `i0` proved necessary while `wide_equalizer_is_equalizer` and `wide_coequalizer_is_coequalizer` merely ASSUME it, no unpointed version of those being refuted. The proof is in three steps: `wide_empty_id_IsWideEqualizer` (the identity satisfies the record over an empty index), `wide_unpointed_round_trip_gives_hom` (an unpointed round trip landing at that same apex, applied to it, manufactures an arrow `x ~> y` from nothing, by reading the limit leg at `ParY`), and `wide_round_trip_needs_point` (instantiate at `_2` with `x := TwoY`, `y := TwoX`, whose hom-set is empty — `False`). The binary donor never meets this because its `fork_legs h ParY := f ∘ h` silently uses the distinguished member. What needs NO inhabitant, holding at every index including the empty one: `wide_equalizer_monic`/`wide_coequalizer_epic`, `wide_equalizer_unique`/`wide_coequalizer_unique`, `wide_limit_equalizes`/`wide_colimit_coequalizes`. `HasWideEqualizers`/`HasWideCoequalizers` are declared but NOT inhabited for any category. Comparison with the binary shape, strongest form pinned: Leibniz equality of the two `Category` records and convertibility of the hom families are both REFUTED (probes, genuine `cannot unify`), while `WideParallel_bool_Parallel : @Isomorphism StrictCat (WideParallel bool) Parallel` HOLDS — both comparison functors identity-on-objects, both round trips at `Functor_StrictEq_Setoid` with `eq_refl` object components — with the `≅[Cat]` reading derived and labelled the weaker (equivalence) one; and since a shape isomorphism says nothing about diagrams, `AWide_bool_APair` separately proves the wide diagram at a two-element family IS `APair f g ◯ Par_of_Wide`, again at strict functor equality. Universes measured in the constraint blocks: `WideParallel@{i p}` carries three constraints where `DiscreteCat` — the tree's other `Type`-parameterized `eq`-hom shape, and the one `Theory/WeaklyInitial.v` already consumes — carries six; the round trips IDENTIFY index with hom universe (`u0 = u1`), and where that enters is MEASURED rather than attributed: with index and hom declared separately, forming the cone and reading `cone_leg`/`vertex_map` yield only the BOUND `u1 <= u0`, as do the files' own `wfork_cone`, `is_wide_equalizer_cone` and `is_wide_equalizer_cone_ump`, so it is NOT inherited from `Structure/Cone.v` — it first appears at `wide_limit_equalizer_desc` and at the `Limit`-record assembly, both local to these files (an earlier draft attributed it to the donor on the strength of an unannotated probe, which collapses the two universes by minimization; whether any rearrangement avoids it is UNTESTED). It costs a consumer nothing, an identification being satisfiable exactly where the bound would be. Engineering finding, the same FAMILY of hazard as `Build_Quiver_Standard_Eq` in Construction/Free/Quiver/Examples.v though neither the same cause nor the same repair (there a monomorphic `eq_equivalence@{u}` pins the PROOF universe and the fix is to spend the polymorphic one; here minimization pins the HOM universe and the fix is explicit binders): written without annotations, minimization pins the shape's hom universe at `Set` (because `bool : Set`) and `Functor_StrictEq_Setoid` then drags the TARGET's hom and proof universes down with it; explicit binders on `Wide_of_Par`/`Par_of_Wide` lift it. **Theory/WeaklyInitial/Wide.v** is the optional item done ADDITIVELY: `initial_from_weakly_initial_wide` runs Freyd's argument over `HasWideEqualizers`, equalizing ALL endomorphisms of the product at once — dropping the power entirely and needing no separate binary-equalizer supply, with `endo_absorb` becoming ONE instance of the fork condition at `u` and `id` where the original needed four rewrites through two tuplings. **`Theory/WeaklyInitial.v` is byte-identical** (`git diff --exit-code`): the existing theorem keeps its statement, proof and universe constraints, GAFT is NOT rerouted, and both theorems stand with neither derived from the other — chosen because rerouting would change hypotheses at GAFT's call sites and that file's header gives an explicit reason (universe over-commitment) for taking its limits as explicit hypotheses. Four negatives (3 conversion, 1 formability) against 10 controls (8 `Check`s and 2 control `Definition`s) in Test/ProbeWideParallel.v, each stripped and confirmed a genuine `cannot unify` or a universe inconsistency naming the declared levels, the formability pair guarded by a positive control at an index universe declared strictly BELOW the hom universe. 34/34 gated constants closed under the global context. NOT delivered: no leg-carrying elementary record that would round-trip unconditionally; no identification of `WideParallel` at an empty index with a discrete category, hence no "limit = binary product" theorem (the sharpness argument goes through an empty hom-set instead); no `WideParallel poly_unit ≅ _2`; no functoriality of `WideParallel` in `I`; no `HasWideEqualizers`/`HasWideCoequalizers` instance for any category, so the new Freyd theorem is a conditional exactly as its donor is; no comparison between the wide (co)equalizer RECORDS at `I := bool` and the binary ones (the shapes and the diagrams are compared, the records are not); no wide analogue of Structure/Limit/Unique.v's leg-carrying essential uniqueness; no preservation/creation; no split/reflexive wide coequalizers; no presheaf reading of the shape
- **Structure/AbCategory.v**: Mac Lane §I.8's Ab-categories, exactly — the intermediate neither neighbour supplied: `AbEnriched` = `Preadditive` (CMon-enrichment) + negation + the inverse law, with NO zero object or biproducts (the reviewer bar; `Additive` bundles both), negation's bilinearity DERIVED by uniqueness of additive inverses (`compose_abneg_left`/`_right`), and `Additive_AbEnriched` reading every additive category down. `AdditiveFunctor` has preservation of `padd` as its ONLY field — `fmap_pzero` ("whence T0 = 0", by the idempotency cancellation `padd_idem_zero`) and `fmap_abneg` are theorems — with identity and composition closure instances. `AbCat` is Mac Lane's category of Ab-categories: sigma objects, functors bundled with the additivity law, hom-equivalence `Functor_Setoid` (the Cat choice, documented). The standalone witness is Ab itself — `Ab_Preadditive`/`Ab_AbEnriched` by pointwise addition and negation of homomorphisms, closing the instance Instance/Ab.v's header records as available but not attempted. The QA increment corrects Construction/Enriched.v's "hand-built Ab-enriched" misdescription (it is CMon-enriched) and points the Preadditive/Additive headers at the new class. The tensor side (Mac Lane §I.8 def 5 + §VII.1): Instance/Ab/Tensor.v builds `AbTensor G H` as an inductive setoid quotient of formal sums by exactly the group laws and bilinearity (Coend.v shape, reflexivity derived), with the `Bilinear` interface, the definitionally-computing `tensor_ump`, the agreement-on-generators engine `tensor_hom_ext`, and `AbTensor_Functor : Ab ∏ Ab ⟶ Ab` (the issue's free-abelian-group machinery deliberately traded for the direct quotient — disclosed; the reusable surface is the Bilinear/ump/hom_ext interface); Instance/Ab/Monoidal.v assembles ⟨Ab, ⊗, ℤ⟩ — integer action `zsmul` by nat iteration (additivity via `Z.pos_sub_spec`), unit `ZAb := ring_ab Int_Ring`, full `Ab_Monoidal`/`Ab_Braided`/`Ab_Symmetric` with every coherence law a generator computation; Construction/Enriched/Ab.v proves Mac Lane's "described completely in these terms": `Enriched_Ab_iff_AbEnriched : @Enriched Ab Ab_Monoidal ↔ { C : Category & AbEnriched C }`, both directions constructive and identity on objects/hom-carriers, exercised at `Enriched_Ab_itself` (Ab enriched over itself). Universe enablement in Theory/Algebra/Rig.v: Type-ascribed `Z_eqT` and plain-definition `Int_Rig`/`Int_Ring` with explicit binders (minimization had pinned the setoid-proof levels to Set, which would have confined `Enriched Ab` to Set-sized homs); the monoidal instances carry matching annotations, `Ab_Monoidal@{s o} : Monoidal@{o s}` fully parametric
- **Structure/Kernel/Universal.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 remark 4 — the kernel as a UNIVERSAL, packaged so it survives where zero morphisms do not. The "kill-f" presheaf `KillPresheaf f : C^op ⟶ Sets`, `d ↦ { h : d ~> x & f ∘ h ≈ zmor }`, and `IsKernel f i` identified with `⟨k,i⟩` being a universal element of it, over #303's `AUniversalElement`. **Delivered at THREE strengths, deliberately kept apart because they are three different claims.** (1) Universe-free passages `kernel_aue`/`aue_kernel`, both `:=` with NO tactic — the two records' field types are convertible one for one, since `homset[C^op] k d` IS `homset[C] d k` on the nose and the presheaf's `≈` unfolds to a morphism equation — with the underlying morphism surviving both round trips by `eq_refl` and the mediator surviving one direction as the SAME TERM (`kernel_aue_mediator`). (2) Representability: `kernel_Representable` (`repr_obj = k` by `eq_refl`) and `kernel_representation : [Hom ─,k] ≅[[C^op,Sets]] KillPresheaf f` with component `u ↦ i ∘ u` by `eq_refl`, routed through Element.v's DIRECT (Yoneda-free) constructions, plus `kernel_iff_representable`. (3) The bundled `kernel_universal_element_iso`, an isomorphism of setoids in `Sets` — **and this one alone carries a universe restriction, measured and pinned**: `obj[Sets@{o so}]` is `SetoidObject@{o o}`, which IDENTIFIES a setoid's carrier and relation universes, so the bundled form is stated over `C : Category@{u u0 u0}` with `u <= u0` (objects at or below homs) while `Ab@{u u0}` is declared with `u0 < u` — it does NOT instantiate at `Ab`, and its equalizer twin does not reach `Rng`. Both refusals are `Fail`-pinned against a positive control at an abstract `C`; the universe-free forms and `kernel_representation` DO reach `Ab`, and that pair IS the control. This is a DIFFERENT wall from the Yoneda one Element.v records, and the Yoneda-free route does not rescue it; not claimed unavoidable (an annotated variant was attempted and abandoned as the wrong bet for a tree building on 8.19–9.1). **The fork packaging is definitionally TIGHTER than the kill packaging** — the opposite of what the naming suggests: `aue_equalizer (equalizer_aue E) = E` closes by `eq_refl` on the WHOLE RECORD (`IsEqualizer` has primitive projections with eta; `eq_desc` closes by function eta) and the fork-side mediator round trip is Leibniz, whereas the kill-side composites are blocked by the `Qed`-opaque `kill_fork`/`fork_kill` reshaping (`f ∘ h ≈ 0` versus `f ∘ h ≈ 0 ∘ h`); the other fork round trip fails ONLY for want of `sigT` eta, which `aue_equalizer_round_universal` states positively. `IsKernel f i = IsKernelOf f i` and `zmor (ZeroMorphisms_of_ZeroObject Z) = zero_mor` both by `eq_refl`, so everything applies to Structure/Kernel.v's kernels with no adapter. Deliverable 2 at two grades: `ZeroMorphisms` (a chosen family plus two absorption laws — weaker than `ZeroObject`, with `ZeroMorphisms_of_ZeroObject` giving the one direction that IS witnessed; the converse non-implication is true but has NO in-tree witness, and Rng's having neither is a different, weaker statement — disclosed rather than asserted) and, below it, `ForkPresheaf f g` for an ARBITRARY parallel pair with `equalizer_universal_element_iso`, needing nothing at all; `kill_fork_iso : KillPresheaf f ≅ ForkPresheaf f zmor` says exactly how much the zero morphism buys. **Instance/Ab.v's kernel gains its universal property here** (`ab_kernel_IsKernel` — **the tree's FIRST concrete `IsKernel` inhabitant**, and the claim is sourced rather than surveyed: `AbKernel`/`Grp_kernel`/`RModKernel` existed as objects with inclusions but no universal property of any of them was proved, and every other `IsKernel`-typed term in the tree — `Structure/Abelian.v`'s fields, accessors and hypothesis binders, plus two lemma conclusions in `Structure/Kernel.v` — is conditional on `Abelian` or `HasKernels`, NEITHER of which has an instance — `Instance/Ab.v:17` says it "instantiates NONE of `Preadditive`, `Additive` or `Abelian`" and docs/INHABITATION.md records the same for `image_mediator_epic`. So the issue's "no algebraic category exists to instantiate it" was wrong in the letter and right in the spirit: the categories were there, the universal property was not. A new *Witnessed results* row in docs/INHABITATION.md records the witness and its scope). Witness: `ab_parity : ℤ ⟶ Z/2` whose kernel is the even integers, PROPER and nontrivial (`ab_parity_kernel_has_two`, `ab_parity_kernel_proper`), with `ab_parity_med_computes` evaluating the unique factorization of doubling at 3 to 6 by `eq_refl`. The `Rng` half is a necessity argument, not a generalization for its own sake: `Rng_no_hom_zero_to_Z` shows the hom-set from the zero ring to ℤ is EMPTY (1 ≈ 0 there would force 1 = 0 in ℤ), hence `Rng_no_zero_morphisms` and `Rng_no_zero_object`, and `Rng_fork_presheaf`/`Rng_equalizer_aue` show what survives. Deferred: no cokernel dual (inherits Element.v's own missing couniversal-element class); no uniqueness-up-to-unique-iso in `C` (instantiating `universal_element_unique` lands in `C^op` and the transport is not performed); no `HasKernels` instance; no Grp/RMod instantiation; `ZeroMorphisms` lives in this file rather than its own, disclosed as a move awaiting a second consumer
- **Structure/Abelian.v**: The additive-structure spine — zero objects with the tunnelled `zero_mor` (Structure/ZeroObject.v), UMP-form biproducts (Structure/Biproduct.v), commutative-monoid hom-enrichment with opt-in `addition_scope` (Structure/Preadditive.v), the two semiadditivity theorems (Structure/Semiadditive.v: biproducts compute the enrichment; a bicartesian category with invertible coproduct-to-product comparison is preadditive, by Eckmann–Hilton), additive categories with negation (Structure/Additive.v), the elementary fork API `IsEqualizer` (Structure/Equalizer/Fork.v), kernels/cokernels with normality and the cokernel⇒regular-epi bridge (Structure/Kernel.v), and abelian categories with the full epi-mono factorization (`image_mediator_epic` by the Freyd/Borceux chase) assembled as `Abelian_OFS`; the concrete semiadditive witness is CMon (Instance/CMon.v, Instance/CMon/Biproduct.v — commutative monoids over setoids, universe-polymorphic)
- **Structure/Biproduct/Cartesian.v**, **Instance/{CMon,Ab,Mod}/Coproduct.v**, **Instance/Top/{Coproduct,Wedge}.v**: Mac Lane §III.3's roster of concrete coproducts (+ Awodey §3.2 Examples 3.6/3.10, Riehl §3.1 Ex 3.1.xi) — direct sums, disjoint unions and wedge sums, over a general engine the tree did NOT have. **The engine is the missing direction of an existing pair**: `Structure/Semiadditive.v:228,:244` supplies Cartesian+Preadditive ⟹ Biproducts, and nothing supplied the converse, so `biproduct_Cartesian`/`biproduct_Cocartesian` do — a category with a zero object and binary biproducts is both cartesian and cocartesian. Its headline is a CONVERSION, not a constructed comparison: `biproduct_prod_is_coprod` records `x × y = x + y = biproduct_obj (biproduct x y)` by **`eq_refl`**, which is the sentence "in a category with biproducts the binary product and the binary coproduct are the same object" machine-checked rather than asserted, with both mediators and all four structural morphisms likewise `eq_refl`. The round trip against Semiadditive is graded rather than claimed uniform: object and **both projections** return by `eq_refl`, the **injections only up to `≈`** (rebuilt as `id △ 0`), with the two strict failures pinned as conversion negatives. Neither is registered as an `Instance` — doing so would close a resolution cycle with `cartesian_has_biproducts`, and the header says so. **THREE roster entries ride it**: `CMon_Cocartesian` (80 lines, ZERO new proof — pure bridge application, and the cleanest demonstration that the engine is general), `Ab_Cocartesian` and `RMod_Cocartesian`. **Two more are built DIRECTLY and provably cannot ride it** — the engine takes a `ZeroObject` and `Instance/Top.v:505`'s `Top_no_zero_object` refutes one for `Top` — namely `Top_Cocartesian` (disjoint union) and `Top_pointed_Cocartesian` (wedge sum), each assembled as a `Build_Cartesian` on the opposite category with no `Biproduct` module required at all. **The transfer from CMon to Ab is far cheaper than a reader would guess, and the file corrects its own first draft on the point**: `AbHom A B := CMonHom A B` is a bare `Definition` (`Instance/Ab.v:184`), so Ab's arrows ARE CMon's, and `@zero_mor Ab Ab_Zero M N` is `@zero_mor CMon CMon_Zero M N` by `eq_refl` (`ab_zero_mor_is_cmon_zero_mor`, the reason being that `Ab_Zero`'s `one`/`zero` fields are literally `CMon_one`/`CMon_zero_hom`) — so **ten of the biproduct record's eleven fields transfer by `:=` with no tactic**, only `biproduct_obj` being new. An earlier draft claimed those laws "do not transfer" and were "not even well-typed here"; that was false and is named as false in-header. At R-Mod the transfer is graded in three: **data** must be rebuilt (record wrapper plus one `rm_map_smul` obligation each), **laws** transfer unchanged, and **`∃!`-packages** must be repackaged though not re-argued — that last being a genuine formability failure, pinned. Awodey's `O(X+Y) ≅ O(X) × O(Y)` is `eq_refl` (it IS the definition of `sum_open`), and "the disjoint union of discrete spaces is discrete" is proved as a biconditional of the two openness predicates rather than as an isomorphism. The two topological entries are proved DISTINCT objects rather than assumed: `wedge_basepoints_identified` against `wedge_is_not_sum`, the same two points being provably distinct in `Sum_Top`. Non-vacuity throughout — ℤ⊕ℤ computes by `eq_refl` (merge, projections, scalar action), injections provably differ, wedge non-basepoints provably not glued. Universes measured in the constraint blocks with levels declared APART (not by minimization): `Cartesian` IDENTIFIES hom and proof and `biproduct_Cartesian` inherits that from `Cartesian`'s signature — but the CAUSE is minimization, not `Terminal`, and an audit refuted the causal reading: a one-field class `{ t1 : obj }` over an unannotated `Context {C : Category}` with no `Terminal` or `Cartesian` in scope acquires the same `Category@{u u0 u0}`, and the field shape re-elaborates at `Category@{uo uh up}` when the levels are declared apart, so the content never required it; the `Terminal` negative records shared shape, not causation; `Sum_Top`/`WedgeSum` carry `u = u0`, a genuine identification forced by the glued carrier being one `SetoidObject@{o o}`. **A donor `Set` pin is LOCATED rather than guessed and deliberately NOT repaired**: `Instance/Ab.v:227`'s `Ab_trivial` is declared with zero universe binders at `AbObject@{Set Set Set}` while `Instance/CMon/Biproduct.v:72`'s `CMon_trivial@{o}` is polymorphic, so everything through `Ab_Zero` inherits it and `RMod_trivial` propagates it to R-Mod — the evidence being that `CMon_Cocartesian` is `Set`-free while `Ab_Cocartesian` is not, though the direct-sum OBJECTS on both sides are free; same family as the `Grp_trivial` pin recorded above for Instance/Grp/Quotient/Colimit.v. **SCOPE IS PRINCIPLED, NOT ARBITRARY**: delivered are exactly the coproducts computed from the underlying data of the summands; deferred are the two needing a NEW CARRIER by generators and relations — the Grp free product and the CRng tensor — which is the asymmetry `Structure/Cocartesian.v`'s own header quotes from Mac Lane 1950 ("the proof that the direct product exists is not dual to the proof that the free product exists"). Nine negatives against twenty-eight positive commands, each stripped once and its failure KIND confirmed, with the pairing audited so every negative's constants appear in a succeeding control; among them a compiled obstruction showing that ONE natural proof-relevant encoding of a product topology does not fit at `Type@{o}` — which is weaker than "no `Cartesian Top`", since the impredicative-truncation idiom of `Instance/Sets/Powerset.v` elaborates a squashed variant and is not investigated. A false pass was caught in the probe's own construction and recorded: a wedge negative first written against the non-existent name `WedgeSum_Top` passed for "reference not found", found only by stripping the `Fail` and reading the error. Two corrections to the surrounding prior-art picture, both compile-verified: **`Rel_Cocartesian` is NOT live** — `Instance/Rel.v:130` sits inside a comment spanning :99–:160 that also swallows `Rel_Cartesian` — and `Instance/Proset/Limit.v`'s `Proset_Cocartesian` IS live (a `Program Definition` taking join data, not a registered instance). 35/35 gated constants closed under the global context. NOT delivered, scoped to this slice: the Grp free product and the CRng tensor as above; Riehl §4.6.6's `F(X ⊔ Y) ≅ F X ∗ F Y`, which needs both of those AND a `Cocartesian`-level left-adjoint-preservation bridge the tree lacks (only general LAPC at `Colimit` level exists, and the dual of `Structure/Limit/Cartesian.v`'s product half is missing, which `Instance/Two/Discrete.v:22-24` asserts in prose); no indexed or infinite coproducts (all binary, matching `Cocartesian`); no `Cartesian Top` or `Cartesian Top_pointed`; no initial object of `Top_pointed`, so the wedge's unit laws are unavailable though `coprod_comm`/`coprod_assoc` are exhibited; no `Additive`/`Abelian` for Ab or RMod; no smash product or descent of the wedge to `Toph_pointed`; and Awodey's poset clauses of Example 3.6 (juxtaposition, rooted posets) are untouched, only the pointed-SPACE reading being delivered
- **Structure/Pushout/Split.v**, **Instance/Grp/Pushout.v**, **Instance/Top/Pushout.v**: Mac Lane §III.3 p. 66 — pushouts in `Grp` and `Top`, and the amalgamated product. **Both substrates delivered**, `Grp_HasPushouts` and `Top_HasPushouts`, neither dominating the other (≈739 and ≈577 lines, cores ~250 each). On the `Grp` side the PRESENTATION route costs almost nothing — every group law is a CONSTRUCTOR of the congruence, so `AmalgamGrp` is a record literal with ZERO proof obligations and `am_eval` is a `Fixpoint`, making the mediator's homomorphism laws close by `reflexivity`; the UMP's price is two inductions (twelve cases over the congruence, five over the word), with two more outside it — `am_eq_refl` and `Grp_Cocartesian`'s `fork_respects` — so four in the file. **It subsumes the free product**: `Grp_free_product` is an INSTANCE of the amalgamated product and `Grp_Cocartesian : @Cocartesian Grp` is built and registered, which discharges part of what #1194 owns — and both injections are **split monic with no hypothesis at all** — but read that precisely: what is discharged is the strictly weaker FACTORIZATION hypothesis (`Grp_fp_inl_Section` routes through `am_inj1_Section_of_factor`, not `..._of_Retraction`), and Mac Lane's own monic-legs hypothesis is FALSE for that span, its legs being CONSTANT maps and so not monic into a nontrivial group; this is his CONCLUSION by a different, weaker route, not an instance of his refinement. **The universe route matters and the first attempt was refuted by its own measurement**: building the free product through `Grp_Zero` (the obvious route, and the one I suggested) yields `GrpObject@{Set Set Set}` — `Instance/Grp.v`'s `Grp_zero_hom` is itself declared there, the donor defect `Instance/Grp/Quotient/Colimit.v` already records — so a `Cocartesian Grp` on that route would cover **Set-sized groups only**, materially weakening the subsumption. Routing instead through the amalgam along CONSTANT legs needs no zero object (the cocone condition is automatic, every hom preserving the unit) and gives `Grp_free_product@{u u0}` and `Grp_Cocartesian@{u u0}` with **no `Set` anywhere**; `Grp_zero_hom_Section` is kept as the honest record of the route not taken, with the pin pinned as a formability negative. **THE MONIC-LEGS REFINEMENT IS AT SPLIT STRENGTH ONLY, AND THE GAP IS STATED RATHER THAN BLURRED**: `pushout_both_Monic`/`Grp_split_legs_monic_injections` prove that split legs give monic injections, by feeding the cocone `(id, f ∘ retraction)` to the UMP — a generic argument, which is why it lives in `Structure/Pushout/Split.v` and applies to `Top` verbatim. For merely MONIC legs this is the Schreier normal-form theorem, whose usual (van der Waerden) proof builds an action on alternating normal forms and must CHOOSE coset transversals; that is not attempted, the split hypothesis is NOT claimed necessary, and no in-tree monic-but-not-split group hom is exhibited, so the strictness of the gap is ARGUED, not proved. A second honesty point sits beside it: **"the vertex is the amalgamated product" is DEFINITIONAL here, not a theorem** — the vertex IS the presented group and nothing relates it to an independent definition. On the `Top` side the pushout is the quotient topology on the disjoint union, glued by an inductive closure in the SHAPE of `Instance/Sets/Pushout.v`'s `pushout_eq` — same five constructors, RESTATED for spaces, that file not being `Require`d, so a structural copy rather than reuse; `adjunction_glues` is the motivating adjunction-space case. **A prior-art correction that inverted the expected cost**: the tree DOES carry quotient-topology donors — `Instance/Top.v:635`'s `CP_open` (the cokernel-pair space) and `Instance/Top/Wedge.v:141`'s section literally headed "The quotient topology" with `wedge_open` — so the `Top` half is those two with the relation generalized from a closed-form `match` to the inductive closure, and was CHEAP rather than expensive. (An earlier measurement of mine reported no such donor; that was produced by truncating an absence search with `head -5` when it had thirteen hits, and the donors were below the cut. The constants are named `CP_open`/`wedge_open`, not `*quotient*`.) **#862's boundary is respected, not claimed**: the general "pushout over the initial object IS the binary coproduct" is NOT built; both files carry a cross-reference paragraph naming #862 as owning it and stating that what is built is the ad-hoc instance with the commutation hypothesis discharged once, leaving literally the coproduct UMP — `Top_pushout_empty_iso` is the same move on the topological side, and is an `≅[Top]` rather than an identity, two conversion negatives showing the glued setoid and the glued topology both differ from `Sum_Top`'s. Strengths graded: apex, both injections, carrier and all three group operations at **Leibniz `=`** on the `Grp` side, apex and both injections likewise on the `Top` side, with `pushout_med` vs the named mediator only `≈` — diagnosed to DONOR OPACITY, `Structure/Pushout.v` stating `pushout_ump` with `Qed`, and pinned identically on BOTH substrates so the cause is visibly the donor rather than either instance. Universes measured with causes probed rather than attributed: `AmalgamGrp`'s binder displays three free object triples while its constraint block collapses all nine, but the cause is NOT the amalgamation — the bare `fun (A B : GrpObject) (f : @hom Grp A B) => B`, which mentions no pushout, already elaborates at `GrpObject@{u0 u0 u0}`, so merely NAMING a `Grp` hom is what identifies them; `Pushout_Top`'s `u = u0`/`u = u1` is an identification with the same probed cause, and no `Top` constraint block contains `Set`. Non-vacuity on both substrates and in both directions: `fp_generators_distinct`/`fp_inl_injective` by mapping OUT into `Z2` (no induction on a generated congruence can yield a negative) against `amalgam_over_Z2_merges`, which merges the very pair the free product keeps apart; and `empty_span_keeps_summands_apart` — via a `tp_side` invariant, since `tpr_trans` passes through arbitrary intermediates and plain induction will not do it — against `point_span_merges`. Six negatives against eleven controls (eighteen positive commands), **with the pairing verified by RENAME SIMULATION — 25 constants by the author, and an audit independently renamed 31 with all 31 breaking at a control and none going vacuous**; that exercise FOUND three genuine pairing gaps (`pushout_med`, `Top_po_med`, and `IsOpen`/`Sum_Top` with `Grp_free_product`/`Grp_fp_inl`) where a rename would have been swallowed, and the controls closing them exist for that reason. **16/16 gated constants closed under the global context — read that precisely: 144 constants were verified once by `Print Assumptions`, and the SIXTEEN principal artifacts are what the `make print-assumptions` gate carries permanently.** `Theory/Morphisms/CokernelPair.v:268`'s count of `HasPushouts` instances is corrected in the same commit from three to five, naming the two new ones and recording that the count read three when that note was written. NOT delivered, scoped: on the `Grp` side the Schreier/normal-form refinement for merely monic legs, any normal form or decision procedure for `am_eq`, HNN extensions, Bass–Serre theory, functoriality of the pushout in the span, and any comparison with `Instance/Grp/Free.v`'s free group; on the `Top` side any separation or compactness of the glued space (nothing about when an adjunction space is Hausdorff), CW/cell attachment, mapping cylinders or cones, a free-standing general quotient-topology construction, indexed pushouts, `Cocomplete Top`, a pointed variant, and — though `Structure/Pushout/Split.v` applies verbatim — no `Top` span whose legs split is exhibited, so there is no `Top` monic-legs instance; and across both, no van Kampen theorem, hence no bridge between the two new files and no connection to `Instance/Top/FundamentalGroupoid.v`
- **Structure/Factorization.v**: Orthogonal factorization systems — `MorphismClass` vocabulary (Theory/Morphisms/Classes.v), orthogonal lifting `e ⫫ m` with left-class closure (Theory/Orthogonality.v), `Factorization`/`OFS` with uniqueness up to unique iso and the Fact.v comparison, strong epis (Factorization/StrongEpi.v: composition, cancellation, split ⇒ strong, strong+mono ⇒ iso); pullback pasting/stability toolkit in Theory/Morphisms/Stability.v (apex-pinned `IsPullback`, pasting both ways, monos/isos pullback-stable, `pullback_transport`); elementary cofork API `IsCoequalizer` with Colimit conversions in Structure/Coequalizer.v; regular categories in Structure/Regular.v (kernel pairs, `RegularEpi`, stability field) with the (RegularEpi, Mono) image factorization `Regular_OFS` in Regular/Factorization.v (double-cover monicity argument); concrete images in Instance/Sets/Image.v
- **Structure/Dagger.v**: Dagger categories — the light `DaggerCategory` class (involution + `dagger_id` + the `dagger_compose` anti-homomorphism law, with `†` notation), the common vocabulary the ZX and compact-closed prose had been citing without a definition; deliberately minimal, no monoidal compatibility demanded. First instance: Instance/Rel/Dagger.v — Mac Lane §I.7's converse relation and Awodey §1.9 Exercise 2 resolved: `Rel_Dagger`; the converse as an identity-on-objects functor with the self-duality at BOTH strengths — `Rel_self_dual_strict : Rel ≅[StrictCat] Rel^op` (the genuine isomorphism of categories Awodey asks for, free by strict functor equality) and the `Rel_self_dual : Rel ≅[Cat] Rel^op` equivalence reading, the Groupoid/Inversion.v two-tier presentation (Awodey (a), affirmative); `Relation_Functor_Faithful` (functions with pointwise-equal graphs are equal — Coq genuinely a WIDE subcategory of Rel, closing Rel.v's comment-only claim); `Rel_Terminal`/`Rel_Zero` completing the zero-object story Rel.v recorded as missing; and Awodey (c) `Powerset_self_dual : Powerset X ≅[Cat] (Powerset X)^op` by complementation over DECIDABLE (bool-valued) subsets — the constructive transposition, disclosed: the Prop-valued complement is an involution only up to double negation (Top/Closed.v's `pred_comparison_forces_DNE` precedent), while the bool round trips close by case analysis, axiom-free. Awodey (b) — Sets is NOT self-dual — is PROVED (`Sets_not_self_dual`): a Cat-isomorphism repackages as an equivalence (`Cat_Iso_to_Equivalence`), hence full+faithful; the empty Sets(1, ∅) read through fullness empties carrier (F 1), and faithfulness then collapses Sets(1, 2)'s two points into vacuously equivalent parallel pairs — true = false. All three parts of the exercise answered: (a) yes, (b) no, (c) yes
- **Structure/Coend.v**: The coend calculus — covariant coend accessors (`coend_obj`, `coend_inj`, the cowedge condition, `coend_ump`/`coend_med`/`coend_med_eq`, and the smart constructor `Build_Coend`) over the definitional `Coend F := @End (C^op)(D^op)(F^op)` (`Coend` itself, like `End`, is declared in Structure/End.v); the end and coend in `Sets` funext-free (Instance/Sets/End.v as the subsetoid of compatible families, Instance/Sets/Coend.v as the inductive setoid quotient by exactly the dinaturality relation); the ninja (co-)Yoneda reductions and the Fubini interchange (Theory/Coend/{Yoneda,Fubini}.v); profunctors `C ⇸ D := C^op ∏ D ⟶ Sets` with representables, composition by the coend `∫^d P(c,d) × Q(d,e)`, the unit/associator laws, and `representable_adjunction : (F ⊣ U) ↔ (Repr_left F ≅ Repr_right U)` (Theory/Profunctor.v, Construction/Profunctor/{Compose,Laws}.v, Theory/Profunctor/Adjunction.v); Day convolution `∫^{a,b} C(a ⨂ b, -) × F a × G b` with unitors and associator (Construction/Day.v)
- **Structure/Groupoid.v**: Groupoids as a *property* — `IsGroupoid C := ∀ x y (f : x ~> y), IsIsomorphism f` (Mac Lane §I.5 Definition 9; Awodey §7.7; Riehl Definition 1.1.12), the predicate the tree previously lacked: `Construction/Groupoid.v`'s `Groupoid C` is the *core construction*, and `core_is_groupoid` is the first proof that it satisfies a groupoid property. A plain `Definition`, not a `Class`, and never registered for resolution (the Theory/Equivalence.v rule — it carries a *chosen* inverse), with the `ginv` calculus (`ginv_unique_r`/`ginv_unique_l` from `comp_inverse_unique`, `ginv_respects` derived rather than assumed, involution/identity/composite, and `ginv_choice_irrelevant`: any two `IsGroupoid` structures agree up to `≈`). Groups ARE one-object groupoids, as a biconditional: `deloop_groupoid_iff : MonInverses M ↔ IsGroupoid (Deloop M)` (Fong–Spivak §3.2.5 Exercise 3.32), with both witnesses PROVED — `deloop_nat_not_groupoid` exhibits the arrow `1` of `B (ℕ,+)` as non-invertible, `deloop_bool_groupoid` is `B (Z/2)` — and `vertex_group G x : GrpObject` for the converse, round-tripping with `Deloop` on the nose in both data fields. `conjugation_iso G f : MonIso (vertex_group G x) (vertex_group G x')` is Mac Lane's conjugation, over locally defined `MonHom`/`MonIso` (a group homomorphism needs no separate class: `MonHom_grp_inv` derives preservation of inverses). **Structure/Groupoid/Connected.v** adds `ZigZag`/`Connected` in Riehl's §1.5 finite-zig-zag form — with `Fence` (strictly alternating spans) interderivable with it, and `zigzag_hom`/`hom_zigzag` collapsing it to the one-arrow form for groupoids — and the structure theorem `connected_deloop_equiv` (Riehl Proposition 1.5.13): a connected groupoid is equivalent to `B (hom(x,x))` at any object x, through `vertex_incl` (fully faithful with no hypothesis, essentially surjective by connectedness). The hypothesis is proven NECESSARY: `connected_iff_deloop_equiv` is a biconditional, and `Two_Discrete_no_deloop_equivalence` refutes the conclusion for the disconnected 2-object discrete groupoid. Non-vacuity: `WideDeloop M A` (the delooping widened over an object type A) gives the 2-object connected groupoid `Bool_Wide` with vertex group Z/2 at which the theorem is instantiated; `Roof_Connected` + `Roof_not_arrow_connected` prove the zig-zag form STRICTLY weaker than the one-arrow form outside groupoids. **Structure/Groupoid/Inversion.v** is Riehl Example 1.3.14: the identity-on-objects inversion functor and `Inversion_iso : C ≅[StrictCat] C^op` — stated in `StrictCat` because `≅[Cat]` in this library IS equivalence, with `Inversion_Cat_iso` the weaker derived form; the Z/2 instance is flagged degenerate (every element is its own inverse) and `Z3_Grp` is added so `Z3_inversion_not_identity` exhibits the functor actually moving an arrow. **Structure/Groupoid/Basepoint.v** turns the structure theorem into a group isomorphism: `Full_Compose`/`Faithful_Compose`, then `deloop_ff_moniso` — a fully faithful functor between one-object categories IS a monoid isomorphism, the content being that `Full`'s `prefmap` is only a section with no functoriality demanded, so that it is a homomorphism must be proved, and faithfulness is what proves it — and `deloop_equiv_moniso`, `connected_vertex_moniso` (the vertex groups at any two objects of a connected groupoid are isomorphic), instantiated at `Bool_Wide_vertex_moniso`. That instantiation is honest about its own weakness: `WideDeloop` fixes the hom type uniformly (`hom := fun _ _ => carrier M`), so the vertex groups at the two DISTINCT objects are the SAME monoid — `Bool_Wide_vertex_groups_agree` records it by `eq_refl` and `Bool_Wide_vertex_moniso_trivial` inhabits the same type with the identity maps alone — so the witness exercises the two HYPOTHESES and the derivation, not the conclusion; no in-tree object does better (docs/INHABITATION.md carries the note). The longer route is taken deliberately over `conjugation_iso`'s short one; the two words are kept in their exact senses (the structure theorem gives an equivalence of categories, what comes out is a bijection of underlying setoids preserving unit and multiplication). Zero axioms: all 27 constants report "Closed under the global context", and the file is wired into the `print-assumptions` make target alongside its `Structure/Groupoid` siblings

### Constructions (External Combinators)
- **Construction/FreeMonoidal.v**: The free monoidal category on one generator — Mac Lane §VII.2 in full (`maclane:VII.2:def1/construction1/thm1`). Binary words `Word` (exported standalone for the §VII.2 coherence corollary and §XI word-functors), the category `W` with hom the length equation and a constantly-true hom-setoid (Instance/Proset.v's thin-category device), so `W_thin` discharges every categorical, bifunctorial and monoidal law uniformly — the file contains no coherence argument, and `wlen`'s structural `+` makes the left unitor literally `eq_refl`. FreeMonoidal/Normal.v is the engine room via the strictification/NbE route (Beylin–Dybjer, Schauenburg; disclosed — the statement is Mac Lane's, the route is not his rank induction): the tail-parameterised `nf`/`J`/`subst`/`can` all typecheck by conversion with no transport in any definition, every cast has the single shape `id_cast (f_equal (nf -) e)` over a nat equality (so cast-UIP is Hedberg, axiom-free, and no `ObjDecEq` on the target is needed), and the two theorems where the target's coherence is spent are `J_assoc` (the pentagon) and `J_right_unit` (triangle + Kelly). FreeMonoidal/Universal.v proves Theorem 1: `Subst` with `Subst_tensor` (Mac Lane's `T(f□g) = Tf□'Tg`), the three strictness equations, `Subst_Monoidal` (strong) and `Subst_Strict` — both object equations `eq_refl` ON THE NOSE, witnessed by Examples — and the uniqueness triad `JW_unique`/`canW_unique`/`Subst_unique` with the package `FreeMonoidal_universal`: object agreement `Theta` is DERIVED from strictness plus the single datum `G WI = b` (where the PROP precedent takes the whole family as a hypothesis), and arrow agreement holds for every arrow by thinness decomposition through the normalisers. The morphism notion is STRICT because that is Mac Lane's: his Moncat has strict morphisms as arrows (`maclane:VII.1:construction2`), and uniqueness among strong monoidal functors is false; uniqueness up to monoidal natural isomorphism needs a class the tree lacks (ledger 19)
- **Construction/Deloop.v**: Delooping — Mac Lane §I.2 constructions 3 and 4, the monoid/one-object-category dictionary. `MonObject` is a bare setoid monoid (carrier + unit + op + `Proper` + the three laws), deliberately NOT `Theory/Algebra/Monoid.v`'s internal monoid object, since the construction needs an *element*-level multiplication and nothing about an ambient monoidal category; it is `Instance/CMon.v`'s `CMonObject` minus commutativity, so it carries both unit laws as fields rather than deriving one. `Deloop M : Category` has `obj := poly_unit` (universe-polymorphic, so the object universe stays free rather than pinned at `Set`) and `hom _ _ := carrier M`, composition the multiplication. The converse is `hom_monoid C a : MonObject`, the endomorphism monoid of any object, and the two compose definitionally: `hom_monoid_Deloop : hom_monoid (Deloop M) ttt = M` holds by `eq_refl`. (The opposite composite has no equational form — `Deloop (hom_monoid C a)` is one-object while `C` need not be — and the honest statement, that it is the full subcategory on `{a}`, is functor-level and deferred with the §I.3 dictionary.) `GrpObject` extends `MonObject` with a two-sided inverse, and `Deloop_group_invertible` makes every arrow of `Deloop G` invertible, with `to` the arrow and `from` the group inverse — a one-object groupoid. (The companion `Deloop_group_iso` has a vacuous *type*, since `obj[Deloop G]` is a singleton; the content is in `Deloop_group_invertible`.) `Construction/Deloop/Opposite.v` adds the opposite monoid and group and Riehl Example 1.2.2(iii), `Deloop_op : (Deloop M)^op ≅ Deloop (MonObject_op M)`. Witnesses `Nat_Plus`, `Bool_Xor`, `Bool_Xor_Grp` compute. Distinct from `Theory/Bicategory/OneObject.v`, which deloops a *monoidal category* into a one-object *bicategory*, one level up
- **Construction/Product/Special.v**: Mac Lane §II.3 Ex. 1 — `∏` on Cat restricts to the classical products along the standard full embeddings (a prose note; no Mon ⟶ Cat embedding functor exists in-tree): `MonObject_prod`/`GrpObject_prod` over Construction/Deloop.v's records (the group's monoid part definitional by `eq_refl`; distinct from Instance/Grp.v's identically-named record and its `Grp_product`, which live a layer down), `Deloop_prod_iso : Deloop (MonObject_prod M N) ≅[Cat] Deloop M ∏ Deloop N` with both functors identity on arrows and unit-conjugation object bookkeeping, `Deloop_grp_prod` the same term read at product groups, and `Discrete_Product : DiscreteCat (A * B) ≅[Cat] DiscreteCat A ∏ DiscreteCat B` — UIP-free, over a transparent `pair_eta`/`pair_equal` kit (the stdlib's `surjective_pairing`/`f_equal2` are opaque and would leave the arrow actions stuck); computing witnesses at `Nat_Plus`/`Bool_Xor` and a concrete discrete pair; Cat (equivalence strength) chosen over the issue's StrictCat alternative, disclosed
- **Construction/Product/Indexed.v**: Mac Lane §II.3 Ex. 3 — set-indexed products of categories: `PiCat (C : I → Category)` with objects the choice functions `∀ i, C i` and componentwise homs/setoid/laws, projections `PiCat_Proj`, pairing `PiCat_Pair` with the triangle `PiCat_Pair_Proj` (components `iso_id` — the composite is definitionally `R i`) uniqueness `PiCat_ump_unique` up to Cat's hom-equivalence (the setoid rendering of the book's on-the-nose uniqueness), and the bundled `PiCat_ump : ∃! F, ∀ i, P i ◯ F ≈ R i` matching `iprod_ump`'s packaging. One `Category` universe instance for the whole family (the function type forces it), distinct from Structure/Limit/Product.v's `iprod` (products of objects INSIDE one category); the binary case's recovery of `Cat_Cartesian`'s fork is a disclosed prose note
- **Construction/Deloop/Transform.v**: Mac Lane §I.4 Exercise 3 — natural transformations between group homomorphisms are conjugations. The functor-level half of the delooping dictionary (`Deloop_map`/`Deloop_unmap`, with `Deloop_unmap (Deloop_map f) = f` by `eq_refl`); the characterization in BOTH directions, over monoids as the intertwiner equation `T g · h ≈ h · S g` (`transform_iff_intertwines`, whose forward direction is literally the `naturality` field) and over groups as `transform_iff_conjugate : (Deloop_map S ⟹ Deloop_map T) ↔ ∃ h, T g ≈ h · S g · h⁻¹`; the setoid-level correspondence at BOTH levels, each a genuine bijection of setoids — `transform_intertwiner_iso : Deloop_Transforms ≅[Sets] Deloop_Intertwiners` over a bare monoid (where there are no inverses, so 'conjugator' would have no referent) and `transform_conjugator_iso : Deloop_Transforms ≅[Sets] Deloop_Conjugators` over a group, which is the issue's literal 'set of conjugating elements'; both identify witnesses by their underlying element, disclosed rather than silent. `transform_iff_conjugate_SWAPPED` gives the source-on-the-left form, the two ∃-level statements being equivalent in-file by composing biconditionals with a common left-hand side (h ↦ h⁻¹), while the COMPONENT-level statements remain direction-sensitive. Awodey §7.7 Example 7.21's consequence: `Fun_IsGroupoid : IsGroupoid D → IsGroupoid [C, D]` (stronger than Awodey's, whose domain is a groupoid too), hence `Deloop_Fun_IsGroupoid`, with the inverse's component `= grp_inv h` by `eq_refl` and `deloop_ginv_conjugates` showing it conjugates back — and H^G is NOT a group: `Deloop_S3_two_objects` gives two provably distinct objects, and `Deloop_Fun_S3_not_deloop` (+ `_rev`) shows it is not equivalent to the delooping of any monoid, in either direction. COMPOSITION ORDER is derived, not assumed (`deloop_compose_mon_op` by `eq_refl`; `conjugates_is_conjugation` identifies `Conjugates` with Structure/Groupoid.v's `conjugate` on the nose): the TARGET multiplies on the left of h, matching Awodey's 2nd edition (Oxford Logic Guides 52, p. 167: `g(x)·h = h·f(x)`, inner automorphism `y ↦ h·y·h⁻¹`). Awodey CHANGED this formula between editions — the 1st-edition CMU pre-print (Sept 2005) §7.7 Ex. 7.21, printed p. 175, has `f(x)·h = h·g(x)` with `y ↦ h⁻¹·y·h` — and issue #241 cites the 1st edition and quotes it CORRECTLY; both readings are consistent under their own edition's composition-order convention, and there is no erratum in the issue. VACUITY is proved away rather than asserted — `abelian_conjugates_agree` shows the theorem is empty over an abelian target and `Bool_Xor_abelian`/`Z3_abelian` disqualify the older Z/2 and Z/3 witnesses, so the witness is S3: two homomorphisms Z/2 → S3 conjugate by the non-central 3-cycle (`S3_hom_conjugate`, `S3_conjugator_noncentral`), with `S3_conjugation_needs_nonabelian` deriving nonabelianness from the witness itself
- **Construction/Deloop/Functors.v**: Mac Lane §I.3 Exercise 3, complete across its four-book coverage — what a functor IS at the special categories, built by REUSE: the homomorphism record is Structure/Groupoid.v's `MonHom` (inverse preservation already its `MonHom_grp_inv`), and part (b) is already Construction/Deloop/Transform.v's `Deloop_map`/`Deloop_unmap` with its stronger eq_refl record-equality round trip — neither is re-proved. The spine is one correspondence: functors `Deloop M ⟶ C` are a choice of object c with a monoid homomorphism `M → hom_monoid C c` (`functor_of_hom_monoid`/`hom_monoid_of_functor`, round trips both ways), and what the file adds at (b) is `Deloop_map_spine` (`Deloop_map` IS the spine at the single object, the types agreeing definitionally by `hom_monoid_Deloop`) plus the functor-side round trip at full `Functor_Setoid` strength (`Deloop_functor_round`; Transform.v states it only pointwise). Part (c): functors `B G ⟶ Sets` = `MSetoidAction`s (permutation representations, Riehl Example 1.3.9 left convention) and `B G ⟶ Matr R` = `MatrixRep`s (dimension = the chosen object); right actions translate to left actions of the opposite monoid along `Deloop_op` (mutual inversion NOT stated — it would combine `action_functor_round` with `Deloop_op`'s iso laws); Riehl Corollary 1.3.10 is the named `fobj_iso` instantiation `action_automorphism` — the iso TYPE is vacuous at one object, the content is the definitional `_to`/`_from` readings; Awodey §7.1's cocycle characterization `Cocycle`/`functor_of_cocycle`/`cocycle_of_functor` with round trips on BOTH sides (`cocycle_round`, `cocycle_functor_round`) and the worked witness `translation_cocycle` ((ℤ,≤)→(ℤ,+) by differences — Awodey's ℝ transposed to ℤ for axiom-freedom). Instance/Proset/Monotone.v is part (a): the ordinary, DECIDABILITY-FREE correspondence between functors `Proset P ⟶ Proset Q` and bare `MonotoneFun`s — completing two in-tree fragments: the enriched `EnrichedFunctor_Two_monotone` case-splits on `tpre_dec`, and Instance/Pos.v's `MonotoneAsFunctor` is the forward leg only over posets — with the thin-target smart constructor operationalizing "functor laws are automatic in a thin category" (Seven Sketches 3.42), both round trips, and witnesses: `seventeen_twentyfour` (Example 60's 17/24 map as a genuine `MonotoneFun` over bool's false≤true order, its functor via the constructor; plus a hand-built `_2` reading, `_2` not being a `Proset`), endofunctors of (ℕ,≤) = non-decreasing sequences, and the tree-of-life `taxonomy_rank` (Example 61 — five terms, four generating edges via `clos_refl_trans`, monotonicity by induction on the generating relation). Mac Lane §II.4's construction 2 rides the same dictionary one level up (Instance/Fun/Action.v): `MSet M` — M-actions with `Equivariant` maps — with the equivalence `MSet_Fun_equiv : [Deloop M, Sets] ≅[Cat] MSet M` (a natural transformation over one object IS a single equivariant map; the functor-side round trip rebuilt with a transparent component family since the in-tree lemma is opaque), with `MSet_hom_iso` stating the unwinding literally as a hom-setoid isomorphism, and Exercise 5's permutation reading: `perm_of_group_element` (= the donor's `action_automorphism` read at FinSet, disclosed reuse) plus the object-level identification `FinPermRep`/`functor_of_perm_rep`/`perm_rep_of_functor` along the spine with invertibility automatic (`perm_rep_acts_by_bijections`); no separate category of permutation representations and no group-finiteness hypothesis, disclosed in the header
- **Construction/Elements/Kan.v**: Mac Lane §III.2 Ex 3 — Kan's form of the coyoneda lemma, `Nat(K, D(a,−)) ≅ Nat(Δa, Q)` for `K : D ⟶ Sets` with `Q := Elements_proj K`, delivered WITH the naturality in `a` the exercise's "natural isomorphism" reading requires: **`kan_coyoneda : KanNat K ≅[[D^op, Sets]] KanCone K`**, an isomorphism in the functor category, with `kan_coyoneda_to_component`/`_from_component` `eq_refl` against the pointwise `kan_iso_at`'s legs — so the naturality statement is a machine-checked UPGRADE of the pointwise one, not a parallel construction. `KanNat := Curried_Hom ([D,Sets]) K ◯ Curried_Hom D` is a PURE ASSEMBLY (zero obligations, all three functor laws inherited) and `KanCone := ConePresheaf (Elements_proj K)` is pure reuse; both object actions pinned by `eq_refl`. **The primary form is `ACone`, deliberately**: `Structure/Cone.v:79`'s `ConePresheaf` already IS the right-hand side as a functor of `a` with its functor laws proved, so taking the Δ-reading as primary would mean rebuilding that presheaf for no gain — the Δ-reading is delivered as a proved bridge both ways (`kan_iso_transform a : KanNat a ≅[Sets] KanDelta a`, Mac Lane's literal statement) rather than dropped, and the two are provably NOT convertible, the cone law `fmap f ∘ ψx ≈ ψy` sitting one `id_right` from Δ-naturality `fmap f ∘ τx ≈ τy ∘ id` with `Transform` additionally carrying `naturality_sym`. **The mathematical crux is that objects of `Elements K` are compared by LEIBNIZ equality**, so `(d,x)` and `(d,y)` are different objects when `x ≈ y` without `x = y`; the backward passage therefore needs its component at `d` to respect `≈`, and that respectfulness is DERIVED (`kan_leg_respects`) from cone coherence at the identity-carried morphism `elements_same` rather than assumed — the same shape as `Theory/Natural/Transformation/Arrows.v`'s `τ_arr_respects`. **The universe restriction enters at the PACKAGING, not the mathematics, and that is pinned rather than inferred**: `KanNat` and `KanCone` each carry `u <= u0` (D's objects at or below its homs), hence so do `kan_iso_at`, `kan_coyoneda` and `kan_iso_transform`, while `kan_cone_of_nat` and `kan_nat_of_cone` carry no such constraint — three formability negatives reject the packaged forms under `Constraint uh < uo` while two positive controls accept the raw passages at that very setting; the two halves are independent (the RHS through `ConePresheaf` chained via `Elements`, the LHS because `KanNat K a` must be a `Type@{u0}` indexed by `obj[D] : Type@{u}`), the inherited donor pins (`Fun`'s `u0 = u2`, `Compose`'s single hom-and-proof level) are named and neither introduced here, and none is claimed unavoidable. Seven conversion negatives reduce to THREE causes — rebuilt law fields (both records have primitive projections WITH eta, so record equality is field equality and the law fields are this file's own opaque lemmas; a positive control shows the underlying FUNCTIONS agree by `eq_refl`, locating the obstruction exactly at the proofs), missing `sigT` eta (stdlib `sigT` is not covered by `Lib/Foundation.v`'s `Set Primitive Projections`, which defeats even the bare leg family), and the Δ-bridge's `id_right` (where the DATA returns on the nose in both directions, so only the law field fails). Engineering finding recorded in-header: `nat_id`'s component is `fmap[F] id` and not `id` (`Theory/Natural/Transformation.v:220`), so the identity of a FUNCTOR category reindexes and `kan_coyoneda`'s inverse-law obligations need an `id_right` that `kan_iso_at`'s — living in `Sets`, where `id` is `setoid_morphism_id` — do not; the tree records the same `nat_id` fact twice, at `Functor/Representable.v:319-321` about `repr_induced_id` and at `Functor/Representable/Functorial.v:239-244` about the comparison with `repr_pair_iso`, which is itself declared in `Functor/Hom/Yoneda/Iso.v:162`. Distinct from `Theory/Coend/Yoneda.v:174`'s `coyoneda_reduction` (∫^x C(x,c) × F x ≅ F c), a different theorem sharing the name, which gets its own header paragraph. 46/46 constants closed under the global context. NOT delivered: the presheaf/contravariant orientation over `PElements`, naturality in `K` as well as `a`, any identification of either side with `Structure/Limit/Kan/Pointwise.v`'s pointwise formula or of `Q` as a discrete opfibration, and — the one worth knowing — the Δ-reading is NOT upgraded to a naturality statement, `KanDelta` never being exhibited as a functor `D^op ⟶ Sets`, so Mac Lane's own phrasing lands one strength below the `ACone` headline

- **Instance/Cat/Pullback.v**: Mac Lane §III.5 Ex 3 — the fibre product of categories, and which category of categories it is a pullback IN. **THE ISSUE'S REQUESTED ARTIFACT `Cat_Pullbacks` IS NOT BUILT, AND ITS NATURAL CANDIDATE IS REFUTED**: `FibreProduct_not_Cat_pullback` shows the fibre product is NOT a pullback in `Cat` at the cospan `1 --true--> Indiscrete bool <--false-- 1`, where the iso `true ≅ false` supplies the natural isomorphism Ho(Cat)'s square condition asks for while `IB_FibreProduct_empty` shows the apex has no objects at all, so no mediator can exist. **Read that narrowly and the file insists on it**: `point_cospan_Cat_IsPullback` proves the SAME cospan DOES have a `Cat` pullback (the terminal category), so what is refuted is this CANDIDATE APEX, not pullbacks in `Cat` — `HasPullbacks Cat` is NEITHER PROVED NOR REFUTED and the iso-comma/pseudopullback, the standard replacement, is explicitly UNATTEMPTED. The cause is the ambient: `Instance/Cat.v:28-37` says in terms that this `Cat` is Ho(Cat), `Functor_Setoid` identifying naturally isomorphic functors, while the fibre product is built out of Leibniz equalities `F a = G b`. So the universal property is proved in `StrictCat` (`Instance/StrictCat.v:56`, `Functor_StrictEq_Setoid`): `FibreProduct_IsPullback : ObjUIP C → IsPullback F G (FibreProduct F G) FP_fst FP_snd`, stated first at `Theory/Morphisms/Stability.v:53`'s APEX-PINNED `IsPullback` and only then packaged as `FibreProduct_Pullback`/`StrictCat_HasPullbacks` (the #326 discipline). **THE HYPOTHESIS IS PROVED NECESSARY, NOT MERELY TAKEN**: `FP_uniqueness_forces_UIP` shows that deriving the uniqueness clause uniformly — every cospan, no hypothesis on the base — entails UIP for every type, the countermodel being `1 --x--> Indiscrete X <--x-- 1` whose fibre-product objects ARE the loops at `x`, each naming a mediator satisfying both triangles; it is the shape of `arrow_mul_respects_forces_UIP` (Theory/Category/Monoid.v) and `Discrete_DiscreteRigid_forces_UIP` (Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v), and it is axiom-free (`inj_pair2_eq_dec` at a decidable index). Scope it precisely: it is about the uniqueness clause OF THIS APEX, so it does not refute `HasPullbacks StrictCat` with some other apex, and no such refutation is offered — hence `StrictCat_HasPullbacks` is delivered only from the blanket `∀ C, ObjUIP C`, free for decidable object equality by Hedberg (`ObjUIP_of_ObjDecEq`, `FibreProduct_IsPullback_dec`). **Where the hypothesis is spent is measured rather than gestured at, and the two halves differ**: on the fibre product exactly ONE of the pullback's fields needs it (`FP_med_unique`, through its sole-consumer helper `FP_obj_eq`) while `FP_commutes_strict`, `FP_med`, `FP_med_fst` and `FP_med_snd` take no hypothesis at all; on the comma side it is spent by THREE LEMMAS in FOUR USES per side — `slice_arrow_reflect` (where it does work nothing else could: the codomain coordinate of a transported morphism equality is a loop at `c` and must be `eq_refl`) plus `Slice_med_arrow`, which spends it twice, and `Slice_med_unique`, both through `fp_hom_cast_irr` — a header sentence claiming a single point, and a later one claiming three uses, were both corrected before landing. The six `Program Definition`s of those sections (`Slice_proj`/`Slice_Arrow`/`Slice_med` and the coslice twins) are genuinely hypothesis-free, `Default Proof Using "All"` reaching only `Qed`/`Defined` results — a header claiming otherwise was corrected too, and it had erred toward making the file look WORSE than it is. **The comma half IS delivered**, consuming `Construction/Comma.v:105-108`'s own PIE-limit framing rather than restating it — and the tree's PRE-EXISTING comma UMP over these same two arrow-category projections, `comma_diagram_ump` (`Construction/Comma/Diagram.v:484`, Mac Lane §II.6 Ex 5, in a module this file Requires and whose own header calls it "what exhibits it as a 'pullback'-style limit"), is disclosed rather than left to be found: it mediates into the GENERAL comma `(S ↓ T)` over the `MediatesDiagram` competitor class at `≈[Cat]`, where these two are about the ONE-OBJECT cospans, use the pullback's own competitor class, and are stated at `≈[StrictCat]`: `Slice_IsPullback` (C/c is the pullback of `Arrow_cod` along `Diagonal 1 c`) and `Coslice_IsPullback` (dually, `Arrow_dom`), both in `StrictCat`, both with the tree's OWN `Slice`/`Coslice` as apex — genuine assemblies, not instances of the general theorem, and no isomorphism between `Slice C c` and the fibre product of its cospan is built. Strengths measured strict-first: `FP_commutes_strict` is a `:=` with NO tactic (the object component IS the equality each object carries, the morphism component IS the equation each morphism carries), both triangles have `eq_refl` object components with `reflexivity` arrow halves, and `fobj`/`fmap` of `FP_fst ◯ FP_med` are `q1`'s at LEIBNIZ equality — while the whole-record equality is REFUTED, the three `Functor` law fields being rebuilt proofs, as is `Slice C c = FibreProduct Arrow_cod (Diagonal 1 c)`, those two being different categories (one stores an arrow, the other a pair with a proof). **Universes measured off the CONSTRAINT BLOCKS with the packaging blamed rather than the construction**: `FibreProduct` carries bounds only and no identification between A's and B's object universes, whereas `FibreProduct_IsPullback` displays three separate category binders while its block contains `u = u1`, `u = u3`, `u0 = u2` and `u0 = u4` — all three object universes identified and all three hom-and-proof universes too, which is what stating an `IsPullback` in `StrictCat` costs, inherited and not claimed unavoidable; the probe's first negative localizes it by accepting `FibreProduct`, `FP_fst`/`FP_snd` and `ObjUIP` at three object universes declared strictly apart and rejecting only the packaged form. `FP_uniqueness_forces_UIP` has its HYPOTHESIS at `Category@{u0 Set Set}`, which weakens the hypothesis and so strengthens the theorem; `Slice_IsPullback`/`Coslice_IsPullback` carry no `Set` at all. Prior art measured by shape, not by name alone: nothing in the tree states an `IsPullback` or `Pullback` in either `Cat` or `StrictCat`, `FibreProduct` is a new name, and the only two "fibre/fiber product" occurrences are prose about schemes (`Structure/Pullback.v:113`) and about base change along a slice (`Construction/Slice/Pullback.v:26`); the concrete `HasPullbacks` roster BEFORE this commit was `Sets_HasPullbacks` and `FinSet_Pullbacks` with three generic conditionals besides, cited from `Instance/Sets/Pullback.v`'s own header rather than re-derived — the issue's "exactly one inhabitant" reading being stale, and stale because of that file — with `StrictCat_HasPullbacks` a FOURTH conditional added here, not a generic one, being for one named category under a blanket hypothesis. `Test/ProbeCatPullback337.v` pins the boundary with **4 negatives of THREE kinds** kept lexically apart — one FORMABILITY (the universe identification, with its localizing controls), two CONVERSION differing in CAUSE (Negative 2 fails only in the LAW fields, its controls showing the data agrees at `eq_refl`; Negative 3 fails in the DATA) and one TYPING (`IB_FibreProduct_empty` at term level, controlled at the AGREEING cospan so the failure is the cospan's) — plus an instrument check, each stripped and its kind read off the WHOLE error message — deliberately not the tail, since the typing negative's tail ends in a `cannot unify` that would misread as conversion, its distinguishing `cannot satisfy constraint` sitting mid-message — rename-simulated 10/10 unpadded against an instrument-checked baseline. **108/108 constants closed under the global context** (62 by the glob, the other 46 `Program` obligations the glob does not record, all reached by fully-qualified name). NOT delivered: no `HasPullbacks Cat` and no refutation of it; no iso-comma, pseudopullback, bilimit or 2-categorical universal property; no unconditional `HasPullbacks StrictCat`; no essential-uniqueness statement for pullbacks in `StrictCat` at all; the GENERAL comma `(S ↓ T)` as the pullback of `⟨dom, cod⟩` along `S ∣→ T` is not built, only the two one-object cospans; and nothing about pasting, stability, equalizers, other finite limits, or completeness for either category of categories
- **Construction/Coproduct/Indexed.v**, **Instance/Cat/Coproduct.v**: Mac Lane §III.5 Ex 4 + Riehl 2nd ed. §3.6 — the indexed disjoint union of categories and **`Cat_HasIndexedCoproducts : HasIndexedCoproducts Cat`**, the dual the tree had only in its product half. **THE HEADLINE IS THAT NO HYPOTHESIS IS SPENT ON THE CONSTRUCTION OR ITS UNIVERSAL PROPERTY** — `SigmaCat`, `SigmaCat_inj`, `SigmaCat_case`, `SigmaCat_case_inj`, `SigmaCat_case_unique` and `SigmaCat_ump` take NO decidability and NO UIP, on the index or on anything else (verified at the signatures, not inferred). The reason is structural: every obligation quantifies over BOTH endpoint indices, so the index proof `e : i = j` relates two universally quantified variables and can be ELIMINATED by `destruct`, after which every transport disappears and the goal is the summand's own law; uniqueness survives because `∀ i, G ◯ inj i ≈ F i` is a DEPENDENT FUNCTION of `i` and so already carries whatever coherence the index type's paths demand. **The encoding is what buys that**: `hom (i;x) (j;y) := ∃ e : i = j, ob_cast C e x ~> y` with the hom-SETOID `∃ p : e₁ = e₂, mor_cast C p f ≈ g` comparing the two index proofs at LEIBNIZ equality — the `Theory/OGraph.v` move of localizing the escape to a SETOID CHOICE. Read that precisely: the setoid QUOTIENTS BY NOTHING, which is exactly why fullness later needs UIP. **Rival encodings are refuted BY ARGUMENT, and the file says so rather than implying they were compiled** — THREE refuted plus one excluded, an earlier revision of this bullet having called all four "refuted": a decider is not refuted at all, it is EXCLUDED by the exercise's hypotheses; the `∀ e : i = j, …` reading fails twice over, once on MATHEMATICS (for provably distinct `i`, `j` that type is INHABITED by the vacuous function, so it adds a morphism between every cross-summand pair and yields the INDISCRETE category on the summands, not their disjoint union — this half IS compiled, in the probe, as `probe_forall_encoding_inhabited`) and once because no identity is definable at a loop; the sigma-plus-∀ HYBRID inherits that second failure; and a conversion-only setoid on index proofs is not a category, index-proof associativity holding only propositionally. **WHERE A HYPOTHESIS IS SPENT IT IS PROVED NECESSARY**: `SigmaCat_inj_Full` takes `IdxUIP I`, and **`sigma_inj_Full_forces_UIP`** shows fullness of ONE injection — given an object of that summand with the relevant hom-sets inhabited — ENTAILS `∀ e : i = i, e = eq_refl`, instantiated at the terminal category as `inj_Full_forces_UIP_at_One` (`_1`'s hom family ignores its endpoints, so the inhabitation hypothesis is `ttt`); the pattern is `Theory/OGraph.v`'s `coarse_respectfulness_entails_UIP` and `Theory/Category/Monoid.v`'s `arrow_mul_respects_forces_UIP`. **Faithfulness SPLITS**: free for a CONSTANT family (`SigmaCat_const_inj_Faithful`, via the retraction `SigmaCat_case (fun _ => Id)`, which is definitionally the identity on the summand), `IdxUIP` plus the axiom-free `UIP_shift` in general — and **no necessity result is claimed for faithfulness**, no countermodel being exhibited. By-product: **`Faithful_cancel : Faithful (G ◯ F) → Faithful F`** is new, the tree having only the closure direction `Faithful_Compose` (`Theory/Functor.v:411`). **The `Colimit`/`DiscreteCat_Functor` route the issue prescribes is DECLINED for TWO independent reasons kept apart**: routing an indexed product through `Limit (DiscreteCat_Functor …)` pins the ambient hom and proof universes to `Set` — fatal at `Cat`. **CORRECTED by #339's measurement, which this sentence originally got wrong by naming one donor: it takes TWO.** `DiscreteCat_Functor` (`Instance/Discrete.v:52`) is universe-unannotated and fixes the SHAPE at `DiscreteCat@{u Set Set}`, but ALONE it leaves the ambient FREE (`C : Category@{u0 u2 u2}`), and a `Cone` over it is free too, so `Cone` is NOT a co-donor; the ambient pin arrives only with `IsALimit`/`Limit`, which identify the shape's hom and proof with the ambient's. `Structure/Limit/Product/Finite.v`'s bullet cites the composite and is sound; `Functor/Hom/Limit.v`'s names both donors and was right all along. `DiscreteCat` ITSELF is properly annotated `@{o h p}`, so the defect is the FUNCTOR's — AND, independently, **the passage does not exist**: `Structure/Limit/Coproduct.v:113` carries `colimit_is_indexed_coproduct` (Limit → IsIndexedCoproduct) with NO converse, so a `Colimit` reading is not one step from what is proved. The elementary apex-pinned `IsIndexedCoproduct` is taken first and `Build_HasIndexedCoproducts` second (the #326/#335/#336 discipline), both `:=` with NO tactic — and **the packaging is transparent in ALL THREE fields**, the class's object, injection AND universal-property fields returning the elementary data at `eq_refl` (probe), so the bundled class carries the very record `SigmaCat_IsIndexedCoproduct` proves rather than a rebuilt copy. **The binary comparison reaches ISOMORPHISM OF CATEGORIES**: `SigmaBool_strict_iso : SigmaCat (BoolFam C D) ≅[StrictCat] C ∐ D`, both round trips at `Functor_StrictEq_Setoid` with every object component `eq_refl`, with `SigmaBool_iso` the DERIVED and weaker `≅[Cat]` (equivalence) reading — no stronger identification is ACHIEVED (what is established is that `eq_refl` does not typecheck; no impossibility of a Leibniz equality is proved), the object types being a dependent pair against a tagged sum, refuted at `eq_refl` and pinned. **Universes measured in the CONSTRAINT BLOCKS**: `SigmaCat@{ui uo oh op us}`'s block is ALL BOUNDS and no identification, and it is SIX constraints rather than the three an earlier revision of this bullet listed — `ui <= us`, `uo <= us`, `oh <= op` (the last being `Class Category`'s own) PLUS `ui <= Projections.u0`, `uo <= Projections.u1`, `oh <= Projections.u1`, which are the stdlib's, `projT1`/`projT2` not being universe polymorphic, so every universe passing through a sigma is additionally capped below a fixed global level; no constraint in the block is an EQUATION, which is the headline, but the enumeration was incomplete — and **the explicit binders are LOAD-BEARING**, measured rather than assumed: written unannotated the same body minimizes so as to IDENTIFY the summands' hom and proof universes and is then rejected under `Constraint uh < up` where the annotated form is accepted (pinned). **The index universe does NOT bound the hom universe, because `eq` is Prop-valued** — a genuine asymmetry with `Construction/Product/Indexed.v`'s `PiCat`, whose hom IS an I-indexed dependent function, so that file's note about `I` bounding the hom universe is correct for the PRODUCT and does not carry over. The UMP-level results DO identify hom with proof (`C : Category@{a b b}`) and the cause is localized to a DONOR with controls: `Functor_Setoid` (`Theory/Functor.v:149`, an unannotated `Program Instance`) is rejected at separated levels while the functor TYPE `C ⟶ D` and a `Unique` over a hom-setoid both elaborate — and since `≈` on functors IS that setoid, no statement of the UMP in this library's vocabulary avoids it; not introduced here, not claimed unavoidable. **Smallness is measured with a PINNED instance**: at an index as large as `obj[Cat@{a b c d e}]` the construction still elaborates but lands one universe UP, so `SigmaCat_IsIndexedCoproduct` is rejected — written unpinned it succeeds, exactly the `Check (Cat : obj[Cat])` phenomenon `Theory/Size.v`'s erratum records. The Grothendieck route is MEASURED, not built: `Construction/Grothendieck/Strict.v:119`'s `Fuip` is UIP on the OBJECTS of every fibre (not on the index), so that route costs strictly more than the direct construction pays, disclosed as a measurement of the donor's stated hypothesis with no claim it could not be rearranged. `Construction/Product/Indexed.v`'s `PiCat` is the exact dual and is treated as a STRUCTURAL PRECEDENT only — the coproduct is not `PiCat` of opposites. Non-vacuity at empty (`SigmaCat_empty_no_obj`), singleton (`SigmaCat_unit_iso`, its one round trip's UIP on `poly_unit` discharged by Hedberg) and two-element indices (`bool_cross_empty_lr`/`_rl`, `bool_summands_not_isomorphic`, and the case functor computing by `eq_refl` with `case_separates_summands` by `discriminate`), with the general `sigma_hom_cross_empty`. **87/87 constants closed under the global context** (50 + 37, counted by `Print Module` so `Program` obligations are included). `Test/ProbeCatCoproduct338.v` pins the three boundaries the targets cannot — 2 FORMABILITY (the load-bearing binders; the `Functor_Setoid` localization, both needing a section that declares levels strictly apart) and 1 CONVERSION (the binary object types) — plus an instrument check and a compiled POSITIVE (`probe_forall_encoding_inhabited`, the inhabitedness half of the encoding-(c) refutation), each negative stripped and its kind read off the error, **rename-simulated 3/3 unpadded** against an instrument-checked baseline; the denominator counts only constants a NEGATIVE names, read off the `Fail` commands rather than from memory, an earlier revision having scored it 4/4 by crediting `SigmaCat_inj`, which occurs only in a control. TWO probe defects were caught before landing and are recorded rather than quietly fixed: a fourth negative fired on an over-application typing error while the statement it reached for was itself FALSE (the packaging being definitional in all three fields), and `Instance/Cat/Coproduct.v` shipped a FALSE PASS whose `Fail` fired on `∐` binding looser than `=` — `SigmaCat (BoolFam C D) = C ∐ D` parses as `(SigmaCat (BoolFam C D) = C) ∐ D` — with the adjacent comment asserting a conversion failure it did not have. - **Construction/Comma/Special.v**: Mac Lane §II.6's remark as recorded statements — the comma of two CONSTANT functors is the "discrete category on the hom-set", the historical origin of the comma notation, delivered at the honest setoid strength: a comma morphism's square degenerates to a WITNESS of `f ≈ g`, so the target is `DiscreteSetoidCat` (objects the carrier, homs the `≈`-witnesses — built here because Instance/Proset.v's `Proset` is Prop-valued `relation` while `≈` is a crelation and the comma carries its square as sigT DATA; `Blurry` of Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v is this construction at the `eq` setoid, cited not imported), with `Comma_Discrete_Hom : (=(b) ↓ =(a)) ≅[Cat] HomDiscrete b a` (one round trip definitional on objects, the other renormalizing unit paddings; the comma-side laws instances of `comma_const_thin`, the HomDiscrete-side ones discharged by that category's own trivially-true hom-setoid). The plain `DiscreteCat` reading is PRICED EXACTLY: `Comma_Discrete_eq` (unconditional functor, ESO), `Full ↔ HomStrict` (≈→=), `Faithful ↔ HomUIP`, the conditional `Comma_Discrete_Hom_eq` under BOTH hypotheses — HE alone is NOT sufficient, since DiscreteCat's hom-setoid is strict eq of proofs — the unconditional necessity `comma_discrete_iso_forces_UIP` (ANY Cat-iso forces HomUIP, via `Cat_Iso_to_Faithful` + thinness), and the countermodel `Blur_no_discrete_iso` (one object, bool homs, trivially-true hom-setoid: HomUIP HOLDS yet the plain reading is FALSE — all comma objects isomorphic, `fobj_iso` extracts `true = false`), with `One_Comma_Discrete_Hom_eq` the degenerate inhabitation check.
