- **Construction/Free/Quiver/Examples.v**: Mac Lane §II.7's worked free categories and Exercise 2 — the concrete identifications the tree's `FreeOnQuiver` had never been pointed at — it is used substantially elsewhere (Construction/Free/Quiver/Presented.v builds the presented category on it, Theory/Diagram.v the diagram/functor bridge), but nothing said what any PARTICULAR free category IS. The engine is ONE reusable lemma: `graded_free_thin` — a quiver carrying a rank function that every edge raises by one, injective on nodes, with edges unique up to `≈`, has a THIN free category — after which every round trip and every functor law below is an equation between parallel arrows and closes in a line; node-UIP is DERIVED from rank-injectivity by `Eqdep_dec.UIP_dec` on nat (Hedberg, axiom-free), so no UIP hypothesis is taken anywhere, and the real content is `graded_het`, the induction in the HETEROGENEOUS form `tlist'_quiver_equiv` demands (parallel paths pass through nodes only propositionally equal, that equality carried as data). **`ordinal_free : FreeOnQuiver (LinQuiver m) ≅[StrictCat] Ordinal m`** — Exercise 2, every finite ordinal free on its linear quiver, at ISOMORPHISM-OF-CATEGORIES strength (the `≅[Cat]` form is derived and labelled weaker), for EVERY m including the empty ordinal (`ordinal_free_0` is separate because the donor `Functor_of_Steps` is stated only for `Ordinal (S n)`; without it the headline would silently exclude the empty case). This is the theorem Instance/Ordinal.v's header had recorded as "remains open" — that note and its bullet above are updated in the same commit rather than left contradicting the tree. `arrow_free : FreeOnQuiver ArrowQuiver ≅[StrictCat] _2` with both comparison functors identity-on-objects, so both strict witnesses are literally `eq_refl`; the composable pair is the m=3 INSTANCE (`chain_free := ordinal_free 3`), not a duplicate construction, with its content delivered as PROVED hom classification — three singleton ascending hom-sets, three identity-only diagonals, three provably EMPTY descending ones, and `chain_e10_length = 2` pinning the composite as the two-edge path rather than a new generator — plus `linear_hom_iff` (a hom-set is inhabited exactly when the indices are ordered, and parallel paths agree). Bonus `Ordinal_2_strict_iso` supplies the strict form Ordinal.v had only at Cat strength. **ERRATUM (#300): the universe pin this bullet used to call "PROVED forced" is GONE. Read the correction precisely — the pin was REAL and the mechanism diagnosed here was RIGHT; what was wrong was only its PERMANENCE. It was forced by the constructor as written, and the constructor has since been generalized.** `Build_Quiver_Standard_Eq` did return `Quiver@{u u0 Set}`, and `FreeOnQuiver`'s `o <= h <= p` did drag objects down — but only because the constructor resolved `eq_equivalence` to the stdlib's `Corelib.Classes.CRelationClasses.eq_equivalence@{u}`, which carries ONE universe binder and so cannot separate carrier level from proof level. Naming the proof universe and spending `Lib/Setoid.v:47`'s own `eq_equivalence@{t u}` — polymorphic since it was written, for exactly this purpose — lifts it in six lines. **Scope the lift precisely** (an earlier draft of this erratum overstated it and was itself corrected): the lift is at the CONSTRUCTOR, demonstrated by `FreeOnQuiver@{uo uh up} (LinQuiver@{uo uh up} 3)` being accepted now and REJECTED before with `Cannot enforce Set = up`. It does NOT move the identifications in that file — their statements were untouched, so `ordinal_free` still reads `FreeOnQuiver@{Set Set Set}` before and after, and their outer binders were instantiable above `Set` beforehand anyway, so accepting `Set < o` on them demonstrates nothing. **The old note's closing caveat therefore STANDS**: these are identifications of SMALL categories at `Set`, and a consumer wanting them larger cannot get there by instantiation, only by restating them. What the lift buys is that NEWLY WRITTEN statements over these quivers may be stated at an arbitrary level — which is what Instance/Square.v needs. Of the old note's two corrected measurements, only one was an error (`Ordinal_2_strict_iso` is NOT pinned by `TwoHom : … → Set`, since `Set <= h` is not `h = Set`, and it accepted a larger instance before the lift too); its record of `linear_hom_iff` as `@{}` was CORRECT when written and became stale only because this commit changed that constant to `@{u}`. The ENGINE was never pinned (`graded_free_thin@{u u0 u1}` over an arbitrary quiver). Mac Lane's loop example is NOT built here — issue #802 owns it and its file is not yet in tree, credited as such. 72 named constants / 80 with obligations, all closed under the global context
- **Construction/Free/Quiver/Constructions.v**: Mac Lane §II.7 Ex 1 — opposite and product graphs, defined so the forgetful functor preserves them, and here it does ON THE NOSE: `QuiverOfCat (C^op) = QuiverOp (QuiverOfCat C)` and `QuiverOfCat (C ∏ D) = QuiverProd …` (with the `Forgetful`-phrased forms `Forgetful_preserves_op`/`_prod`) all hold at **Leibniz equality of the whole `Quiver` record, by `eq_refl`** — not field-by-field, not an isomorphism in `QuiverCategory`. Three measured facts allow it: `QuiverOfCat` carries no Program obligation, `Opposite`/`Product` are plain Definitions, and `Quiver` and `Setoid` both have primitive projections with eta. Arrow-level preservation for the opposite is `eq_refl` too (`Forgetful_preserves_op_fmap`), and `QuiverOp_invol`/`QuiverOp_map_invol` are `eq_refl` where the donor's own `op_invol` (Construction/Opposite.v) is Qed-opaque. A product UMP is proved and HONESTLY WEAKENED: both triangles `QuiverPair_Fst`/`_Snd` hold at Leibniz equality, `QuiverPair_eta` at `≈`, and `QuiverPair_unique`'s hypotheses are Leibniz rather than `≈` — the `≈` form needs respectfulness of `QuiverPair` in its arguments, which is NOT proved and is disclosed; no `Cartesian QuiverCategory` instance is claimed. The cases that do NOT reach `eq_refl` were measured with `Fail` rather than assumed: `Forgetful_preserves_fst`/`_snd` (`Fst`/`Snd` are `Program Instance`s, so `fmap_respects` is opaque) and `QuiverSwap_invol` (surjective pairing is not definitional for stdlib `prod`), both delivered at `≈`; likewise `Lib/Datatypes.v`'s `prod_setoid` is shown unusable as the edgeset donor, which is why `edgeset_prod` spells its `Equivalence` fields explicitly. Those measurements are GUARDED, not merely recorded: **Test/ProbeQuiverConstructions.v** (the Test/ProbeFunnyPoly.v convention) states each strengthening as a `Fail` — the two projections, `QuiverSwap_invol`, the `prod_setoid` substitution, and `QuiverPair_eta` — every one pinned to a genuine CONVERSION failure and paired with a positive control that must SUCCEED, so a rename or a record change breaks the file loudly instead of turning the `Fail`s vacuously green. Disclosed and NOT witnessed: no competitor failing the two triangles is exhibited, so `QuiverPair_unique`'s discriminating power rests on eta's non-definitionality alone. - **Construction/Free/Quiver/Presented.v**: Categories presented by generators and relations — Mac Lane §II.8 chained onto §II.7, closing the free-and-quotient story Construction/Free.v's header narrates. The general engine is in Construction/Quotient.v: the Type-valued relation family `HomRelT` (over which `HomCongruence`/`Quotient`/`QuotientLift` are now stated, `HomRel` surviving as the Prop-valued abbreviation the PROP towers use and `Quotient_HomEqProp` staying pinned to it), the five-constructor generated congruence `CongClosure` with `cc_least`, and Mac Lane's Proposition 1 as `cc_kernel` — the kernel lemma is a one-line corollary of leastness at `FunctorKernel F`, and the closure must be Type-valued precisely because that kernel congruence is (the hom-setoid `≈` is a crelation, so a Prop closure cannot be eliminated into it). On top: `PresentedQuiver G R` as the quotient of `FreeOnQuiver G` by the congruence generated by path equations, with `presented_universal` — naming the images of the generators subject to R determines a functor out of the presented category uniquely — and `presented_restrict`/`presented_restrict_satisfies` for the converse leg, so the correspondence runs both ways though it is not packaged as a bijection; uniqueness is at STRICT (`hom_cast`/`Functor_StrictEq_Setoid`) strength, stronger than a Cat-level statement and implying it through `strict_equiv_implies_fun_equiv`. Parallelism of equations is structural (`PathRel` is indexed by a shared source and target) and the projection is full but deliberately not faithful — Fong and Spivak's "equations merge, never add". Finiteness is data (`FiniteQuiver`/`FiniteEquations` over `Fin.t` indices, with retractions on the quiver's nodes and edges, no choice principle), witnessed by the commutative-triangle presentation whose two halves are proved (`ord3_free_distinct`, `ord3_relation_holds`) — the isomorphism with `Instance/Ordinal.v`'s `Ordinal 3` is stated as a target, not proven; Riehl's two distinct diagonals are proved natively over the square quiver (with `presented_no_eqns_separates` showing the empty presentation collapses nothing), and presented monoids fall out as the one-object case through `hom_monoid`

A graph cone needs **NO functoriality of the diagram**, and the file shows exactly where that lives: `AGraphCone D c` carries `gvertex_map (x : nodes G) : c ~> D x` with **one triangle per EDGE** (`gcone_coherence e : dedge D e ∘ gvertex_map x ≈ gvertex_map y`), where `Structure/Cone.v`'s `ACone` states one per ARROW. **The issue's suggested donors were already in tree under other names and are REUSED rather than rebuilt**: `Theory/Diagram.v:151` already defines `Diagram G C := QuiverHomomorphism G (QuiverOfCat C)` — the issue's "graph morphism into the underlying graph of C" — and `:240`'s `FunctorOfDiagram D := InducedFunctor G D` is already the factorization through the free category, so nothing here re-derives from raw `QuiverHomomorphism`. (The issue's own line numbers are largely STALE — it cites `:205`/`:398`/`:464`/`:518`/`:550` in `Construction/Free/Quiver.v`, of which only `:54` is right; `:518` is `UnitQuiverCatAdjunction`, not `UniversalArrowQuiverCat` — the same error recorded in the **`Instance/Rng/Polynomial.v`** bullet below, there being no `Construction/Free/Quiver.v` bullet in this index at all. The real sites are `:216`/`:409`/`:475`/`:529`/`:561`.) **THE CORRESPONDENCE RUNS BOTH WAYS AND THE LIMIT HALF IS PURE CONVERSION**: `ACone_of_AGraphCone` extends a graph cone along PATHS (the file's one induction, via `gcone_dpath`) and `AGraphCone_of_ACone` restricts to single edges; at limit level `graph_limitcone` and `limitcone_graph_limit` are `:=` terms with **NO tactic** — machine-checked by printing the bodies, which are bare applications — because apex and legs agree on the nose and `obj[FreeOnQuiver G]` IS `nodes G`, so the two `∃!` statements are convertible and every bit of proof content sits in the cone-level passages. `graph_limit_iff_limitcone` packages both. `graph_limit_IsALimit` is the apex-only reading, labelled the WEAKER one — no separation is proved, so "strictly" is not claimed. Strengths measured rather than guessed: legs and apexes cross **both ways at `eq_refl`**, while the three WHOLE-RECORD round trips are REFUTED at `eq_refl` — all four records have primitive projections with eta, so record equality is field equality and the coherence fields are rebuilt proofs (`≈` being `crelation`-valued makes them data); the cone-side round trip is delivered at `≈` through `AConeEquiv`. **A UNIVERSE QUESTION THE FILE DECLINED TO ANSWER IS ANSWERED HERE, AND ATTRIBUTED**: `AGraphCone` displays `C : Category@{u u0 u0}` where `ACone` displays hom and proof apart, and the identification is REAL rather than a display artifact — and it is **`Diagram`'s**, not this file's. The localization needs three controls, not two: under `Constraint uh < up`, `QuiverOfCat C`, `ACone c F` **and `QuiverHomomorphism G (QuiverOfCat C)`** are all formable while only `Diagram G C` is rejected (`Cannot enforce up = uh`) — an earlier revision shipped only the first two, which leave standing the obvious alternative that `Diagram` definitionally unfolds to. Sharper still, and it changes what the pin means: `Diagram` opens with an unannotated `Context {C : Category}` (`Theory/Diagram.v:143-151`), so this is a minimization artifact of the family `Construction/Free/Quiver/Examples.v` records, repairable upstream with explicit binders rather than inherent content. Non-vacuity is general in the category and the two arrows, though NOT in the diagram: `triangle_graph_limit u v : IsLimitGraphCone (TriangleGraphCone u v)` holds for an ARBITRARY `C` and arbitrary composable `u`, `v`, but at the diagram `TriangleDiagram u v (v ∘ u)` whose third edge is LITERALLY the composite — and that restriction is load-bearing, since for a general `w` the same legs are not even a cone (the X→Z coherence would demand `w ≈ v ∘ u`). The `.v` header says this; an earlier revision of this bullet dropped it, with `graph_limit_med … (tri_gcone_of_arrow f) = f` by `eq_refl` and a mediator that COMPUTES over `Coq`; the `loop_*` block exhibits the no-functoriality point at a shape with arbitrarily long paths (`loop_paths_distinct`), and the header discloses that it is a witness for the cone condition only — no limit, since that would be an equalizer nobody builds here. 73/73 constants closed under the global context (counted over the glob's `def`+`prf`+`proj`+`rec` entries plus the three `Build_*` constructors, which appear in none of the usual sources). `Test/ProbeQuiverLimit332.v` pins the boundary with 2 negatives of DIFFERENT KINDS — one CONVERSION (`dpath D p` is not `fmap[FunctorOfDiagram D] p` at a VARIABLE path, `dpath` being a `Fixpoint` against a `tlist'_rect`; they DO agree at one edge, which is the shipped `dpath_singleton_is_fmap`) and one FORMABILITY (the `Diagram` universe identification, whose two controls localize the cause) — plus an instrument check, each stripped and its kind confirmed, rename-simulated at 7/7. NOT delivered: no dual (cocones/colimits over a graph); no setoid on `AGraphCone` and hence no essential uniqueness; no preservation, reflection or creation; no link to `Theory/Diagram.v`'s `Commutative`; no `GraphLimit` from a completeness hypothesis; and the three refuted whole-record round trips are measured in the header but pinned NOWHERE
- **Construction/Free/Groupoid.v**: Mac Lane §II.7 Ex 3 — the free groupoid on a graph, and (Instance/Grp/Free.v) the free group with its adjunction. Built by REUSE, not by a new inductive: `FreeGroupoid G := QuotientCong (FreeOnQuiver (SignedQuiver G)) FreeGroupoidRel` — adjoin formally reversed edges, then quotient by exactly TWO generating cancellation equations, `CongClosure` (Construction/Quotient.v) supplying symmetry, transitivity, composition-congruence and `≈`-inclusion, and `cc_kernel` supplying `QuotientLift`'s hypothesis with no second induction. `FreeGroupoid_IsGroupoid : IsGroupoid (FreeGroupoid G)` is the PREDICATE (Structure/Groupoid.v), NOT Construction/Groupoid.v's core construction — the two are different things and the file says so. **First consumer of `Lib/TList.v`'s `tlist_rev`**, the edge-flip-parameterized reversal kit that had sat unused since it was written (measured: 21 lines of TList.v, no other file; the `Lib/NETList.v` hit is the sibling `netlist_rev`, a different function, disclosed so a later grep does not appear to contradict the claim). That reuse buys STRENGTH, not convenience: `tlist_rev_app_distr` IS the anti-homomorphism law and `tlist_rev_involutive` IS involutivity, both at **Leibniz `=`** — so `fginv_involutive`, `fginv_compose`, `fginv_gen` and `sflip_involutive` are `=`, while the inverse LAWS (`fginv f ∘ f`) are `≈` only, pinned by a `Fail` probe. The UMP `free_groupoid_universal` bundles `Hobj` INSIDE the sigma (Presented.v's shape, forced: the generator clause is not well-typed without object agreement in scope — also pinned by a `Fail`), is STRICT (`hom_cast`/`Functor_StrictEq_Setoid`), and its real content is that the generator clause constrains only POSITIVE generators, the negative ones being FORCED through `ginv_unique_l` (`unique_neg`) rather than assumed; both round trips are proved. The free group is built DIRECTLY as an Instance/Grp.v `GrpObject` from the one-node word quotient — deliberately not through `vertex_group`, which yields Construction/Deloop.v's different `GrpObject`, the two in-tree bridges from `Instance/Grp.v`'s record to a `MonObject` (`Instance/Rep.v`'s `grp_mon` and this file's own `grp_deloop_monoid`, not convertible with each other, a measurement `Instance/Grp/Galois.v`'s header records (out of tree, pinned nowhere)) both dropping the inverse and so NOT supplying `Deloop_IsGroupoid`'s hypothesis. `FreeGrp : Sets ⟶ Grp` (the free group on a SETOID, since `Grp_Forget` lands in `Sets`) with `free_group_adjunction : FreeGrp ⊣ Grp_Forget` via the universal-arrow route, unit and counit NAMED and both triangles proved. Asymmetry measured, not glossed: the UNIT computes (`free_group_unit X a = fg_insert X a` by `eq_refl`, as do `FreeGrp X = FreeGrpObject X` and the three data fields of the underlying monoid) but the COUNIT does NOT — it routes through the `Qed`-opaque `ump_universal_arrows`, so what holds is agreement with word evaluation up to `≈` (`free_group_counit_evaluates`), and the negative is pinned in Test/ProbeFreeGroupoid.v (5 `Fail` probes against 7 positive controls, instrument sanity-checked, each negative stripped and confirmed to be a genuine conversion or typing failure). Non-vacuity PROVED at every point that matters: `free_signed_not_groupoid` (the quotient is NECESSARY — the free category on the signed quiver is not a groupoid once an edge exists), `free_signed_no_cancellation`, `fg_proj_not_faithful`, `loop_generator_not_identity`, and the two-generator witness `free_group_two_generators_nonabelian`/`_distinct` over `Deloop S3_Grp` — not a one-generator degeneracy. DEFERRED and disclosed: no category of groupoids in tree, so "free ⊣ forgetful for groupoids" is not statable and `FreeGroupoid` is not exhibited as a functor out of `QuiverCategory` (only the UMP plus both round trips); no reduced-word normal form, hence no word problem and no Nielsen–Schreier, every negative going through a concrete groupoid or a path-length invariant; extend/restrict respectfulness unproved, so the correspondence is not a bijection of types or of setoid quotients. #442 reaches a free-group left adjoint by the adjoint-functor-theorem route with no normal form — a genuinely different construction, cross-referenced; Mac Lane presents both deliberately
- **Construction/PROP/**: Free PROP on a signature, complete spine — shared Monoidal/Braided/Symmetric/Strict instances, bundled FreePROP, interpretation with universal property (Interp.v, Universal.v), models (Algebra.v), presented PROPs by generators and equations with factorization (Presentation.v, Presentation/Universal.v), Tietze/definitional extensions (Tietze.v); generic hom-congruence quotients in Construction/Quotient.v; skeletal FinSet with computable coproducts in Instance/FinSet.v
- **Construction/ColouredPROP/**: Coloured PROPs, complete — coloured signatures and terms over list-of-colour boundaries, free coloured PROP instantiating the class with definitional coherence, interpretation + universal property, presentations, colour base change with identity/composition functoriality laws up to hom_cast (pseudofunctor coherence cells not formalized), per-colour selective supplies (Fong–Spivak) with linear fan-out discipline, and the LNL bridge; generic comonoid tensor in Theory/Algebra/Comonoid/Tensor.v
- **Construction/Funny.v**: Funny tensor product C □ D — same objects as C × D, morphisms freely generated by one-sided steps with no interchange law; satellites in Construction/Funny/ (bifunctor, unitors, swap, associator, comparison to ×, unnatural-transformation hom ⟦-,-⟧, closed structure), symmetric monoidal instance on StrictCat in Instance/StrictCat/Funny.v
- **Construction/FAlg.v** / **Construction/FCoalg.v**: The categories of F-(co)algebras — `FAlg F` bundles carriers `∃ a, FAlgebra F a` with the `FAlgHom` commuting-square hom class; `FCoalg F := (FAlg (F^op))^op` by duality with covariant accessors and `FCoalg_Forget`. The initial-algebra / final-coalgebra theory rides these: the ω-chain `Chain F : Omega ⟶ C` (Construction/Chain.v) over the ordinal ω (Instance/Omega.v, a Type-valued `le_t` order avoiding stdlib `le`, with `Cochain` by duality); Lambek's lemma both ways (`lambek : F μ ≅ μ`, `lambek_final : ν ≅ F ν`, Theory/Lambek.v); catamorphism/anamorphism universal properties (`cata`/`ana` with commutes/unique/fusion, Theory/Recursion.v); Adámek's theorem over the honest `AdamekData` hypothesis (`adamek : AdamekData → Initial (FAlg F)`, Theory/Adamek.v — the `PreservesColimit → AdamekData` leg-agreement bridge is withheld, ledger 17); the `Cocomplete` corollary supplies the ambient colimit but STILL requires the `AdamekData` leg-agreement as a hypothesis, and no concrete `AdamekData` is constructed in-tree — the initial algebras below (list/nat/streams) are proved directly (the `NatF` note, Theory/Adamek/Corollaries.v); concrete witnesses `list A` as the initial `ListF`-algebra on Coq (Instance/Coq/Lists.v, axiom-free), `nat` as the initial `NatF`-algebra (Instance/Coq/Nat.v: `nat_initial` with primitive recursion as the mediator, `nat_lambek : 1 + nat ≅ nat`, the algebra-iso characterisation `nat_initial_unique`, the Riehl triples bridge `alg_of_triple`/`alg_of_triple_eta`, and the §2.4 representability reading — `Endos := FAlg Id[Coq]` with `nat_succ_represents` exhibiting `(nat, S)` as representing the forgetful functor with universal element `0`, and `nat_initial_via_universal_element` re-deriving initiality through the universal-element criterion), and streams as the final `StreamF`-coalgebra on Sets via bisimilarity (Instance/Sets/Streams.v)
- **Construction/Elements.v**: The category of elements of `K : D ⟶ Sets` — objects the pairs `(d, x)` with `x` an element of the setoid `K d`, morphisms the `D`-morphisms carrying the element (`fmap[K] f x ≈ y`), with the hom-setoid comparing only the underlying `D`-morphism, so faithfulness of `Elements_proj` is definitional (`Elements_proj_faithful_definitional` is the identity implication); the chosen lift `Elements_lift` with `Elements_lift_over` by reflexivity; the comma presentation `ElementsComma := =(1) ↓ K` related by the proved comparison `Elements_Comma : Elements K ≅[Cat] ElementsComma` (an isomorphism in Cat, hence an equivalence — one round trip is the identity on objects and morphisms, the other agrees pointwise up to `≈`), with projection compatibility as the identity-component natural isomorphism `Elements_proj_comma_iso`; the presheaf orientation `PElements P := (Elements P)^op` for `P : C^op ⟶ Sets`, whose hom shape `fmap[P] f x' ≈ x` holds by `reflexivity` and whose projection `PElements_proj` stays covariant into `C`. The header records why the Grothendieck/`IndexedCat` route is not used (`DiscreteCat`'s strict-equality homs versus setoid fibres).
- **Construction/Karoubi.v**: The Karoubi envelope — objects are idempotents, the identity of `(x, e)` is `e` itself; fully faithful embedding, every envelope idempotent splits (`SplitIdempotent` witnesses). Universal property in Karoubi/Universal.v: `IdempotentsSplit` (chosen splittings, satisfied by every envelope), `Karoubi_Extend` along the embedding with `Extend ∘ Embed ≈ G` and uniqueness up to `≈`, `CauchyComplete := IdempotentsSplit`, and split idempotents ⇒ the embedding is an equivalence (forward direction); Sets is Cauchy complete (Instance/Sets/Karoubi.v via the fixed-point sub-setoid)
- **Construction/Grothendieck.v**: Displayed categories, fibrations, and the Grothendieck construction — `Displayed C` (Theory/Displayed.v) as the primitive with `dtransport` and the interchange fields `dtransport_comp_l`/`dtransport_comp_r` (underivable from the base laws by a 2-cocycle countermodel in the header, and load-bearing — without them the total category's composition is not a congruence); the total category `Total`/`Total_Proj` (Construction/Displayed/Total.v); fibrations in both presentations (Theory/Fibration.v: `DCartesian`, `Cleaving`, `CartesianMorphism`, `ClovenFibration`, `SplitCleaving`, opfibrations by `Displayed_op`); the coherent pseudofunctor-lite `IndexedCat` (Construction/Indexed.v, with an honesty note on why a bare `Functor B Cat` is insufficient in a setoid library — a central-automorphism twist of `fmap_comp` breaks the cross-application cocycle); the Grothendieck construction `Grothendieck_Displayed`/`Grothendieck`/`Grothendieck_Proj` with every displayed law discharged from the `IndexedCat` coherence pack; the projection is a split opfibration (Construction/Grothendieck/Fibration.v); fibre categories and the fibre equivalence `fiber_grothendieck_equiv` (Construction/Grothendieck/Fiber.v); constructors from strict functors under fibrewise UIP with an inline axiom-free Hedberg (Construction/Grothendieck/Strict.v: `IndexedCat_of_StrictFunctor`, `Constant_IndexedCat`, `Grothendieck_Constant_iso : Grothendieck (Constant_IndexedCat B D) ≅[Cat] B ∏ D` — note that `≅[Cat]` is an EQUIVALENCE of categories, not an isomorphism, since Cat's hom-setoid is `Functor_Setoid` (Instance/Cat.v), so the name reads stronger than the statement); the fibred-to-indexed round-trip `IndexedCat_of_SplitCleaving` with `RoundTrip_Comparison` fully faithful and the full `RoundTrip_Equivalence` (Construction/Grothendieck/RoundTrip.v, over a split opfibration of P — the split laws are provably inert, so a cloven cleaving already suffices); and the codomain displayed category with cartesian-lifts-iff-pullbacks `codomain_cleaving`/`codomain_cleaving_pullbacks` (Construction/Displayed/Codomain.v)
- **Construction/Enriched/Natural.v**: The enriched upgrade over Construction/Enriched.v — V-natural transformations `EnrichedTransform` with the unitor-conjugated V-naturality square `enaturality` (typed-equality `<< A ~~> B >>` form) and componentwise setoid; identity/composite enriched functors and both whiskerings (Construction/Enriched/Compose.v); the ordinary category of V-functors `Enriched_Fun` with vertical composition by `ecompose` under the inverse unitor at I (Construction/Enriched/Fun.v — genuine V-category hom-OBJECTS need ends in the base, ledger 11); V=Sets recovers ordinary category theory at all three levels, the transformation level being `EnrichedTransform_is_Transform` over the two in-tree `Defined` round trips (Construction/Enriched/Sets.v); enrichment in the walking arrow = preorders both ways with enriched functors = monotone maps (`Enriched_Two_preorder`, `EnrichedFunctor_Two_monotone`, Construction/Enriched/Two.v, over Instance/Two/Monoidal.v's cartesian `Two_Monoidal`). Sets-weighted (co)limits by representability with `HomDiagram`/`WeightedLimit`/`WeightedColimit` and the conical case `conical_weighted` proved in both directions (Structure/Limit/Weighted.v; V-valued weights need ends, ledger 11)

### Concrete Instances
- **Instance/Ab.v**: `Ab`, the category of abelian groups (Mac Lane §I.7) — `AbObject` extends `Instance/CMon.v`'s `CMonObject` by a negation (`ab_cmon :> CMonObject`), so carrier and the four monoid laws are inherited; `AbHom` IS `CMonHom`, making preservation of negation the theorem `ab_map_neg` rather than a field. `Ab_Forget : Ab ⟶ Sets`; `Ab_Zero` from the one-element group. Mac Lane's §I.7 proposition in both halves: `ab_monic_iff_injective` probing with `AbKernel`, and `ab_epic_iff_surjective` probing with the quotient `AbQuotient` = B/fA. The quotient probe is available here for ONE reason — commutativity: `ab_coset_eq` is a congruence only because `f a` can be commuted past another element (`cmon_plus_comm` in `AbQuotient`'s `cmon_plus_respects`), which is what makes B/fA an OBJECT of `Ab` rather than a bare setoid. No comparative claim is made against `Sets`: `Instance/Sets.v:509` now proves `surjectivity_is_epic` outright via a cokernel-pair setoid — the same kind of quotient probe — so both categories have the result and both get it the same way. NOTE the monic half is NOT special to `Ab` — monic ⟹ injective already holds in `CMon`, probing with `nat`; the kernel is a convenient probe, not a necessary one. SCOPE: `Preadditive Ab` and a forgetful `Ab ⟶ Grp` are NOT delivered, and this file instantiates none of `Preadditive`/`Additive`/`Abelian`. Erratum in #256: `Instance/Comp.v` DOES have group inverses (`inv` :288, `inv_left`/`inv_right` :322-323, `Group` :382, `Bool` witness :405); it is unusable as a donor only because it is a Leibniz-`=` development invoking `functional_extensionality` (:370, :375).
- **Instance/Ab/DirectedColimit.v**: Mac Lane §III.3 Exercise 7 (book p. 68) — an abelian group IS the colimit of its finitely generated subgroups, delivered at CONE level: **`ab_fg_colimit A : IsColimitCocone (FGCocone A)`** (`Structure/Limit/Preservation.v:710`), so every competing cocone factors uniquely through THESE legs; the apex-only `ab_fg_isacolimit : IsAColimit (FGDiagram A) A` is derived through `colimitcocone_isacolimit` and is the WEAKER reading (its legs are unconstrained) — no separation between the two is proved, so "strictly" is not claimed, with `ab_fg_Colimit` the packaged form. **`AbSubgroup` is new here, and the survey behind that was CORRECTED by audit**: `Instance/Grp/Quotient.v:156`'s `Subgroup` is over `GrpObject` and `Instance/Mod/Quotient.v:211`'s `Submodule` over `RModObject`, so neither transfers to `AbObject` — but a THIRD record does exist over the right base type and an earlier draft missed it: `Instance/Ab/Character/Finite.v:624`'s `Record Subgroup (G : AbObject)` has exactly `AbSubgroup`'s five fields PLUS `sg_dec`, demanding DECIDABLE membership, and carries its own `Generated` (`:835`). The real ground for not reusing it is `sg_dec` — membership here is the inductive `InGen`, which nothing constructively decides — not the false ground first given (that no in-tree record is over `AbObject`). The miss was avoidable: the donor header this file cites names that record twice, four lines above the cited line. `Instance/Mod/Quotient.v:136` asks for exactly this interface IN `Instance/Ab.v`; it is declared here instead and that placement is disclosed, not silently taken. **`Proset` CANNOT HOST *THIS* INDEX — AND AN EARLIER DRAFT OVERSTATED THAT INTO AN IMPOSSIBILITY, WHICH A COMPILING COUNTER-DEVELOPMENT REFUTES**: `Proset` takes a stdlib `relation A` = `A → A → Prop` while `FGHom` is `Type`-valued *because `absub_mem` is*, so `Fail Check (FGHom ab_Z : relation (FGObj ab_Z))` is a genuine universe inconsistency against a PASSING control at the `inhabited`-squashed variant — that measurement is real and pinned. What it does NOT show is that #330's suggested `Proset` route is impossible: the argument covered only SQUASHING a Type-valued hom, never making MEMBERSHIP itself Prop-valued, and under that variant `FGHom` is natively a `relation`, nothing is squashed, `fmap` merely applies a `Prop → Prop` function into a `sigT`, and the colimit goes through (audit-built, closed under the global context; the transport and the `Proset` application independently re-checked). So the honest claim is *impossible GIVEN this file's Type-valued membership convention* — a design choice following `Instance/Grp/Quotient.v`, not a necessity. The index is built DIRECTLY, COPYING (not reusing — the file never `Require`s it) `Instance/Proset.v:41`'s trivially-true hom-setoid, with `FGSub_Thin` inhabiting `Structure/Thin.v`'s `Thin` rather than a lookalike, though that lemma is consumed nowhere. Directedness — the exercise's word — is `FGSub_directed : ∀ X Y, {Z & hom X Z * hom Y Z}`, the join presented by `++` on generator lists. The engine is `gen_least` (the file's ONLY induction over `InGen`), and `InGen` follows `Instance/Grp/Abelianization.v`'s `InCommutator` shape, its `ig_resp` constructor being what makes the generated set ≈-saturated. **SCOPE, STATED RATHER THAN GLOSSED**: index objects carry a `FinGen` PRESENTATION, so two presentations of ONE subgroup are DISTINCT objects — a deviation from the literal "poset of subgroups", harmless because the category is thin (`Ztwo_dup_iso` exhibits such a pair as isomorphic) and unavoidable because truncating the presentation would block extracting the generators that BOTH directedness and the mediator consume; the two presentations are NOT proved unequal, and no comparison with a poset-indexed diagram is formalized. `fg_med_commutes` is `≈` and NOT Leibniz, the cause being the design rather than an accident — the mediator evaluates the competing cocone at the cyclic object `cyc a` while the triangle's other side evaluates at `X`, different objects of the index — with seven other identifications holding at `eq_refl` and shipped as `Example`s. Non-vacuity over `ab_Z` (REUSED from `Instance/Ab/Coproduct.v:264`, no new group): `Ztwo_proper`/`Zthree_proper` (1 ∉ 2ℤ, 1 ∉ 3ℤ, so the subgroups are PROPER), **`Zsub_incomparable`** — no morphism EITHER WAY between 2ℤ and 3ℤ, so the index is genuinely not a chain — and `Zjoin_strictly_larger`, the join containing 1 = 3 + (−2) that neither factor does. 108/108 constants closed under the global context — the audit reconciled three counts: 101 is what `Print Module` prints, 106 adds the five `InGen` constructors the glob records separately, and 108 further adds `Build_AbSubgroup`/`Build_FinGen`, which none of those sources lists; so the earlier 106 was an UNDERCOUNT, not an overcount. Of these, EIGHT are in the permanent `make print-assumptions` gate; the rest were verified once. `Test/ProbeDirectedColimit330.v` pins the boundary with 2 negatives of DIFFERENT KINDS kept lexically apart — one CONVERSION (the triangle at Leibniz) and one FORMABILITY (the `Proset` universe inconsistency) — plus a separate instrument check, each stripped and its kind confirmed, rename-simulated at 7/7 over the constants in the NEGATIVES. NOT delivered: no `Filtered` class and no general filtered/directed-colimit theory (there is NONE in tree — `FGSub_directed` is a lemma about one concrete index, not an instance of a class); no `Cocomplete Ab` and no other colimit in `Ab`; no analogue for non-abelian groups or modules; and `AbSubgroup` is not placed in `Instance/Ab.v`, so `Instance/Mod/Quotient.v`'s request is acknowledged rather than discharged
- **Instance/Ab/TorsionFree.v**: Mac Lane §IV.3 Exercise 2 (book p. 92, `maclane:IV.3:ex2`) — **the torsion-free abelian groups form a full reflective subcategory of `Ab`**, delivered as the RECORD `TorsionFree_Reflective : Reflective TorsionFree_Sub` (`Construction/Reflective.v:60`'s three fields: `TorsionFree_Full`, `TorsionFree_reflector`, `TorsionFree_adj`), which at `9a1fe0f2` is the tree's FIRST `Reflective` built at a named concrete category — measured: `rg -n 'Build_Reflective' --glob '*.v'` returns exactly one prior hit, `Construction/Reflective/Idempotent.v:346`, over an abstract category with an idempotent monad, every other `Reflective`-typed term being a hypothesis. **THE ISSUE'S "Current state" IS STALE**: it says there is no `Ab` and that "torsion" has zero hits; `Ab` is `Instance/Ab.v:201` (#256) and the seven case-insensitive hits at `9a1fe0f2` (excluding the new file and its probe) are five lines of prose, the lemma `ZZ_no_2_torsion` (`Instance/Ab/Character/NonNatural.v:416`) and its one use — a statement about one group at one exponent, not a predicate. Torsion is `torsion_mem A a := { k : nat & (0 < k) * (nat_smul A k a ≈ 0) }` with the EXPONENT AS DATA, so two exponents are multiplied when torsion is closed under addition and read back when the quotient is proved torsion-free, and no choice principle appears anywhere; the torsion subgroup is `Instance/Ab/DirectedColimit.v:273`'s `AbSubgroup` REUSED (`TorsionSub`, `TorsionAb := AbSubgroupAb`, `torsion_incl := absub_incl`) — not a third subgroup record, the other one in tree (`Instance/Ab/Character/Finite.v:624`'s `Subgroup`) demanding DECIDABLE membership that an exponent found rather than decided cannot supply — and A/T(A) is `Instance/Ab.v:472`'s pre-existing image quotient applied to that inclusion (`AbModTorsion A := AbQuotient (torsion_incl A)`), so NO new quotient machinery and no coset object anywhere. - **Instance/Grp/Epi.v**: epimorphisms of groups are surjective (Mac Lane §I.5 ex. 5). The entire constructive content is the unconditional, axiom-free density theorem `grp_epic_image_dense` (`Epic f → ∀ h, ¬¬ GrpImage f h`), reached through the CONTRAPOSITIVE WITH AN EXPLICIT WITNESS `grp_not_epic_of_witness` — the classical "choose an element outside the image" is `¬∀ → ∃¬` and is not available — with `grp_surjective_is_epic` for the easy direction. The classical biconditional `grp_epic_iff_surjective` is exactly the double-negation elimination of density, and NO separate "cost" can be isolated: under `Epic f` the named hypothesis `GrpImageStable` is EQUIVALENT to the conclusion (`stability_is_the_conclusion`, `surjective_gives_stable`), and so is the strictly weaker `DenseImpliesSurjective` (`weaker_hypothesis_suffices`, `stable_implies_weaker`, `weaker_is_the_conclusion`) — the conclusion is the weakest hypothesis that completes the proof. The design point is a theorem rather than an assertion: `transposition_decides_image` shows that ANY permutation of `Grp_Coset f ⊔ pt` satisfying only HALF the textbook transposition's specification (image cosets ↦ the adjoined point) already yields full `GrpImageDecidable f`, by matching on the coproduct constructor with no decision procedure — so the transposition is replaced by a twist on the `¬¬`-stable power set `GrpCosetPower` that complements at the base coset without deciding which case obtains, involutive precisely because the values are stable. Machinery: the coset space of an image and `SymGrp`, the symmetric group of a setoid (permutations as invertible setoid maps, the inverse being data, so no choice). Two deliberately non-interchangeable witnesses: `grp_two_incl` (Z/2 into Z/2 × Z/2) exercises the outside-the-image half, but its image is normal and the equivariance step degenerates there — `grp_two_incl_image_acts_trivially` proves the whole image acts as the IDENTITY; `grp_two_sym3` (Z/2 into the symmetric group of a three-letter setoid, as a transposition subgroup — built as `SymGrp`, so associativity is composition's) has a NON-NORMAL image, with `grp_two_sym3_conj_outside`, `grp_two_sym3_moves_a_coset` and `grp_two_sym3_image_acts_nontrivially` exhibiting a concrete stable subset that an element OF the image moves. `Epic` and `Monic` are incomparable in Grp, both halves witnessed (`grp_two_epic_monic_incomparable`). Mac Lane's own hint (2nd ed., printed p. 21) instead permutes the SET H, with three cosets M, Mu, Mv and a separate index-2 case; the `Perm(H/M ⊔ pt)` form built here is the one issue #251 specifies
- **Instance/Grp.v**: Grp, the category of groups over `Sets` (Mac Lane §I.6; Riehl §1.4) — the catalog's most-depended-on Chapter I gap, mirroring `Instance/CMon.v`. `GrpObject` (setoid carrier, unit, multiplication, inverse; the right unit/inverse laws and inverse-respectfulness DERIVED from the left ones, so the record carries only what a group genuinely needs), `GrpHom` preserving unit and multiplication with inverse preservation derived (`grp_map_inv`), the category `Grp`, and faithful `Grp_Forget : Grp ⟶ Sets`. One-element group as `Grp_Zero : ZeroObject Grp` (initial and terminal coincide on the nose, `zero_coincide := iso_id`); componentwise `Grp_Cartesian`. `Grp_injectivity_is_monic` is a genuine biconditional — the monic→injective direction probes f with its KERNEL inclusion against the trivial map, no ℤ needed. Reconciliation both ways with `Structure/Group.v`'s internal `GroupObject` at `Sets`' cartesian monoidal structure (`Grp_GroupObject`/`GroupObject_GrpObject`). Riehl §1.4: the opposite-group endofunctor `Grp_Op` with `Grp_op_Isomorphism : Id[Grp] ≅ Grp_Op`, whose component is inversion — an anti-homomorphism, which is exactly why it is a homomorphism into the opposite group. `Z2` on `bool` is the nontrivial witness (audit-supplied): `Monic_in_Grp_is_not_vacuous` and the kernel's non-degeneracy demonstrate the machinery beyond the one-element group, and the `rt_back_*` examples record the second reconciliation round trip
- **Instance/EnsV.v**: Mac Lane's Ens_V (§I.2) — the category of sets within a set V, as codes with decoding: the general `Spanned C V El` (objects codes `V`, homs ALL C-morphisms between decoded objects) instantiated as `EnsV El := Spanned Coq` and `EnsV_Sets El := Spanned Sets`, each with its inclusion functor. The recovery at the tautological family is **definitional** and general: `Spanned_recovers_ambient : @Spanned C (@obj C) (λ x, x) = C` for *every* C by `eq_refl` (this is where primitive-projection eta is genuinely load-bearing — C is a variable, so no delta is available), with `EnsV_recovers_Coq`/`EnsV_Sets_recovers_Sets` its instances (each also checkable by delta alone, their ambients being transparent record constants) — measured, not asserted. Distinct from `Instance/Ens.v`'s `Ens`/`EnsT`, which deliberately constrain morphisms to preserve and reflect membership; both are kept, cross-linked
- **Instance/Simplex.v**: The simplicial category Δ (Mac Lane §I.2) — objects `nat`, morphisms monotone maps `Fin.t m → Fin.t n` (function + monotonicity witness, hom-setoid comparing the function pointwise), the algebraist's Δ including the empty ordinal. The wide inclusion `Simplex_FinSet : Simplex ⟶ FinSet` is proved faithful AND proved non-full — `no_monotone_swap`/`Simplex_FinSet_not_Full` exhibit the swap on `Fin.t 2` as a FinSet map hit by no monotone map. Face and degeneracy generators `sface`/`sdegen` with computing examples, and ALL FIVE simplicial identities proven (`simplicial_face_face`, `simplicial_degen_degen`, and `simplicial_degen_face` in its lt/eq/succ/gt cases). Named `Simplex` because `Δ` in this tree is already the diagonal functor (`Functor/Diagonal.v:57`, notations `Δ[J](c)`/`Δ(c)`), with lowercase `delta` also taken by comonoid comultiplication
- **Instance/Matr.v**: Mac Lane §I.2's matrix category, stated over a RIG per the issue's QA corrections (the category laws never subtract): `Matr (R : RigObject)` with objects the naturals and an arrow `n ~> m` an m × n matrix `Fin.t m → Fin.t n → carrier R` (Mac Lane's orientation, documented), composition the matrix product, identity the Kronecker `delta` (spending `Fin.eq_dec`), morphism equality entrywise. The engine is a reusable rig-parameterized finite-sum library — `fin_sum` with congruence, additivity, exchange (`fin_sum_swap`) spending only the additive half (`rig_cmon`; a monoid-level refounding is disclosed-deferred), bilinearity (`fin_sum_mul_l/r`, where distributivity and annihilation are spent), and the Kronecker collapses (`fin_sum_delta_l/r`) — kept general for the GL_n/determinant issue. `Matr_N := Matr Nat_Rig` is Awodey §1.4's category of ℕ-matrices with computing `eq_refl` examples. The transpose self-duality (Mac Lane §II.3 Ex 4, Riehl 1.3.14(v)): for a COMMUTATIVE rig — exactly where (AB)ᵀ = BᵀAᵀ spends commutation — `Matr_Transpose` is an identity-on-objects functor to the opposite, involutive on the nose, packaged as the isomorphism of categories `Matr_transpose_iso : Matr R ≅[Cat] (Matr R)^op`, instantiated at ℕ (`Matr_N_transpose_iso`) and at Mac Lane's own commutative-ring headline ℤ (`Matr_Z`, `Matr_Z_transpose_iso`), with the one-element-rig collapse sanity (`Matr_zero_collapse`); over a non-commutative rig the transpose lands in matrices over the opposite rig, noted and deferred. Instance/Matr/Yoneda.v is Riehl Corollary 2.2.10 made categorical: a row operation IS a natural endomorphism of the represented presheaf `[Hom ─,n]` (naturality = commuting with right multiplication, both directions stated), and `row_operations_are_left_multiplication` derives — purely from `Yoneda_Embedding`'s round trips, no entry ever inspected — that every row operation is left multiplication by the unique matrix obtained by applying it to the identity (`representing_matrix_at_id`, `representing_matrix_unique`), with the two-row swap as the computing witness (its representing matrix is the permutation matrix by `eq_refl` over ℕ)
- **Instance/Matr/Elimination.v**, **Instance/Matr/Coequalizer.v**: Mac Lane §III.3 Exercise 3 — coequalizers in the matrix category, delivered at FULL strength: **`Matr_HasCoequalizers`** inhabits `HasCoequalizers (Matr K)` for EVERY parallel pair over ANY field with a zero decider, built on a genuine Gaussian-elimination engine rather than an interface. `Instance/Matr/Elimination.v` is that engine: `LeftNullBasis` (`lnb_dim`/`lnb_basis`/`lnb_annih`/`lnb_univ`) with `left_null_basis` inhabited for every matrix, by recursion on COLUMNS (pivot search, then the basis `e_{d r} − (c_{d r}/c_p)·e_p` indexed through `Determinant.v`'s `fin_delete`), plus the pre-shaped `left_null_basis_diff` and the subtraction kit `mat_sub`/`mat_mul_sub_r`/`mat_sub_zero`/`mat_mul_sub_zero_iff`. **THE DECIDABILITY HYPOTHESIS IS FORCED RATHER THAN CHOSEN — argued, not proved**: `FieldObject` (Instance/FdVect.v:167) carries NO decider — `finv` is TOTAL and `finv_l` is GUARDED by `x ≉ 0` — and elimination must DECIDE whether a pivot is zero, so a zero-decider is carried as explicit data on the `Instance/Field.v:300` `field_dec_stable` precedent. Correspondingly **a rig will not do**: `Matr R` is over a `RigObject`, which has no subtraction, so the cokernel-of-a-difference framing needs the RING layer's `ring_neg` and nothing here applies to `Matr_N`. **The `Qed`/`Defined` distinction is load-bearing twice over, and both were found by REFUTED probes rather than anticipated.** `Instance/Field.v`'s `F2_Field_dec` (:534) and `Q_Field_dec` (:410) are `Qed` lemmas: they inhabit the hypothesis perfectly and the engine is total and correct when fed them, but nothing REDUCES through an opaque constant, so the coequalizer object does not compute — transparent copies (`f2_dec`, `q_dec`) are used for every `eq_refl` witness, and `Instance/Field.v` is deliberately NOT modified. Dually, keeping `matr_coeq_desc` a transparent `Definition` rather than a `Qed` lemma is what keeps **the MEDIATOR computing** (`unique_obj (coeq_desc …) F1 F1 = true` by `eq_refl`). The stage handoff is by PURE CONVERSION — all twelve section constants of Coequalizer.v are `:=` bodies with no tactic, no `change` and no transport, consuming `Matr K`'s own `∘` and `≈[Matr K]`; the only friction was imports, `Structure/Coequalizer.v` not re-exporting `Coequalizer`/`APair`/`^op`. Orientation pinned by `eq_refl` (`matrix_is_hom`, `mat_mul_is_compose` with object order (c,b,a), `mat_id_is_id`, `mat_equiv_is_homset_equiv`) — and read the `#[local] Instance Matrix_Setoid` correctly: an earlier draft of this bullet said that last Example NEEDS it, which an audit REFUTED by experiment. The two setoids are CONVERTIBLE, so the Examples close by `eq_refl` either way and deleting the instance outright leaves the file compiling; what it buys is that the two `≈`s are the same TERM rather than merely convertible ones. **Non-vacuity is proved four ways, not asserted**: over F₂ with `A = (1,0)ᵀ`, `B = (0,1)ᵀ` the object is `k = 1` at `m = 2` — neither 0 nor m — with `coeq_f2_obj_not_zero`/`_not_two` by `discriminate` off the computation, `coeq_f2_pair_distinct`, and the sharpest, `coeq_f2_map_not_monic` paired with `coeq_f2_map_epic`: the coequalizing map identifies two provably inequivalent arrows, so it is a PROPER QUOTIENT and not an isomorphism in disguise. A second non-degenerate point `k = 2` at `m = 3`, degenerate controls at `k = m` and `k = 0`, and a ℚ witness where `finv` actually divides (`E = (−2, 1)`). Also `matr_coeq_epic`, `matr_coeq_iso`, `matr_coeq_colimit`, and `Matr_op_HasEqualizers` reading the result through #326's `IsEqualizer_op_of_IsCoequalizer`. SCOPE: **no matrix rank function exists in either of these two files** (the tree has a `rank` and seven `*_rank` constants elsewhere, none of them a matrix rank), so `k = m − rank M` is NOT claimed; `k`'s independence of the construction is NOT claimed as a NUMBER (`matr_coeq_iso` gives only `k ≅ k'` in `Matr K`); no echelon or normal form (the three refuted map-normalization probes are three faces of that); no right null space, so **equalizers in `Matr K` itself are NOT delivered** — only `HasEqualizers ((Matr K)^op)`, which the header labels a reread rather than a new theorem; and nothing applies over a bare rig. `Matr_HasCoequalizers` is a plain `Definition`, NOT a registered `Instance`, since resolution can never produce a decider for an abstract field. `Test/ProbeMatrCoeq.v` guards the boundary with 7 conversion negatives plus an instrument check, rename-simulated over the constants appearing in the NEGATIVES — a check that found FIVE unguarded on its first run, and which an independent audit re-ran at 19/19 (my own pass covered 18 names; the audit added one more)

All 35 NAMED constants closed under the global context (47 with the twelve Program obligations, each measured — the house's FunExercises.v convention); universes measured per named constant, with the `Set` instances on six of them attributed to their donors (`ZtoQ`, `rng_from_Z`), the hom/proof identification to `Rng`'s own shape, and the three-parameter collapse at either isomorphism to the cost of stating an `Isomorphism` in ANY category of categories — it is already present at the STRICT form, so `StrictCat` does not keep those universes apart. Deferred and disclosed: the module-theoretic definition via `RModObject` (which this coslice definition sidesteps — no equivalence with a module category is claimed), and smallness. **The non-commutative deferral this file's header names — "there the structure map is required to land in the CENTRE … No centre-valued variant is built here" — is DISCHARGED** by `Instance/Rng/Algebras/Associative.v`'s `AAlg K`, whose `aalg_central` field IS that condition
- **Instance/Rng/Frac.v**: Mac Lane §I.3 Exercise 1 — the field of quotients as a functor, with the exercise's real content being the DOMAIN-CATEGORY CHOICE: a hom sending a nonzero d to 0 supports no map on fractions, so `IntDom` takes integral domains with INJECTIVE ring homs (`DomHom`), the standard monomorphisms fix — injective ⇒ monic in Rng is `rng_injective_monic`, machine-checked per-hom here as `DomHom_monic`, the converse now proved as `rng_monic_injective` (Instance/Rng/Polynomial.v), so injectivity being carried as data is a design choice rather than a necessity. `DomObject` is constructive: commutativity + 1 ≉ 0 + CANCELLATION as the defining field (exactly what the construction consumes; the classical no-zero-divisors disjunction gives it via the `DomObject_of_no_zero_divisors` smart constructor, and `dom_mul_nonzero` — nonzero elements multiply to nonzero — follows from cancellation alone; the reverse implication would need double-negation elimination, so cancellation is primitive). `FracRing D`: pairs (n, d ≉ 0) under cross-multiplication `frac_eq` (transitivity IS cancellation), full commutative-ring laws by deterministic transitivity chains over a shuffle kit, the fraction pairs built through the explicit-`existT` smart constructor `mk_frac` (the 8.19/8.20-safe form; nix builds for 9.1, 8.19, and 8.20 all run locally); `Frac : IntDom ⟶ CRng` acts componentwise; `IntDom_Incl : IntDom ⟶ CRng` with faithfulness proved (`IntDom_Incl_Faithful`); non-fullness is expected (ℤ → ℤ/2ℤ) but left unstated — no quotient rings in tree yet for the witness. Field property constructively: `frac_recip`/`frac_nonzero_recip` (numerator apart from zero ⇒ reciprocal, apartness from the zero fraction = numerator apartness); embedding `frac_embed` n ↦ n/1 injective — the universal-arrow unit, full UP awaiting a Fields category. The Lie-algebra half of the exercise is descoped in the header (smooth manifolds out of scope). Witness ℤ (`Int_Dom` via `Z.mul_cancel_r`): 1/2 + 1/3 ≈ 5/6 computes by `eq_refl` (cross-multiplication 30 = 30)
- **Instance/Rng/Polynomial.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 Exercise 7 + Awodey §9.3 Example 9.10 + Riehl §2.1 Example 2.1.5(iv,v) and §2.3 Example 2.3.4 — the polynomial ring as a universal arrow, with `Instance/Rng/Pointed.v` and `Instance/Mod/Representable.v`. `PolyRing K` is built by generators and relations (the Instance/Ab/Tensor.v idiom) over any `RingObject`, with `poly_extend`/`poly_extend_unique`, and Ex 7 lands directly in the PRE-EXISTING `Instance/Rng/Algebras.v` — `poly_universal_element : AUniversalElement (KAlg_Forget K) (PolyAlg K)`, with `poly_representation`, `poly_representable` and both Arrow encodings, so no coslice needed building. **`Instance/Rng.v`'s standing deferral is DISCHARGED**: `rng_monic_injective` and the biconditional `rng_monic_iff_injective` are proved axiom-free, two elements of R being separated by the two homomorphisms out of ℤ[x] sending x to them — and it holds over ALL of `Rng`, not merely `CRng`, because `zring_central` (the integers are central in every unital ring) discharges the commutation for an arbitrary possibly-non-commutative target. That deferral was cited in four `.v` headers (`Instance/Rng.v`, `Instance/Field.v`, `Instance/Rng/Frac.v`, `Instance/Field/Frac.v`) and in two bullets of this file, and all six are corrected; the two `Frac` files keep injectivity as DATA in `DomHom` and only the stated REASON changed — it is now a design choice rather than a necessity. **Awodey's adjunction is stated over COMMUTATIVE rings and the restriction is PROVED rather than assumed**: `poly_hom_value_central` shows x is central in K[x], so its image under any homomorphism commutes with the image of the coefficients, which is exactly why `Rng` will not do — the right object there is a free algebra on a non-commuting generator, named in the header and not attempted. `CRngPt := Elements CRng_Forget` gets pointed objects for free as the category of elements (no new category machinery), with `poly_pointed_adjunction : PolyPointed ⊣ CRngPt_Forget` and evaluation as the transpose in two forms. Riehl's representability boxes are delivered on BOTH sides as natural isomorphisms — `rmod_representation : [Hom R,─] ≅ RMod_Forget R` in `[RMod R, Sets]`, its representing object `Ring_RMod R` on the nose and proved directly rather than through formal sums, and `zpoly_representation : [Hom ℤ[x],─] ≅ Rng_Forget` in `[Rng, Sets]` — with `zpoly_universal_element`'s `aue_elem = poly_x` by `eq_refl` naming the indeterminate as a first-class universal element, and `free_one_generator_iso : FreeModObject SetsOne ≅ Ring_RMod R` WITH its uniqueness clause tying the new representation to #305's free module. Strengths measured strict-first, and **two strict attempts were made and REFUTED**, both pinned as `Fail` probes: preservation of ZERO and ONE by `poly_extend` is NOT definitional (`rig_zero (PolyRing K)` is `pt_const (rig_zero K)`, so the fold returns `phi (rig_zero K)` and closing needs `phi`'s own law) — and the probe is stated at a VARIABLE ring precisely because at ℤ with the initial map it DOES close by `eq_refl`, the counter-behaviour recorded beside it; and `poly_pointed_unit A = poly_pointed_arrow A` is rejected, only the pointwise action being `eq_refl`, which corrected a header sentence claiming the unit IS the coefficient inclusion. What does close strictly: `poly_extend_const`/`poly_extend_x`, preservation of + and ·, and evaluation COMPUTING over ℤ (x²+2x+1 at 3 = 16, at 0 = 1, at −1 = 0). Non-vacuity is general rather than ℤ-only — `poly_const_injective` (K ↪ K[x] for every commutative K) and `poly_x_not_constant` (x is no constant whenever 1 ≉ 0) are proved by evaluating in K itself, so neither needs a normal form. With this, Mac Lane's §III.1 roster of free constructions is COMPLETE in tree, recorded by path rather than issue number: free category (`Construction/Free/Quiver.v:529` — note `:518` is `UnitQuiverCatAdjunction`, an error the issue itself carried), free monoid (`Instance/Coq/Monoid/Free.v:297`), free group (`Instance/Grp/Free.v:405`), free module (`Instance/Mod/Free.v:487`), polynomial ring here. Deferred and disclosed: no degree, no normal form, no coefficient uniqueness, hence no equality decision procedure and no division algorithm; no multivariate K[x,y]; no proof that K[x] is a domain when K is, so no link to `IntDom`; and the kernel of `pt_const` over a non-commutative K is NOT claimed to be precisely the commutator ideal — only the forcing direction is available, and it is immediate from the two constructors rather than stated as a lemma; the converse was cut back when it could not be proved
- **Instance/Field/Frac.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 construction 2 — the field of quotients as a UNIVERSAL ARROW, together with his cautionary non-example, over `Instance/Rng/Frac.v`'s `IntDom`/`FracRing`/`frac_embed` and #971's `Instance/Field.v` (nothing there is rebuilt). **The universal property `frac_ump` is UNCONDITIONAL** — verified at the type: arbitrary domain, arbitrary field, no stability, no decidability, no field structure on the fractions, injectivity spent in exactly one lemma (`frac_hom_den_nonzero`), which is precisely why Mac Lane's domain category has monomorphisms only. **The forgetful functor is where the constructive content sits, and it is NOT unconditional**: a field homomorphism is not constructively injective by any route the tree has, and #971 had already measured why — `field_hom_distinct` (`a ≉ b → f a ≉ f b`) is the unconditional strength and its contrapositive yields only `¬¬(a ≈ b)`, while the strict form is `field_stable_at_zero_suffices`, which consumes ¬¬-stability. Since `DomHom` demands the strict form AS DATA, the functor is delivered in two shapes rather than narrowed silently: `StableField_IntDom : StableField ⟶ IntDom` hypothesis-free over the full subcategory cut by `FieldStableAtZero`, and `Field_IntDom : (∀ F, FieldStableAtZero F) → Field ⟶ IntDom` with the blanket hypothesis explicit (`field_every_monic`'s shape). **`Field_Dom : Field ⟶ Dom` is unconditional, and that asymmetry is the point** — it is proved, not asserted. Necessity of the hypothesis is NOT claimed; whether some other construction gives an unconditional FORGETFUL `Field ⟶ IntDom` is left open in #971's own words (unconditional functors of that type trivially exist — a constant one, for instance — so the open question is about the forgetful one specifically). **The non-example needs no quotient rings and no second prime**: `ZtoF2 := Z.odd : Int_Ring ~{Rng}~> F2_Ring`, where `Z.odd_add`/`Z.odd_mul` ARE the two homomorphism clauses against F₂'s `xorb`/`andb`, and `no_field_over_Q_and_F2` refutes a common field with maps to both ℚ and F₂ — with NO case split on characteristic, since a hom `K → ℚ` delivers `1+1 ≉ 0` in K outright and `field_hom_nonzero` then refutes `K → F₂`. So Mac Lane's ℤ/pℤ family is not needed; #971's own ℚ-vs-F₂ pair separates. Stronger than the issue asked: `no_DomHom_Z_F2` (`IntDom` has NO morphism ℤ → F₂ at all) gives `IntDom_Dom_not_Full` for the new inclusion, and — on the SAME witness — `IntDom_Incl_not_Full` for `IntDom_Incl : IntDom ⟶ CRng`, which is the different functor `Instance/Rng/Frac.v` actually left open, having deferred it for want of a counterexample OBJECT. No quotient ring is needed for that object after all: F₂ is a field, hence a domain. The refutation consumes only the EXISTENCE of the factorizations — uniqueness and the arrow ℤ → K are never used (`no_field_maps_to_all_fields`) — and is stated over BOTH field categories so it is not an artifact of `Field` being the larger one; `frac_universal_over_monos_not_over_all` packages the contrast. Two hypotheses, both disclosed and neither Mac Lane's: ¬¬-stability above, and `DomZeroDec`, needed ONLY to name Q(D) as an OBJECT because `FieldObject`'s `finv` is TOTAL and a total reciprocal must decide what to return at zero (stated at zero in `FieldStableAtZero`'s located style). Strict-first measurement: `StableField_IntDom_Dom_obj`/`_map` and `Field_IntDom_agrees_obj` close by `eq_refl` (the `_map` one was first written at `≈`, then measured strict and upgraded), and the extension of ℤ ↪ ℚ carries 1/2 to `1 # 2` at LEIBNIZ equality; the one strict attempt REJECTED is `Field_IntDom_agrees_map`, where the two `DomHom`s share an underlying map but carry different `dom_map_inj` proofs — guarded as a `Fail` probe paired with the control that the same shape one functor along IS strict, so the probe says the proofs differ HERE and not that proof-carrying records rarely convert. A probe-hygiene episode is recorded in the probe header: negatives 1–2 in a reduced-import scratch file aborted with "Illegal application" (the `SetoidMorphism` coercion was absent) rather than a unification error — a genuine FALSE PASS, fixed by giving the probe file the real import list; and negative 2 is a real boundary, `2 # 4` being `Qeq`-equal to the answer but not Leibniz-equal. Two shadowing traps recorded in-file: `Coq.QArith.QArith` transitively exports `Field_theory.num`, shadowing `Frac.num` (but not `den`) with an error naming neither culprit, pinned by a local notation rather than import order; and `Construction/Subcategory.v`'s category-indexed `Full` shadows `Theory/Functor.v`'s. Deferred: no `Q(ℤ) ≅ ℚ` isomorphism (the computations relate them only through the extension), `Dom` defined in this file rather than beside `IntDom`, and nothing claimed about fullness of `IntDom_Incl` into `CRng`
- **Instance/Rng/Mod.v**: Restriction of scalars and the fibered category of all modules (Mac Lane §II.2 constructions 8 and 9, p. 35) — `Restrict ρ : RMod S ⟶ RMod R` along a ring hom (same carrier, action through ρ; identity on underlying homs), Mac Lane's RIGHT-module reading riding `RigHom_op` (`RestrictR : ModR S ⟶ ModR R` — a ring hom is its own opposite-ring hom); **`ModIndexed : IndexedCat (Rng^op)`, the first IndexedCat INSTANTIATED at a concrete varying family** (the general constructors `RT_Indexed`/`IndexedCat_of_StrictFunctor` do handle varying fibres but are applied nowhere outside their own files; `Constant_IndexedCat`, the one prior concrete instantiation, has all fibres one category) — every structural iso identity-carrier via the `rmod_iso_of_ab` smart constructor, `idx_id`/`idx_comp` actions DEFINITIONALLY equal (identity ring map is `Datatypes.id`, hom composition is `Basics.compose`), all ten law fields pointwise reflexivity; `ModTotal := Grothendieck ModIndexed` with `ModProj`. The scope point the file is careful about: the covariant machinery yields the OPFIBRED companion — ModTotal's morphisms are the cosemilinear pairs (ρ : S → R, g with g(ρ(s)·m) ≈ s·g(m)) — while **Mac Lane's construction 9 proper is `ModFibred := (Grothendieck OpModIndexed)^op`** over `OpModIndexed` (fibres `(RMod R)^op`, isos the flipped identity-carrier ones), where the (ρ,f)-description is DEFINITIONAL: `ModFibred_hom_unfold` is `eq_refl` at `∃ ρ : R ~{Rng}~> S, M ~> RestrictObj ρ N`, `ModFibred_semilinear` reads f(r·m) ≈ ρ(r)·f(m) (the word "semilinear" sits here; the opfibred pairs are `mod_cosemilinear_hom`), the composite's ring leg is Mac Lane's ρ'∘ρ by `eq_refl`, and the fibre leg is (Restrict ρ f') ∘ f up to the CANONICAL comparison made visible (`ModFibred_compose_fibre` — genuinely needed: iterated and composite restrictions share an action but differ in law fields, so are distinct records; `ModFibred_compose_carrier` shows it invisible on carriers). `ModFibredProj : ModFibred ⟶ Rng` ((Rng^op)^op collapses by `eq_refl`); fibres: `ModTotal_fibre` identifies ModTotal's own fibre with `RMod R` outright; `ModFibred_fibre` lands at `RMod R` from the OPPOSITE of the underlying Grothendieck's fibre (ModFibred is not presented as a displayed total over `Rng`, so `Fiber` cannot apply to it directly — the file states this precisely); ℤ-initial witnesses both ways (`mod_to_Z`, `mod_from_Z`). NOTATION GUARD recorded in-file: `C^op` (category_scope) and `F^op` (functor_scope) are the same token, and Functor/Opposite.v opens functor_scope — the file pins `Open Scope category_scope` and names `Opposite_Functor` explicitly. - **Instance/Grp/Abelianization.v**: Mac Lane §§I.3–I.4's commutator subgroup and abelianization functors, on the setoid-quotient design: G/[G,G] needs NO new carrier — it is G's carrier under the coarser equivalence `abel_eq` (a·b⁻¹ lies in the ≈-saturated inductive generation `InCommutator`), so commutativity of the quotient is the generating constructor itself and the projection family `abel_projection : Id[Grp] ⟹ Ab_to_Grp ◯ Abelianization_Functor` has IDENTITY components with Mac Lane's naturality square closing by reflexivity of the coarser relation. Two inductions over the one generation: `hom_commutator` (homomorphisms push the subgroup forward) serves functoriality (`Commutator_Functor : Grp ⟶ Grp`, Center.v's sigma pattern) and normality (`conj_commutator` := the `Grp_conj` instance — what `abel_eq_mul`/`abel_eq_inv`'s well-definedness spends), while `hom_to_abelian_kills` (homs into abelian groups kill commutator elements) is the descent germ — the reason the optional adjunction Abelianization ⊣ Ab_to_Grp is close at hand, disclosed as future Reflective.v work rather than built. The quotient is SPECIALIZED to `InCommutator` (the congruence proofs consume only the normal-subgroup interface, so a generic `NormalSubgroup` quotient is extractable — **and it has since been extracted**: this file now RIDES `Instance/Grp/Quotient.v`'s generic quotient via `CommutatorNS`, its six `abel_eq_*` lemmas reduced to one-line citations with every Lemma statement byte-identical. The old disclosure named THREE unshared constructions to unify; that count was imprecise, and the header now says so — `Instance/Grp/Epi.v:433`'s `Grp_Coset` is a `SetoidObject`, not a `GrpObject`, being the coset space of a possibly NON-NORMAL image, which is exactly what a normal-subgroup quotient declines to assume, so it is not a candidate at all; `Instance/Ab.v`'s remains separate by a measured decision, routing it would give `Ab.v` a `Grp` dependency it does not have); non-degeneracy is witnessed (`commutator_GrpTwo_proper`, `commutator_S3_nontrivial`, `abelianization_S3_nontrivial` — the quotient of nonabelian S₃ does not collapse, seen through the sign character). `Ab_to_Grp : Ab ⟶ Grp` is the evident inclusion, new here (no bridge existed). Universe-polymorphic throughout (`Abelianization_Functor@{u u0}` — no Set pinning, the Center.v hazard avoided)
- **Instance/Grp/Center.v**: Mac Lane §I.3's remark and Exercise 4 — the center is not functorial. `IsCentral`/`CenterGrp`/`CenterAb`: central elements form a subgroup (closure by the classical chains) that is abelian BY CONSTRUCTION (commutation of two central elements is one's centrality at the other), so G ↦ Z(G) is a genuine object function Grp → Ab; `Center_incl` includes it. Computations: `Center_GrpTwo_iso : CenterGrp GrpTwo ≅[Grp] GrpTwo` (Z(S₂) is everything; ℤ/2 IS S₂, no permutation model built) and `S3_center_trivial` (six-way case analysis, the proof spending only the two generators), with `central_not_preserved` recording Mac Lane's REMARK proper — the inclusion carries ℤ/2's central nonidentity to the non-central reflection. The counterexample retract uses TwoFunctors.v's SEMIDIRECT S3 (rot × bool), whose sign character is definitionally the bool projection — `s3_sign`/`grp_two_s3`/`sign_retract` — with the header disclosing why Epi.v's `GrpSym3`/`grp_two_sym3` (a different S₃ presentation, serving that file's non-normal-image counterexample, with no in-tree retraction) is not the donor, AND the cost of that choice: TwoFunctors' S3 pins the carrier universe at Set, so `no_center_functor` quantifies over functors on Grp at carrier universe Set (the hazard Epi.v's poly_unit carriers exist to avoid; a polymorphic restatement awaits a poly_unit semidirect S₃ or a sign character on GrpSym3). The headline: `no_center_functor` — for ANY functor T : Grp ⟶ Ab (at that universe) with `fobj T G = CenterAb G` strictly on objects (the literal reading of Mac Lane's "has Z as its object function"; a functor agreeing only up to iso escapes the statement, disclosed; arbitrary arrow function), functoriality pushes the retract S₂ → S₃ → S₂ through the trivial Z(S₃), collapsing Z(S₂)'s nontrivial element into the unit — the two elements of ℤ/2 are distinct constructors, contradiction

(The issue's verification block names `square_arrow_count`; the delivered name is `square_arrow_total`, consistent with its sibling counters.) NON-VACUITY three independent ways: `free_square_arrow_total = 10` against 9, so exactly one merge (`square_merge_arithmetic`); `free_square_diagonals_neq` (Leibniz-distinct upstairs) paired with `square_relation_holds` (equal downstairs); and `square_generator_total = 4` computed from the `FiniteQuiver` data, so the diagonal is provably a composite and not a fifth generator. - **Instance/Mon/Coproduct.v**, **Construction/Free/Quiver/Coproduct.v**: Mac Lane SIII.3 Exercise 4 — coproducts in **Mon** and **Grph** (Awodey 1st ed. §3.2 Examples 3.5 and 3.9 cover the same ground). The Cat third was already done (`Cat_Cocartesian`); these are the other two, both delivered as genuine `Cocartesian` INSTANCES rather than bare universal properties. **`Mon_Sets_Cocartesian : @Cocartesian (@Mon Sets Sets_Product_Monoidal)`** is the free product `FreeProd` — an inductive term type `FPTerm` quotiented by a 12-constructor congruence `fp_eq` — fully general over ALL objects with no side condition, no decidability and no smallness, with `Mon_Sets_Initial` alongside so the trivial-factor control lands at isomorphism strength. **The target category was chosen by measurement, not convenience**: `Theory/Algebra/Monoid/Hom.v:83`'s `Mon` is the only category of GENERAL monoids in the tree (`Instance/CMon.v:140`'s `CMon` is a category whose objects are COMMUTATIVE monoids, ruled out for a different reason; an earlier draft of this bullet said "the ONLY monoid category", which the file's own header already qualifies away) — `Construction/Deloop.v:123`'s `MonObject` is a bare record with no category, so a coproduct there could not be stated as `Cocartesian` at all, and `Instance/Coq/Monoid/Free.v`'s `MonCoq` is a DIFFERENT monoid notion over `Coq`. The file deliberately does NOT require `Instance/Roster.v` (which names this same term `Mon_Sets`), because Roster pulls in `Instance/Top` and hence the stdlib reals; the name identification is instead GUARDED in the probe file. **Non-degeneracy is proved twice over ON THE MON SIDE** (the quiver half has no general statement — its `injections_not_equivalent` is at one concrete quiver, an asymmetry recorded in that file's header): generally, both injections are split monic hence injective for ARBITRARY factors (`fp_injections_Monic`, `fp_inl_injective`) — the congruence collapses neither factor, ever; and concretely over (ℕ,+) ∗ (ℕ,+), whose free product is the free monoid on two letters, `fp_generators_do_not_commute` and `fp_word_not_left`/`_right` show the two-letter alternating word is `fp_eq`-distinct from EVERY one-letter word on either side, all by mapping OUT through `fp_eval_resp` into `(list bool, ++, nil)` and `discriminate` — no induction on the congruence can yield a negative. The degenerate one-factor-trivial case is a CONTROL, not the witness. **`QuiverCategory_Cocartesian`** is the componentwise disjoint union, with the cross edge-sets literally `False` (`QuiverCoprod_edges_lr`/`_rl` by `eq_refl`) and `coprod_no_cross_edge` proved by the identity. **It succeeds where the sibling product could not, for a structural reason worth recording**: `Construction/Free/Quiver/Constructions.v` discloses that its `QuiverPair_unique` takes LEIBNIZ hypotheses because respectfulness of `QuiverPair` is unproved — a pairing's node action pairs two functors, so relating two pairings needs an assembled equality of PAIRS with edges transported along it. A copairing's action is a `match`, so at each constructor the required equality IS the given one; hence `QuiverCopair_respects` IS proved and `QuiverCopair_unique` takes `≈` hypotheses. That is also what made the `Cocartesian` instance affordable. Both triangles hold at whole-record LEIBNIZ equality (`QuiverCopair_Inl`/`_Inr` := `eq_refl`), and the gap Constructions.v discloses — no competitor exhibited that FAILS a triangle — is filled by `copair_swap_fails_left`/`_right`. **Forgetful preservation is MEASURED and comes out NEGATIVE at `eq_refl`**, with both causes located rather than guessed: `Construction/Coproduct.v` is a `Program Definition` whose match compilation gives `hom` an equation-passing shape that does not convert under binders, and its `homset`'s `setoid_equiv` is a `Qed`-opaque obligation. The sharp measurement is that `edgeset` fails at constructor arguments where `edges` SUCCEEDS at the very same arguments. Delivered instead as `QuiverOfCat_Coproduct_iso`, whose legs are the identity on nodes AND on edges in every case, plus `Forgetful_preserves_coprod_inl`/`_inr` at `≈` — so the coproduct DIAGRAM is preserved and the failure is presentational; `eq_refl`-level preservation is NOT claimed unreachable, only not achieved. `CoproductInl`/`CoproductInr` had to be built here, the tree having no named inclusion functor into a coproduct of categories. SCOPE: **Awodey §3.2 Ex 5 (`M(A) + M(B) ≅ M(A+B)` via LAPC) is NOT DELIVERED, for a verified reason** — the free-monoid adjunction `free_monoid_adjunction` lives at `MonCoq := @Mon Coq Coq_Monoidal` (`Instance/Coq/Monoid/Free.v:126,326`) while this coproduct is at `@Mon Sets`; they are DIFFERENT CATEGORIES, so there is no adjoint at this category to apply LAPC to. `Construction/Funny.v:112-114`'s unproven prose claim (the funny tensor of one-object categories is the free product in Mon) is NOT proved either and is neither weakened nor strengthened — closing it needs a bridge between `FunHom` words and `FPTerm` words AND between two different monoid records. Also not delivered BY THESE FILES: no alternating normal form, hence no decision procedure for `fp_eq`; no nullary quiver case, so `Cocartesian QuiverCategory` is binary only and no `Initial QuiverCategory` is built; no naturality of the quiver preservation in C and D; no hom-setoid bijection packaging. `Test/ProbeCoproduct328.v` guards the boundary with 5 conversion negatives plus a separate instrument check, rename-simulated at 15/15 over the constants appearing in the NEGATIVES — a check that found one (`edges`) unguarded on its first run

- **Instance/Ordinal.v**: The finite ordinals as categories — the uniform family `Ordinal n` (thin, carrier the record `Ord_obj n` in proved bijection with `Fin.t n`, order `Instance/Omega.v`'s Type-valued `le_t`), where the tree previously stopped at `_2`. Agreement isos `Ordinal 0 ≅ _0`, `Ordinal 1 ≅ _1`, `Ordinal 2 ≅ _2` in `Cat`, with `_3` and `_4` exported for downstream shapes; full and faithful embeddings `Ord_Incl : Ordinal n ⟶ Ordinal (S n)` and `Ord_Omega : Ordinal n ⟶ Omega` ("an ordinal is the set of its predecessors", categorically), commuting in the evident triangle. The Seven Sketches §3.2.1 morphism count is a closed formula by induction, not per-n enumeration: `ord_pairs n` enumerates the arrows duplicate-free and `ord_morphism_count : 2 * length (ord_pairs n) = n * (n+1)`. Riehl §1.6's freeness is stated as a direct UMP at the level of `Cat` (`Construction/Free/Quiver.v`'s `FreeOnQuiver` + `FreeForgetfulAdjunction` do exist, but their universal property lives in `StrictCat` under strict functor equality; the literal `FreeOnQuiver (linear quiver) ≅ Ordinal (S n)` identification is NO LONGER OPEN — it is `ordinal_free` in Construction/Free/Quiver/Examples.v, at `≅[StrictCat]` and for every m including the empty ordinal, though pinned to `Category@{Set Set Set}` by the free side while `Ordinal` here stays polymorphic): a path of n composable arrows (`OrdSteps`) generates `Functor_of_Steps : Ordinal (S n) ⟶ C` acting as the path on generating steps, every functor out of `Ordinal (S n)` is `Functor_Setoid`-equivalent to one so obtained (`Functor_of_Steps_of_Functor`), and naturality on the n generating steps suffices for naturality everywhere (`ord_functor_equiv_from_steps`); shape-4 diagrams specialize this at n=3, the four triangles commuting by thinness. On-the-nose functor↔path bijection is deliberately NOT claimed — a path's object part is a function on all of `nat`, so paths agreeing below n induce the same functor
- **Instance/Ab/Character.v**: Pontryagin duality of finite abelian groups, Mac Lane's §I.4 worked pair — the character group `D(G) := hom(G, ℚ/ℤ)` with `QZ` the circle as an `AbObject` (carrier ℚ identified up to integer difference — the setoid IS the quotient — with DECIDABLE ≈ via `Qred`; ℚ/ℤ-for-ℝ/ℤ disclosed: finite characters land in torsion, and stdlib ℚ is axiom-free where ℝ is not), the contravariant `D : Ab^op ⟶ Ab`, the covariant `DD` (defined directly so both flips unfold), and the natural evaluation `tau`/`tau_natural`/`tau_Transform : Id ⟹ DD`. Character/Finite.v is the substantial half, fully proven and axiom-free: `tau_iso_finite : FiniteCarrier G → G ≅ DD G` over finiteness-as-data (enumeration + ≈-completeness + ≈-decider), via a Type-valued pigeonhole toolkit (stdlib `In`/`NoDup` are Prop and cannot eliminate), `smul_annihilator` (no Lagrange, no well-ordering), the CHARACTER EXTENSION lemma (fuel recursion whose progress lemma `outside_count_generated` re-establishes its own bound — no cardinality anywhere; bounded-minimization coset decomposition with uniqueness by period-minimality; k-th roots computed in ℚ), separation with value 1/k forcing `tau_injective_finite`, and surjectivity by the SAME subgroup induction (`Realizes`/`VanishesOn` — every functional vanishing on H's annihilator is evaluation at a member of H), deliberately avoiding |D(G)| = |G| — though `zmod2_characters` verifies the count concretely on the ℤ/2 witness. Character/NonNatural.v is the negative half (`maclane:I.4:remark1`): the groupoid core `AbIso`, the covariant `D' f = D(f⁻¹)`, and `sigma_not_natural` — no family of isos `σ_G : G ≅ D(G)` survives even the single naturality square at the automorphism ×2 of `Z5` (2q ≈ 3q forces q ≈ 0, so σ(1) is the zero character and σ is not injective) — Mac Lane's m² ≡ 1 obstruction at the first working prime, p = 5
